### 1.15 测试优先级

**Q22: 如何确定测试优先级？**

**参考答案：**

首先，测试优先级评估维度：

```
测试优先级评估维度
├── 业务价值
│   ├── 核心业务功能
│   ├── 用户高频使用
│   └── 收入相关功能
├── 风险程度
│   ├── 历史缺陷高发区
│   ├── 新开发功能
│   └── 复杂业务逻辑
├── 变更影响
│   ├── 代码变更范围
│   ├── 接口变更
│   └── 数据结构变更
└── 依赖关系
    ├── 被依赖模块
    ├── 外部系统集成
    └── 数据依赖
```

其次，测试优先级计算方法：

```python
# 测试优先级评估工具
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict

@dataclass
class TestPriority:
    """测试优先级"""
    test_id: str
    test_name: str
    priority_score: float
    priority_level: str
    factors: Dict

class TestPriorityAnalyzer:
    """测试优先级分析器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.weights = {
            'business_value': 0.3,
            'risk_level': 0.25,
            'change_impact': 0.25,
            'dependency': 0.2
        }
    
    def calculate_priority(self, test_case, factors):
        """计算测试优先级"""
        scores = {}
        
        # 业务价值评分 (1-10)
        business_score = self._evaluate_business_value(
            factors.get('is_core_function', False),
            factors.get('usage_frequency', 0),
            factors.get('revenue_impact', False)
        )
        scores['business_value'] = business_score
        
        # 风险程度评分 (1-10)
        risk_score = self._evaluate_risk(
            factors.get('historical_bugs', 0),
            factors.get('is_new_feature', False),
            factors.get('complexity', 'medium')
        )
        scores['risk_level'] = risk_score
        
        # 变更影响评分 (1-10)
        change_score = self._evaluate_change_impact(
            factors.get('code_changes', 0),
            factors.get('api_changes', False),
            factors.get('data_changes', False)
        )
        scores['change_impact'] = change_score
        
        # 依赖关系评分 (1-10)
        dependency_score = self._evaluate_dependency(
            factors.get('is_depended', False),
            factors.get('external_integration', False),
            factors.get('data_dependency', False)
        )
        scores['dependency'] = dependency_score
        
        # 计算加权总分
        total_score = sum(
            scores[factor] * weight 
            for factor, weight in self.weights.items()
        )
        
        # 确定优先级等级
        priority_level = self._get_priority_level(total_score)
        
        return TestPriority(
            test_id=test_case['id'],
            test_name=test_case['name'],
            priority_score=round(total_score, 2),
            priority_level=priority_level,
            factors=scores
        )
    
    def _evaluate_business_value(self, is_core, frequency, revenue):
        """评估业务价值"""
        score = 5
        if is_core:
            score += 2
        if frequency > 1000:
            score += 2
        elif frequency > 100:
            score += 1
        if revenue:
            score += 1
        return min(score, 10)
    
    def _evaluate_risk(self, bugs, is_new, complexity):
        """评估风险程度"""
        score = 5
        if bugs > 10:
            score += 2
        elif bugs > 5:
            score += 1
        if is_new:
            score += 2
        if complexity == 'high':
            score += 1
        return min(score, 10)
    
    def _evaluate_change_impact(self, code_changes, api_changes, data_changes):
        """评估变更影响"""
        score = 5
        if code_changes > 100:
            score += 2
        elif code_changes > 50:
            score += 1
        if api_changes:
            score += 1
        if data_changes:
            score += 1
        return min(score, 10)
    
    def _evaluate_dependency(self, is_depended, external, data_dep):
        """评估依赖关系"""
        score = 5
        if is_depended:
            score += 2
        if external:
            score += 2
        if data_dep:
            score += 1
        return min(score, 10)
    
    def _get_priority_level(self, score):
        """获取优先级等级"""
        if score >= 8:
            return 'P0-最高'
        elif score >= 6.5:
            return 'P1-高'
        elif score >= 5:
            return 'P2-中'
        else:
            return 'P3-低'
    
    def prioritize_test_suite(self, test_cases):
        """对测试套件进行优先级排序"""
        prioritized = []
        
        for tc in test_cases:
            priority = self.calculate_priority(tc, tc.get('factors', {}))
            prioritized.append(priority)
        
        # 按优先级降序排序
        prioritized.sort(key=lambda x: x.priority_score, reverse=True)
        
        return prioritized
    
    def generate_priority_matrix(self, test_cases):
        """生成优先级矩阵"""
        matrix = {
            'P0-最高': [],
            'P1-高': [],
            'P2-中': [],
            'P3-低': []
        }
        
        prioritized = self.prioritize_test_suite(test_cases)
        
        for p in prioritized:
            matrix[p.priority_level].append({
                'id': p.test_id,
                'name': p.test_name,
                'score': p.priority_score
            })
        
        return matrix

# 使用示例
analyzer = TestPriorityAnalyzer()

test_cases = [
    {
        'id': 'TC-001',
        'name': '用户登录测试',
        'factors': {
            'is_core_function': True,
            'usage_frequency': 5000,
            'revenue_impact': False,
            'historical_bugs': 3,
            'is_new_feature': False,
            'complexity': 'low',
            'code_changes': 20,
            'api_changes': False,
            'data_changes': False,
            'is_depended': True,
            'external_integration': False,
            'data_dependency': True
        }
    },
    {
        'id': 'TC-002',
        'name': '报表导出测试',
        'factors': {
            'is_core_function': False,
            'usage_frequency': 100,
            'revenue_impact': True,
            'historical_bugs': 8,
            'is_new_feature': True,
            'complexity': 'high',
            'code_changes': 150,
            'api_changes': True,
            'data_changes': True,
            'is_depended': False,
            'external_integration': True,
            'data_dependency': False
        }
    }
]

priority_matrix = analyzer.generate_priority_matrix(test_cases)
for level, tests in priority_matrix.items():
    print(f"{level}: {len(tests)}个测试")
```

最后，优先级执行策略：

| 优先级 | 执行时机 | 执行频率 | 适用场景 |
|--------|----------|----------|----------|
| P0-最高 | 每次提交 | 100% | 核心功能、阻塞问题 |
| P1-高 | 每日构建 | 100% | 重要功能、高风险 |
| P2-中 | 每周构建 | 80% | 一般功能、回归测试 |
| P3-低 | 发布前 | 50% | 边缘场景、优化项 |

### 1.16 测试数据管理

**Q23: 如何管理测试数据？**

**参考答案：**

首先，测试数据管理策略：

```
测试数据管理策略
├── 数据准备方式
│   ├── 手工创建
│   ├── 数据工厂
│   ├── 数据库导入
│   └── Mock数据
├── 数据隔离策略
│   ├── 独立测试库
│   ├── 数据快照
│   ├── 事务回滚
│   └── 数据清理
├── 数据版本管理
│   ├── 数据脚本版本化
│   ├── 基线数据管理
│   └── 数据迁移脚本
└── 敏感数据处理
    ├── 数据脱敏
    ├── 数据加密
    └── 虚假数据替换
```

其次，测试数据管理实现：

```python
# 测试数据管理工具
import json
import hashlib
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
import random
import string

class TestDataManager:
    """测试数据管理器"""
    
    def __init__(self, db_connection=None):
        self.db = db_connection
        self.data_cache = {}
        self.created_records = []
    
    def create_test_user(self, **kwargs):
        """创建测试用户"""
        default_user = {
            'username': f"test_user_{self._generate_random_string(6)}",
            'email': f"test_{self._generate_random_string(6)}@test.com",
            'password': self._hash_password('Test@123'),
            'is_active': True,
            'created_at': datetime.now()
        }
        default_user.update(kwargs)
        
        user_id = self._insert_record('users', default_user)
        self.created_records.append({'table': 'users', 'id': user_id})
        return {**default_user, 'id': user_id}
    
    def create_test_project(self, user_id, **kwargs):
        """创建测试项目"""
        default_project = {
            'name': f"测试项目_{self._generate_random_string(4)}",
            'description': '自动化测试创建的项目',
            'owner_id': user_id,
            'status': 'active',
            'created_at': datetime.now()
        }
        default_project.update(kwargs)
        
        project_id = self._insert_record('projects', default_project)
        self.created_records.append({'table': 'projects', 'id': project_id})
        return {**default_project, 'id': project_id}
    
    def create_test_case(self, project_id, **kwargs):
        """创建测试用例"""
        default_case = {
            'title': f"测试用例_{self._generate_random_string(4)}",
            'description': '自动化测试创建的用例',
            'project_id': project_id,
            'priority': 'medium',
            'status': 'active',
            'created_at': datetime.now()
        }
        default_case.update(kwargs)
        
        case_id = self._insert_record('test_cases', default_case)
        self.created_records.append({'table': 'test_cases', 'id': case_id})
        return {**default_case, 'id': case_id}
    
    def load_fixture(self, fixture_name):
        """加载测试固件数据"""
        fixture_path = f"fixtures/{fixture_name}.json"
        with open(fixture_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        return data
    
    def save_fixture(self, fixture_name, data):
        """保存测试固件数据"""
        fixture_path = f"fixtures/{fixture_name}.json"
        with open(fixture_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str)
    
    def cleanup_test_data(self):
        """清理测试数据"""
        # 按创建顺序逆序删除
        for record in reversed(self.created_records):
            self._delete_record(record['table'], record['id'])
        self.created_records = []
    
    def mask_sensitive_data(self, data, fields_to_mask):
        """敏感数据脱敏"""
        masked_data = data.copy()
        for field in fields_to_mask:
            if field in masked_data:
                masked_data[field] = self._mask_value(masked_data[field])
        return masked_data
    
    def _mask_value(self, value):
        """脱敏处理"""
        if isinstance(value, str):
            if '@' in value:  # 邮箱脱敏
                parts = value.split('@')
                return f"***@{parts[1]}"
            elif len(value) > 4:  # 手机号/身份证脱敏
                return value[:3] + '****' + value[-4:]
        return '***'
    
    def _generate_random_string(self, length):
        """生成随机字符串"""
        return ''.join(random.choices(string.ascii_lowercase + string.digits, k=length))
    
    def _hash_password(self, password):
        """密码哈希"""
        return hashlib.sha256(password.encode()).hexdigest()
    
    def _insert_record(self, table, data):
        """插入记录（模拟）"""
        record_id = random.randint(1000, 9999)
        print(f"[模拟] 插入记录到 {table}: {data}")
        return record_id
    
    def _delete_record(self, table, record_id):
        """删除记录（模拟）"""
        print(f"[模拟] 从 {table} 删除记录: {record_id}")


class DataFactory:
    """测试数据工厂"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_user_batch(count, **common_attrs):
        """批量创建用户数据"""
        users = []
        for i in range(count):
            user = {
                'username': f"user_{i:03d}",
                'email': f"user_{i:03d}@test.com",
                'role': 'user',
                **common_attrs
            }
            users.append(user)
        return users
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_project_batch(count, **common_attrs):
        """批量创建项目数据"""
        projects = []
        for i in range(count):
            project = {
                'name': f"项目_{i:03d}",
                'code': f"PRJ{i:03d}",
                'status': random.choice(['active', 'archived']),
                **common_attrs
            }
            projects.append(project)
        return projects
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_test_case_batch(count, **common_attrs):
        """批量创建测试用例数据"""
        cases = []
        priorities = ['high', 'medium', 'low']
        for i in range(count):
            case = {
                'title': f"测试用例_{i:03d}",
                'priority': random.choice(priorities),
                'estimated_time': random.randint(5, 30),
                **common_attrs
            }
            cases.append(case)
        return cases


class DataSnapshot:
    """数据快照管理"""
    
    def __init__(self, db_connection):
        self.db = db_connection
        self.snapshots = {}
    
    def create_snapshot(self, snapshot_name, tables):
        """创建数据快照"""
        snapshot_data = {}
        for table in tables:
            snapshot_data[table] = self._get_table_data(table)
        
        self.snapshots[snapshot_name] = {
            'data': snapshot_data,
            'created_at': datetime.now()
        }
        return snapshot_name
    
    def restore_snapshot(self, snapshot_name):
        """恢复数据快照"""
        if snapshot_name not in self.snapshots:
            raise ValueError(f"快照 {snapshot_name} 不存在")
        
        snapshot = self.snapshots[snapshot_name]
        for table, data in snapshot['data'].items():
            self._restore_table_data(table, data)
    
    def _get_table_data(self, table):
        """获取表数据（模拟）"""
        return []
    
    def _restore_table_data(self, table, data):
        """恢复表数据（模拟）"""
        pass


# 使用示例
data_manager = TestDataManager()

# 创建测试数据
user = data_manager.create_test_user(username='testuser')
project = data_manager.create_test_project(user['id'], name='测试项目')
test_case = data_manager.create_test_case(project['id'], title='登录测试')

# 批量创建数据
users = DataFactory.create_user_batch(10, role='tester')
projects = DataFactory.create_project_batch(5, owner_id=users[0]['id'])

# 数据脱敏
sensitive_user = {'name': '张三', 'phone': '13812345678', 'email': 'zhangsan@test.com'}
masked_user = data_manager.mask_sensitive_data(sensitive_user, ['phone', 'email'])

# 清理测试数据
data_manager.cleanup_test_data()
```

最后，测试数据管理最佳实践：

| 场景 | 推荐策略 | 说明 |
|------|----------|------|
| 单元测试 | 内存数据库/事务回滚 | 快速、隔离 |
| 接口测试 | 独立测试库+数据工厂 | 可重复、可控 |
| UI测试 | 测试环境+数据快照 | 真实、稳定 |
| 性能测试 | 大量数据生成 | 压测场景 |
| 安全测试 | 脱敏数据 | 合规要求 |

### 1.17 测试环境管理

**Q24: 如何管理多个测试环境？**

**参考答案：**

首先，测试环境类型：

```
测试环境架构
├── 开发环境 (DEV)
│   ├── 用途: 开发自测
│   ├── 数据: 模拟数据
│   └── 部署: 频繁更新
├── 测试环境 (TEST)
│   ├── 用途: 功能测试
│   ├── 数据: 测试数据
│   └── 部署: 每日构建
├── 预发布环境 (STAGING)
│   ├── 用途: 验收测试
│   ├── 数据: 类生产数据
│   └── 部署: 发布前
└── 生产环境 (PROD)
    ├── 用途: 线上运行
    ├── 数据: 真实数据
    └── 部署: 正式发布
```

其次，多环境管理实现：

```python
# 测试环境管理工具
import os
import yaml
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Dict, Optional
from enum import Enum

class EnvironmentType(Enum):
    DEV = "development"
    TEST = "testing"
    STAGING = "staging"
    PROD = "production"

@dataclass
class EnvironmentConfig:
    """环境配置"""
    name: str
    env_type: EnvironmentType
    base_url: str
    api_url: str
    db_host: str
    db_port: int
    db_name: str
    redis_host: str
    redis_port: int
    features: Dict

class EnvironmentManager:
    """环境管理器"""
    
    def __init__(self, config_path: str = "config/environments.yaml"):
        self.config_path = config_path
        self.environments: Dict[str, EnvironmentConfig] = {}
        self.current_env: Optional[str] = None
        self._load_configs()
    
    def _load_configs(self):
        """加载环境配置"""
        with open(self.config_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            configs = yaml.safe_load(f)
        
        for env_name, config in configs.items():
            self.environments[env_name] = EnvironmentConfig(
                name=env_name,
                env_type=EnvironmentType(config['type']),
                base_url=config['base_url'],
                api_url=config['api_url'],
                db_host=config['database']['host'],
                db_port=config['database']['port'],
                db_name=config['database']['name'],
                redis_host=config['redis']['host'],
                redis_port=config['redis']['port'],
                features=config.get('features', {})
            )
    
    def get_environment(self, env_name: str) -> EnvironmentConfig:
        """获取环境配置"""
        if env_name not in self.environments:
            raise ValueError(f"环境 {env_name} 不存在")
        return self.environments[env_name]
    
    def set_current_environment(self, env_name: str):
        """设置当前环境"""
        self.current_env = env_name
        os.environ['TEST_ENV'] = env_name
    
    def get_current_environment(self) -> EnvironmentConfig:
        """获取当前环境"""
        if not self.current_env:
            raise ValueError("未设置当前环境")
        return self.environments[self.current_env]
    
    def compare_environments(self, env1: str, env2: str) -> Dict:
        """比较两个环境配置"""
        config1 = self.environments[env1]
        config2 = self.environments[env2]
        
        differences = {}
        
        if config1.base_url != config2.base_url:
            differences['base_url'] = {
                env1: config1.base_url,
                env2: config2.base_url
            }
        
        if config1.db_host != config2.db_host:
            differences['database'] = {
                env1: f"{config1.db_host}:{config1.db_port}",
                env2: f"{config2.db_host}:{config2.db_port}"
            }
        
        return differences
    
    def get_feature_flags(self, env_name: str) -> Dict:
        """获取环境特性开关"""
        return self.environments[env_name].features
    
    def is_feature_enabled(self, env_name: str, feature: str) -> bool:
        """检查特性是否启用"""
        features = self.get_feature_flags(env_name)
        return features.get(feature, False)


class EnvironmentHealthChecker:
    """环境健康检查"""
    
    def __init__(self, env_manager: EnvironmentManager):
        self.env_manager = env_manager
    
    def check_environment(self, env_name: str) -> Dict:
        """检查环境健康状态"""
        env = self.env_manager.get_environment(env_name)
        
        health_status = {
            'environment': env_name,
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
            'checks': {}
        }
        
        # 检查API服务
        health_status['checks']['api'] = self._check_api_health(env.api_url)
        
        # 检查数据库连接
        health_status['checks']['database'] = self._check_database_health(
            env.db_host, env.db_port
        )
        
        # 检查Redis连接
        health_status['checks']['redis'] = self._check_redis_health(
            env.redis_host, env.redis_port
        )
        
        # 计算总体状态
        all_healthy = all(
            check['status'] == 'healthy' 
            for check in health_status['checks'].values()
        )
        health_status['overall_status'] = 'healthy' if all_healthy else 'unhealthy'
        
        return health_status
    
    def _check_api_health(self, api_url):
        """检查API健康"""
        try:
            import requests
            response = requests.get(f"{api_url}/health", timeout=5)
            return {
                'status': 'healthy' if response.status_code == 200 else 'unhealthy',
                'response_time': response.elapsed.total_seconds()
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {'status': 'unhealthy', 'error': str(e)}
    
    def _check_database_health(self, host, port):
        """检查数据库健康"""
        try:
            import socket
            sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            sock.settimeout(5)
            result = sock.connect_ex((host, port))
            sock.close()
            return {'status': 'healthy' if result == 0 else 'unhealthy'}
        except Exception as e:
            return {'status': 'unhealthy', 'error': str(e)}
    
    def _check_redis_health(self, host, port):
        """检查Redis健康"""
        try:
            import socket
            sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            sock.settimeout(5)
            result = sock.connect_ex((host, port))
            sock.close()
            return {'status': 'healthy' if result == 0 else 'unhealthy'}
        except Exception as e:
            return {'status': 'unhealthy', 'error': str(e)}


class EnvironmentSwitcher:
    """环境切换器"""
    
    def __init__(self, env_manager: EnvironmentManager):
        self.env_manager = env_manager
        self.env_history = []
    
    def switch_to(self, env_name: str):
        """切换到指定环境"""
        old_env = self.env_manager.current_env
        self.env_manager.set_current_environment(env_name)
        
        self.env_history.append({
            'from': old_env,
            'to': env_name,
            'timestamp': datetime.now()
        })
        
        print(f"环境切换: {old_env} -> {env_name}")
    
    def get_switch_history(self):
        """获取切换历史"""
        return self.env_history


# 环境配置文件示例 (environments.yaml)
ENVIRONMENTS_CONFIG = """
development:
  type: development
  base_url: "http://localhost:3000"
  api_url: "http://localhost:8000"
  database:
    host: "localhost"
    port: 5432
    name: "test_dev"
  redis:
    host: "localhost"
    port: 6379
  features:
    debug_mode: true
    mock_external_api: true

testing:
  type: testing
  base_url: "http://test.example.com"
  api_url: "http://api.test.example.com"
  database:
    host: "db.test.example.com"
    port: 5432
    name: "test_db"
  redis:
    host: "redis.test.example.com"
    port: 6379
  features:
    debug_mode: false
    mock_external_api: true

staging:
  type: staging
  base_url: "https://staging.example.com"
  api_url: "https://api.staging.example.com"
  database:
    host: "db.staging.example.com"
    port: 5432
    name: "staging_db"
  redis:
    host: "redis.staging.example.com"
    port: 6379
  features:
    debug_mode: false
    mock_external_api: false

production:
  type: production
  base_url: "https://www.example.com"
  api_url: "https://api.example.com"
  database:
    host: "db.example.com"
    port: 5432
    name: "prod_db"
  redis:
    host: "redis.example.com"
    port: 6379
  features:
    debug_mode: false
    mock_external_api: false
"""

# 使用示例
env_manager = EnvironmentManager()
env_manager.set_current_environment('testing')

# 获取当前环境配置
current_env = env_manager.get_current_environment()
print(f"当前环境: {current_env.name}")
print(f"API地址: {current_env.api_url}")

# 环境健康检查
health_checker = EnvironmentHealthChecker(env_manager)
health_status = health_checker.check_environment('testing')
print(f"环境状态: {health_status['overall_status']}")

# 环境切换
switcher = EnvironmentSwitcher(env_manager)
switcher.switch_to('staging')
```

最后，环境管理最佳实践：

| 实践 | 说明 | 好处 |
|------|------|------|
| 配置分离 | 环境配置与代码分离 | 安全、灵活 |
| 环境隔离 | 各环境独立部署 | 避免相互影响 |
| 数据隔离 | 独立数据库 | 数据安全 |
| 自动化部署 | CI/CD流水线 | 效率提升 |
| 监控告警 | 环境健康检查 | 及时发现问题 |

### 1.18 测试报告

**Q25: 如何编写高质量的测试报告？**

**参考答案：**

首先，测试报告的核心要素：

```
测试报告结构
├── 报告概述
│   ├── 测试目的
│   ├── 测试范围
│   └── 测试结论
├── 测试概况
│   ├── 测试时间
│   ├── 测试环境
│   ├── 测试人员
│   └── 测试版本
├── 测试执行情况
│   ├── 用例执行统计
│   ├── 覆盖率统计
│   └── 执行进度
├── 缺陷统计
│   ├── 缺陷分布
│   ├── 缺陷趋势
│   └── 遗留缺陷
├── 质量评估
│   ├── 功能质量
│   ├── 性能质量
│   └── 风险评估
└── 建议
    ├── 发布建议
    ├── 风险提示
    └── 改进建议
```

其次，测试报告生成实现：

```python
# 测试报告生成工具
from datetime import datetime
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional
import json

@dataclass
class TestCaseResult:
    """测试用例结果"""
    case_id: str
    case_name: str
    module: str
    status: str  # passed, failed, blocked, skipped
    duration: float
    error_message: Optional[str] = None
    screenshot: Optional[str] = None

@dataclass
class DefectInfo:
    """缺陷信息"""
    defect_id: str
    title: str
    severity: str
    priority: str
    status: str
    module: str
    create_time: datetime

@dataclass
class TestSummary:
    """测试摘要"""
    total_cases: int = 0
    passed: int = 0
    failed: int = 0
    blocked: int = 0
    skipped: int = 0
    
    @property
    def pass_rate(self) -> float:
        if self.total_cases == 0:
            return 0
        return round((self.passed / self.total_cases) * 100, 2)

class TestReportGenerator:
    """测试报告生成器"""
    
    def __init__(self, project_name: str, version: str):
        self.project_name = project_name
        self.version = version
        self.test_results: List[TestCaseResult] = []
        self.defects: List[DefectInfo] = []
        self.summary = TestSummary()
        self.start_time = datetime.now()
        self.end_time: Optional[datetime] = None
    
    def add_test_result(self, result: TestCaseResult):
        """添加测试结果"""
        self.test_results.append(result)
        self.summary.total_cases += 1
        
        if result.status == 'passed':
            self.summary.passed += 1
        elif result.status == 'failed':
            self.summary.failed += 1
        elif result.status == 'blocked':
            self.summary.blocked += 1
        else:
            self.summary.skipped += 1
    
    def add_defect(self, defect: DefectInfo):
        """添加缺陷"""
        self.defects.append(defect)
    
    def finish_testing(self):
        """结束测试"""
        self.end_time = datetime.now()
    
    def generate_report(self) -> Dict:
        """生成测试报告"""
        return {
            'report_info': {
                'project_name': self.project_name,
                'version': self.version,
                'report_time': datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'),
                'test_duration': self._calculate_duration(),
                'testers': self._get_testers()
            },
            'executive_summary': self._generate_executive_summary(),
            'test_summary': {
                'total': self.summary.total_cases,
                'passed': self.summary.passed,
                'failed': self.summary.failed,
                'blocked': self.summary.blocked,
                'skipped': self.summary.skipped,
                'pass_rate': f"{self.summary.pass_rate}%"
            },
            'module_statistics': self._generate_module_statistics(),
            'defect_analysis': self._generate_defect_analysis(),
            'quality_assessment': self._generate_quality_assessment(),
            'recommendations': self._generate_recommendations(),
            'detailed_results': self._generate_detailed_results()
        }
    
    def _calculate_duration(self) -> str:
        """计算测试时长"""
        if not self.end_time:
            return "测试进行中"
        
        duration = self.end_time - self.start_time
        hours, remainder = divmod(duration.seconds, 3600)
        minutes, seconds = divmod(remainder, 60)
        return f"{hours}小时{minutes}分钟{seconds}秒"
    
    def _get_testers(self) -> List[str]:
        """获取测试人员"""
        return ['测试组']
    
    def _generate_executive_summary(self) -> Dict:
        """生成执行摘要"""
        if self.summary.pass_rate >= 95:
            conclusion = "通过，建议发布"
            risk_level = "低"
        elif self.summary.pass_rate >= 80:
            conclusion = "基本通过，需关注遗留问题"
            risk_level = "中"
        else:
            conclusion = "不通过，存在较大风险"
            risk_level = "高"
        
        return {
            'conclusion': conclusion,
            'risk_level': risk_level,
            'key_findings': self._get_key_findings()
        }
    
    def _get_key_findings(self) -> List[str]:
        """获取关键发现"""
        findings = []
        
        if self.summary.failed > 0:
            findings.append(f"发现{self.summary.failed}个失败用例")
        
        critical_defects = [d for d in self.defects if d.severity == 'critical']
        if critical_defects:
            findings.append(f"存在{len(critical_defects)}个严重缺陷")
        
        if self.summary.pass_rate >= 95:
            findings.append("整体质量良好")
        
        return findings if findings else ["测试全部通过"]
    
    def _generate_module_statistics(self) -> Dict:
        """生成模块统计"""
        module_stats = {}
        
        for result in self.test_results:
            module = result.module
            if module not in module_stats:
                module_stats[module] = {
                    'total': 0, 'passed': 0, 'failed': 0
                }
            
            module_stats[module]['total'] += 1
            if result.status == 'passed':
                module_stats[module]['passed'] += 1
            elif result.status == 'failed':
                module_stats[module]['failed'] += 1
        
        # 计算通过率
        for module, stats in module_stats.items():
            stats['pass_rate'] = round(
                (stats['passed'] / stats['total']) * 100, 2
            ) if stats['total'] > 0 else 0
        
        return module_stats
    
    def _generate_defect_analysis(self) -> Dict:
        """生成缺陷分析"""
        severity_distribution = {}
        status_distribution = {}
        module_distribution = {}
        
        for defect in self.defects:
            # 严重程度分布
            severity = defect.severity
            severity_distribution[severity] = severity_distribution.get(severity, 0) + 1
            
            # 状态分布
            status = defect.status
            status_distribution[status] = status_distribution.get(status, 0) + 1
            
            # 模块分布
            module = defect.module
            module_distribution[module] = module_distribution.get(module, 0) + 1
        
        return {
            'total_defects': len(self.defects),
            'severity_distribution': severity_distribution,
            'status_distribution': status_distribution,
            'module_distribution': module_distribution,
            'open_defects': len([d for d in self.defects if d.status == 'open'])
        }
    
    def _generate_quality_assessment(self) -> Dict:
        """生成质量评估"""
        return {
            'functional_quality': {
                'score': self.summary.pass_rate,
                'level': self._get_quality_level(self.summary.pass_rate),
                'description': f"功能测试通过率{self.summary.pass_rate}%"
            },
            'defect_quality': {
                'score': self._calculate_defect_score(),
                'level': self._get_quality_level(self._calculate_defect_score()),
                'description': f"共发现{len(self.defects)}个缺陷"
            },
            'overall_assessment': self._get_overall_assessment()
        }
    
    def _calculate_defect_score(self) -> float:
        """计算缺陷得分"""
        if not self.defects:
            return 100
        
        # 根据缺陷严重程度扣分
        penalty = 0
        for defect in self.defects:
            if defect.severity == 'critical':
                penalty += 20
            elif defect.severity == 'high':
                penalty += 10
            elif defect.severity == 'medium':
                penalty += 5
            else:
                penalty += 2
        
        return max(0, 100 - penalty)
    
    def _get_quality_level(self, score: float) -> str:
        """获取质量等级"""
        if score >= 90:
            return '优秀'
        elif score >= 80:
            return '良好'
        elif score >= 60:
            return '一般'
        else:
            return '较差'
    
    def _get_overall_assessment(self) -> str:
        """获取总体评估"""
        pass_rate = self.summary.pass_rate
        defect_score = self._calculate_defect_score()
        overall_score = (pass_rate + defect_score) / 2
        
        if overall_score >= 90:
            return "质量优秀，建议发布"
        elif overall_score >= 80:
            return "质量良好，可以发布"
        elif overall_score >= 60:
            return "质量一般，需评估风险后决定"
        else:
            return "质量较差，不建议发布"
    
    def _generate_recommendations(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """生成建议"""
        recommendations = []
        
        # 发布建议
        if self.summary.pass_rate >= 95 and not self._has_critical_open_defects():
            recommendations.append({
                'type': '发布建议',
                'content': '建议按计划发布',
                'priority': '高'
            })
        else:
            recommendations.append({
                'type': '发布建议',
                'content': '建议修复关键问题后再发布',
                'priority': '高'
            })
        
        # 风险提示
        if self.summary.failed > 5:
            recommendations.append({
                'type': '风险提示',
                'content': f'存在{self.summary.failed}个失败用例，需重点关注',
                'priority': '高'
            })
        
        # 改进建议
        failed_modules = self._get_failed_modules()
        if failed_modules:
            recommendations.append({
                'type': '改进建议',
                'content': f'模块{", ".join(failed_modules)}质量需加强',
                'priority': '中'
            })
        
        return recommendations
    
    def _has_critical_open_defects(self) -> bool:
        """是否有严重未关闭缺陷"""
        return any(
            d.severity == 'critical' and d.status == 'open'
            for d in self.defects
        )
    
    def _get_failed_modules(self) -> List[str]:
        """获取失败率高的模块"""
        module_stats = self._generate_module_statistics()
        return [
            module for module, stats in module_stats.items()
            if stats['pass_rate'] < 80
        ]
    
    def _generate_detailed_results(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """生成详细结果"""
        return [
            {
                'case_id': r.case_id,
                'case_name': r.case_name,
                'module': r.module,
                'status': r.status,
                'duration': f"{r.duration:.2f}s",
                'error': r.error_message
            }
            for r in self.test_results
        ]
    
    def export_to_html(self, output_path: str):
        """导出为HTML报告"""
        report = self.generate_report()
        
        html_template = f"""
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>测试报告 - {self.project_name} v{self.version}</title>
    <style>
        body {{ font-family: Arial, sans-serif; margin: 20px; }}
        h1 {{ color: #333; }}
        h2 {{ color: #666; border-bottom: 1px solid #ddd; padding-bottom: 10px; }}
        .summary {{ background: #f5f5f5; padding: 15px; border-radius: 5px; }}
        .pass {{ color: green; }}
        .fail {{ color: red; }}
        table {{ border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; margin: 10px 0; }}
        th, td {{ border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 8px; text-align: left; }}
        th {{ background: #f5f5f5; }}
        .metric {{ display: inline-block; margin: 10px; padding: 15px; background: #e8f4f8; border-radius: 5px; }}
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <h1>测试报告</h1>
    <div class="summary">
        <p><strong>项目:</strong> {report['report_info']['project_name']}</p>
        <p><strong>版本:</strong> {report['report_info']['version']}</p>
        <p><strong>报告时间:</strong> {report['report_info']['report_time']}</p>
        <p><strong>测试时长:</strong> {report['report_info']['test_duration']}</p>
    </div>
    
    <h2>执行摘要</h2>
    <p><strong>结论:</strong> {report['executive_summary']['conclusion']}</p>
    <p><strong>风险等级:</strong> {report['executive_summary']['risk_level']}</p>
    
    <h2>测试统计</h2>
    <div class="metric"><strong>总数:</strong> {report['test_summary']['total']}</div>
    <div class="metric"><strong class="pass">通过:</strong> {report['test_summary']['passed']}</div>
    <div class="metric"><strong class="fail">失败:</strong> {report['test_summary']['failed']}</div>
    <div class="metric"><strong>通过率:</strong> {report['test_summary']['pass_rate']}</div>
    
    <h2>质量评估</h2>
    <p>{report['quality_assessment']['overall_assessment']}</p>
    
    <h2>建议</h2>
    <ul>
        {''.join(f"<li>[{r['type']}] {r['content']}</li>" for r in report['recommendations'])}
    </ul>
</body>
</html>
        """
        
        with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(html_template)

# 使用示例
report_gen = TestReportGenerator("测试用例管理系统", "v1.0.0")

# 添加测试结果
report_gen.add_test_result(TestCaseResult(
    case_id="TC-001",
    case_name="用户登录测试",
    module="用户模块",
    status="passed",
    duration=1.5
))

report_gen.add_test_result(TestCaseResult(
    case_id="TC-002",
    case_name="项目创建测试",
    module="项目模块",
    status="failed",
    duration=2.3,
    error_message="断言失败: 预期状态码200，实际返回500"
))

# 添加缺陷
report_gen.add_defect(DefectInfo(
    defect_id="BUG-001",
    title="项目创建失败",
    severity="high",
    priority="P1",
    status="open",
    module="项目模块",
    create_time=datetime.now()
))

# 结束测试并生成报告
report_gen.finish_testing()
report = report_gen.generate_report()
print(f"测试通过率: {report['test_summary']['pass_rate']}")
print(f"总体评估: {report['quality_assessment']['overall_assessment']}")

# 导出HTML报告
report_gen.export_to_html("test_report.html")
```

最后，测试报告最佳实践：

| 要素 | 最佳实践 | 说明 |
|------|----------|------|
| 及时性 | 测试完成后24小时内发布 | 信息时效性 |
| 准确性 | 数据真实、结论客观 | 可信度 |
| 完整性 | 覆盖所有测试内容 | 全面性 |
| 可读性 | 图表结合、层次清晰 | 易理解 |
| 可追溯 | 关联需求、缺陷 | 可追溯 |

## 二、测试框架开发

### 2.1 框架设计

**Q11: 请设计一个通用的API测试框架**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会设计框架的整体架构：

```
tests/api/
├── core/                    # 核心模块
│   ├── base_api.py         # API基类
│   ├── request.py          # 请求封装
│   ├── response.py         # 响应处理
│   └── assertion.py        # 断言封装
├── api/                     # API封装层
│   ├── user_api.py         # 用户API
│   └── project_api.py      # 项目API
├── tests/                   # 测试用例层
│   ├── test_auth.py        # 认证测试
│   └── test_project.py     # 项目测试
├── data/                    # 测试数据层
│   ├── json/               # JSON数据文件
│   └── factory.py          # 数据工厂
├── utils/                   # 工具模块
│   ├── logger.py           # 日志工具
│   ├── config.py           # 配置管理
│   └── database.py         # 数据库操作
├── reports/                 # 测试报告
│   └── allure/             # Allure报告
├── conftest.py             # Pytest配置
└── pytest.ini              # Pytest配置文件
```

其次，我会实现核心模块：

```python
# core/base_api.py
import requests
import logging
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from core.response import Response

class BaseAPI:
    """
    API基类，封装通用的HTTP请求方法
    """
    def __init__(self, base_url: str, timeout: int = 10):
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip('/')
        self.timeout = timeout
        self.session = requests.Session()
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
        
    def set_headers(self, headers: Dict[str, str]):
        """设置请求头"""
        self.session.headers.update(headers)
    
    def set_token(self, token: str):
        """设置认证Token"""
        self.session.headers['Authorization'] = f'Bearer {token}'
    
    def request(
        self, 
        method: str, 
        endpoint: str, 
        **kwargs
    ) -> Response:
        """
        统一请求方法
        
        Args:
            method: HTTP方法
            endpoint: API端点
            **kwargs: 其他请求参数
        
        Returns:
            Response对象
        """
        url = f"{self.base_url}{endpoint}"
        kwargs.setdefault('timeout', self.timeout)
        
        # 记录请求日志
        self.logger.info(f"Request: {method} {url}")
        self.logger.debug(f"Params: {kwargs}")
        
        try:
            response = self.session.request(method, url, **kwargs)
            return Response(response)
        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Request failed: {e}")
            raise
    
    def get(self, endpoint: str, params: Optional[Dict] = None) -> Response:
        """GET请求"""
        return self.request('GET', endpoint, params=params)
    
    def post(self, endpoint: str, data: Optional[Dict] = None) -> Response:
        """POST请求"""
        return self.request('POST', endpoint, json=data)
    
    def put(self, endpoint: str, data: Optional[Dict] = None) -> Response:
        """PUT请求"""
        return self.request('PUT', endpoint, json=data)
    
    def delete(self, endpoint: str) -> Response:
        """DELETE请求"""
        return self.request('DELETE', endpoint)
```

```python
# core/response.py
import json
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

class Response:
    """
    响应封装类，提供统一的响应访问接口
    """
    def __init__(self, response):
        self._response = response
        self._json_data = None
        
    @property
    def status_code(self) -> int:
        """HTTP状态码"""
        return self._response.status_code
    
    @property
    def headers(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """响应头"""
        return dict(self._response.headers)
    
    @property
    def data(self) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """响应数据"""
        if self._json_data is None:
            try:
                self._json_data = self._response.json()
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                self._json_data = {'raw_text': self._response.text}
        return self._json_data
    
    @property
    def code(self) -> Optional[int]:
        """业务状态码"""
        return self.data.get('code') if self.data else None
    
    @property
    def message(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """业务消息"""
        return self.data.get('message') if self.data else None
    
    @property
    def success(self) -> bool:
        """请求是否成功"""
        return self.status_code == 200 and self.code == 200
    
    def assert_status_code(self, expected: int):
        """断言状态码"""
        assert self.status_code == expected, \
            f"Expected status code {expected}, but got {self.status_code}"
    
    def assert_code(self, expected: int):
        """断言业务状态码"""
        assert self.code == expected, \
            f"Expected code {expected}, but got {self.code}"
    
    def assert_success(self):
        """断言请求成功"""
        assert self.success, \
            f"Request failed: {self.message}"
    
    def assert_contains(self, key: str):
        """断言响应包含指定字段"""
        assert key in self.data, \
            f"Response does not contain key: {key}"
```

然后，我会实现API封装层：

```python
# api/project_api.py
from core.base_api import BaseAPI
from core.response import Response
from typing import Dict, Optional

class ProjectAPI(BaseAPI):
    """
    项目API封装
    """
    def __init__(self, base_url: str):
        super().__init__(base_url)
        self.endpoint = '/api/projects/'
    
    def get_projects(self, params: Optional[Dict] = None) -> Response:
        """获取项目列表"""
        return self.get(self.endpoint, params=params)
    
    def get_project(self, project_id: int) -> Response:
        """获取项目详情"""
        return self.get(f"{self.endpoint}{project_id}/")
    
    def create_project(self, data: Dict) -> Response:
        """创建项目"""
        return self.post(self.endpoint, data=data)
    
    def update_project(self, project_id: int, data: Dict) -> Response:
        """更新项目"""
        return self.put(f"{self.endpoint}{project_id}/", data=data)
    
    def delete_project(self, project_id: int) -> Response:
        """删除项目"""
        return self.delete(f"{self.endpoint}{project_id}/")
```

最后，我会编写测试用例：

```python
# tests/test_project.py
import pytest
import allure
from api.project_api import ProjectAPI
from data.factory import DataFactory

@allure.feature('项目管理')
class TestProject:
    
    @pytest.fixture
    def project_api(self, base_url, admin_token):
        """项目API fixture"""
        api = ProjectAPI(base_url)
        api.set_token(admin_token)
        return api
    
    @allure.story('创建项目')
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    def test_create_project_success(self, project_api):
        """测试创建项目成功"""
        # Given
        project_data = DataFactory.create_project_data()
        
        # When
        with allure.step('创建项目'):
            response = project_api.create_project(project_data)
        
        # Then
        with allure.step('验证创建成功'):
            response.assert_success()
            response.assert_contains('id')
            assert response.data['name'] == project_data['name']
    
    @allure.story('获取项目列表')
    def test_get_projects_success(self, project_api):
        """测试获取项目列表成功"""
        # When
        response = project_api.get_projects()
        
        # Then
        response.assert_success()
        response.assert_contains('results')
        assert isinstance(response.data['results'], list)
```

**为什么这样设计？**
- **分层设计**：核心层、API层、测试层职责清晰
- **封装统一**：请求、响应、断言统一封装，便于维护
- **易于扩展**：新增API只需继承BaseAPI，新增测试只需编写测试用例

**参考答案：**

首先，我会设计框架的整体架构：

```
tests/ui/
├── core/                    # 核心模块
│   ├── base_page.py        # 页面基类
│   ├── element.py          # 元素封装
│   └── wait.py             # 等待封装
├── pages/                   # 页面对象
│   ├── login_page.py       # 登录页
│   ├── project_list_page.py # 项目列表页

│   └── test_project.py     # 项目测试
├── config/                  # 配置文件
│   ├── config.py           # 配置管理

```

其次，我会实现核心模块：

```python
# core/base_page.py
from playwright.sync_api import Page, Locator
from typing import Optional, List
import logging

class BasePage:
    """
    页面基类，封装通用的页面操作
    """
    def __init__(self, page: Page):
        self.page = page
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    
    def navigate(self, url: str):
        """导航到指定URL"""
        self.logger.info(f"Navigating to: {url}")
        self.page.goto(url)
    
    def find_element(self, selector: str) -> Locator:
        """查找元素"""
        return self.page.locator(selector)
    
    def find_elements(self, selector: str) -> List[Locator]:
        """查找多个元素"""
        return self.page.locator(selector).all()
    
    def click(self, selector: str, timeout: int = 10000):
        """点击元素"""
        self.logger.info(f"Clicking: {selector}")
        self.page.click(selector, timeout=timeout)
    
    def fill(self, selector: str, value: str, timeout: int = 10000):
        """填充输入框"""
        self.logger.info(f"Filling {selector} with: {value}")
        self.page.fill(selector, value, timeout=timeout)
    
    def select(self, selector: str, value: str):
        """选择下拉框"""
        self.logger.info(f"Selecting {value} from {selector}")
        self.page.select_option(selector, value)
    
    def get_text(self, selector: str) -> str:
        """获取元素文本"""
        return self.page.locator(selector).text_content()
    
    def is_visible(self, selector: str, timeout: int = 10000) -> bool:
        """判断元素是否可见"""
        try:
            self.page.wait_for_selector(selector, state='visible', timeout=timeout)
            return True
        except:
            return False
    
    def wait_for_element(self, selector: str, timeout: int = 10000):
        """等待元素出现"""
        self.page.wait_for_selector(selector, timeout=timeout)
    
    def wait_for_url(self, pattern: str, timeout: int = 30000):
        """等待URL匹配"""
        self.page.wait_for_url(f"**/{pattern}", timeout=timeout)
    
    def screenshot(self, path: str):
        """截图"""
        self.page.screenshot(path=path)
    
    def expect(self, selector: str):
        """返回断言对象"""
        from playwright.sync_api import expect
        return expect(self.page.locator(selector))
```

然后，我会实现页面对象：

```python
# pages/project_list_page.py
from core.base_page import BasePage
from playwright.sync_api import Page
from typing import Dict

class ProjectListPage(BasePage):
    """
    项目列表页面
    """
    # 元素选择器
    CREATE_BUTTON = 'button:has-text("新建项目")'
    SEARCH_INPUT = 'input[placeholder*="搜索"]'
    PROJECT_ITEM = '.project-item'
    PROJECT_NAME = '.project-name'
    DELETE_BUTTON = '.delete-button'
    
    def __init__(self, page: Page, base_url: str):
        super().__init__(page)
        self.base_url = base_url
    
    def navigate_to_projects(self):
        """导航到项目列表页"""
        self.navigate(f"{self.base_url}/projects")
        self.wait_for_element(self.PROJECT_ITEM)
    
    def click_create_project(self):
        """点击新建项目按钮"""
        self.click(self.CREATE_BUTTON)
    
    def search_project(self, keyword: str):
        """搜索项目"""
        self.fill(self.SEARCH_INPUT, keyword)
        self.page.press(self.SEARCH_INPUT, 'Enter')
    
    def get_project_names(self) -> list:
        """获取所有项目名称"""
        elements = self.find_elements(f"{self.PROJECT_ITEM} {self.PROJECT_NAME}")
        return [elem.text_content() for elem in elements]
    
    def delete_project(self, project_name: str):
        """删除项目"""
        selector = f"{self.PROJECT_ITEM}:has-text('{project_name}') {self.DELETE_BUTTON}"
        self.click(selector)
        # 确认删除
        self.page.on('dialog', lambda dialog: dialog.accept())
    
    def is_project_exists(self, project_name: str) -> bool:
        """判断项目是否存在"""
        return self.is_visible(f"{self.PROJECT_ITEM}:has-text('{project_name}')")
```

**深度思考：** UI测试框架设计的关键点：
- **稳定性**：使用显式等待，避免元素未加载导致的失败
- **可维护性**：选择器集中管理，页面变化时只需修改一处
- **可扩展性**：基类提供通用方法，子类扩展特定功能
- **可读性**：方法命名清晰，测试用例易读

### 2.2 测试数据管理

**Q5: 如何设计测试数据管理方案？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析测试数据管理的需求：
- **数据隔离**：不同测试用例使用独立数据
- **数据复用**：相同场景的数据可以复用
- **数据清理**：测试后自动清理数据
- **数据生成**：支持动态生成测试数据

其次，我会设计数据管理方案：

**方案1：数据工厂模式**

```python
# data/factory.py
import uuid
from faker import Faker
from typing import Dict, Optional

fake = Faker('zh_CN')

class DataFactory:
    """
    数据工厂，用于生成测试数据
    """
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_user_data(
        username: Optional[str] = None,
        email: Optional[str] = None,
        password: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> Dict:
        """生成用户数据"""
        return {
            'username': username or f'user_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}',
            'email': email or fake.email(),
            'password': password or 'Test@123456',
            **kwargs
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_project_data(
        name: Optional[str] = None,
        description: Optional[str] = None,
        status: str = 'active',
        **kwargs
    ) -> Dict:
        """生成项目数据"""
        return {
            'name': name or f'项目_{fake.company()}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4]}',
            'description': description or fake.text(),
            'status': status,
            **kwargs
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_testcase_data(
        title: Optional[str] = None,
        priority: str = 'P1',
        status: str = 'draft',
        **kwargs
    ) -> Dict:
        """生成测试用例数据"""
        return {
            'title': title or f'测试用例_{fake.sentence()}',
            'priority': priority,
            'status': status,
            **kwargs
        }
```

**方案2：Fixture数据管理**

```python
# conftest.py
import pytest
from data.factory import DataFactory
from api.project_api import ProjectAPI

@pytest.fixture
def test_user(db):
    """创建测试用户"""
    user_data = DataFactory.create_user_data()
    user = User.objects.create_user(**user_data)
    yield user
    # 清理数据
    user.delete()

@pytest.fixture
def test_project(db, test_user):
    """创建测试项目"""
    project_data = DataFactory.create_project_data()
    project = Project.objects.create(
        **project_data,
        created_by=test_user
    )
    yield project
    # 清理数据
    project.delete()

@pytest.fixture
def test_projects(db, test_user):
    """创建多个测试项目"""
    projects = []
    for i in range(5):
        project = Project.objects.create(
            name=f'测试项目_{i}',
            created_by=test_user
        )
        projects.append(project)
    
    yield projects
    
    # 清理数据
    for project in projects:
        project.delete()
```

**方案3：数据库事务管理**

```python
# conftest.py
import pytest
from django.db import transaction

@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def db_transaction():
    """
    数据库事务管理
    每个测试用例执行后自动回滚
    """
    # 开始事务
    transaction.set_autocommit(False)
    
    yield
    
    # 回滚事务
    transaction.rollback()
    transaction.set_autocommit(True)
```

**方案4：数据快照管理**

```python
# utils/db_snapshot.py
import json
from django.core.management import call_command
from io import StringIO

class DatabaseSnapshot:
    """
    数据库快照管理
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.snapshot = None
    
    def save(self):
        """保存快照"""
        output = StringIO()
        call_command('dumpdata', 'apps', stdout=output)
        self.snapshot = output.getvalue()
    
    def restore(self):
        """恢复快照"""
        if self.snapshot:
            call_command('loaddata', self.snapshot, format='json')

# 使用
@pytest.fixture(scope='module')
def db_snapshot():
    snapshot = DatabaseSnapshot()
    snapshot.save()
    yield
    snapshot.restore()
```

最后，我会说明选择策略：

| 场景 | 推荐方案 | 原因 |
|------|----------|------|
| 单元测试 | 数据库事务 | 快速、自动回滚 |
| API测试 | Fixture + 工厂模式 | 灵活、可复用 |
| UI测试 | 数据工厂 | 独立、可预测 |
| 性能测试 | 数据快照 | 可重复、稳定 |

**实际经验：** 在本项目中，我使用了Fixture + 工厂模式的组合方案：
- 使用工厂模式生成测试数据
- 使用Fixture管理数据生命周期
- 使用事务确保数据隔离

## 三、自动化测试开发

### 3.1 测试脚本开发

**Q6: 如何编写高质量的自动化测试脚本？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会遵循测试脚本编写原则：

**原则1：单一职责**
```python
# 不好的做法 - 一个测试验证多个功能
def test_project_operations():
    # 创建项目
    # 编辑项目
    # 删除项目
    # 验证所有操作
    pass

# 好的做法 - 每个测试验证一个功能
def test_create_project():
    """测试创建项目"""
    pass

def test_edit_project():
    """测试编辑项目"""
    pass

def test_delete_project():
    """测试删除项目"""
    pass
```

**原则2：独立性**
```python
# 不好的做法 - 测试之间有依赖
def test_create_project():
    global project_id
    project_id = create_project()

def test_delete_project():
    delete_project(project_id)  # 依赖上一个测试

# 好的做法 - 每个测试独立
def test_create_project():
    project_id = create_project()
    delete_project(project_id)  # 自己清理

def test_delete_project():
    project_id = create_project()  # 自己创建
    delete_project(project_id)
```

**原则3：可读性**
```python
# 不好的做法 - 魔法数字和硬编码
def test_login():
    page.fill('input', 'admin')
    page.fill('input:nth-child(2)', '123456')
    page.click('button')
    assert page.url == 'http://localhost:3000/dashboard'

# 好的做法 - 清晰的命名和常量
def test_login_success(login_page, dashboard_page):
    """测试登录成功"""
    # Given
    credentials = {'username': 'admin', 'password': 'Admin@123456'}
    
    # When
    login_page.login(**credentials)
    
    # Then
    assert dashboard_page.is_displayed()
```

其次，我会使用设计模式提升代码质量：

**模式1：Builder模式**
```python
# builders/testcase_builder.py
class TestCaseBuilder:
    """
    测试用例构建器
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.title = "默认标题"
        self.priority = "P1"
        self.status = "draft"
        self.steps = []
    
    def with_title(self, title: str):
        self.title = title
        return self
    
    def with_priority(self, priority: str):
        self.priority = priority
        return self
    
    def with_status(self, status: str):
        self.status = status
        return self
    
    def add_step(self, action: str, expected: str):
        self.steps.append({
            'action': action,
            'expected_result': expected
        })
        return self
    
    def build(self):
        return {
            'title': self.title,
            'priority': self.priority,
            'status': self.status,
            'steps': self.steps
        }

# 使用
testcase = (TestCaseBuilder()
    .with_title("登录测试")
    .with_priority("P0")
    .add_step("输入用户名", "用户名显示正确")
    .add_step("输入密码", "密码显示为密文")
    .build())
```

**模式2：Strategy模式**
```python
# strategies/wait_strategy.py
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

class WaitStrategy(ABC):
    """等待策略抽象类"""
    @abstractmethod
    def wait(self, page, selector):
        pass

class VisibleWaitStrategy(WaitStrategy):
    """等待元素可见"""
    def wait(self, page, selector):
        page.wait_for_selector(selector, state='visible')

class HiddenWaitStrategy(WaitStrategy):
    """等待元素隐藏"""
    def wait(self, page, selector):
        page.wait_for_selector(selector, state='hidden')

class TextWaitStrategy(WaitStrategy):
    """等待文本出现"""
    def __init__(self, text: str):
        self.text = text
    
    def wait(self, page, selector):
        page.wait_for_function(
            f'document.querySelector("{selector}").textContent.includes("{self.text}")'
        )

# 使用
def wait_for_element(page, selector, strategy: WaitStrategy):
    strategy.wait(page, selector)
```

最后，我会添加错误处理和日志：

```python
# utils/test_helper.py
import logging
import functools
from typing import Callable

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

def log_test(func: Callable):
    """测试日志装饰器"""
    @functools.wraps(func)
    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
        logger.info(f"开始测试: {func.__name__}")
        try:
            result = func(*args, **kwargs)
            logger.info(f"测试通过: {func.__name__}")
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"测试失败: {func.__name__}, 错误: {e}")
            raise
    return wrapper

def retry_on_failure(max_retries: int = 3, delay: int = 2):
    """失败重试装饰器"""
    def decorator(func: Callable):
        @functools.wraps(func)
        def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
            for attempt in range(max_retries):
                try:
                    return func(*args, **kwargs)
                except Exception as e:
                    if attempt == max_retries - 1:
                        raise
                    logger.warning(f"测试失败，第{attempt + 1}次重试: {e}")
                    import time
                    time.sleep(delay)
        return wrapper
    return decorator

# 使用
@log_test
@retry_on_failure(max_retries=3)
def test_create_project():
    """测试创建项目"""
    pass
```

### 3.2 测试报告生成

**Q7: 如何生成详细的测试报告？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会使用Allure生成测试报告：

```python
# tests/test_project.py
import pytest
import allure
from api.project_api import ProjectAPI

@allure.feature('项目管理')
@allure.story('项目创建')
@allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
@allure.title('创建项目成功')
@allure.description('验证用户可以成功创建新项目')
def test_create_project_success(project_api):
    """测试创建项目成功"""
    # Given
    project_data = {
        'name': '测试项目',
        'description': '项目描述'
    }
    
    # When
    with allure.step('发送创建项目请求'):
        response = project_api.create_project(project_data)
        
        # 添加请求附件
        allure.attach(
            str(project_data),
            name='请求数据',
            attachment_type=allure.attachment_type.JSON
        )
        
        # 添加响应附件
        allure.attach(
            str(response.data),
            name='响应数据',
            attachment_type=allure.attachment_type.JSON
        )
    
    # Then
    with allure.step('验证创建成功'):
        response.assert_success()
        assert response.data['name'] == project_data['name']
        
        # 添加截图
        allure.attach(
            project_api.page.screenshot(),
            name='项目列表截图',
            attachment_type=allure.attachment_type.PNG
        )
```

其次，我会自定义测试报告：

```python
# utils/report_generator.py
import json
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List

class TestReport:
    """
    测试报告生成器
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.results = []
        self.start_time = None
        self.end_time = None
    
    def start(self):
        """开始记录"""
        self.start_time = datetime.now()
    
    def end(self):
        """结束记录"""
        self.end_time = datetime.now()
    
    def add_result(
        self,
        test_name: str,
        status: str,
        duration: float,
        error: str = None
    ):
        """添加测试结果"""
        self.results.append({
            'test_name': test_name,
            'status': status,
            'duration': duration,
            'error': error,
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
    
    def generate(self) -> Dict:
        """生成报告"""
        total = len(self.results)
        passed = sum(1 for r in self.results if r['status'] == 'passed')
        failed = sum(1 for r in self.results if r['status'] == 'failed')
        
        return {
            'summary': {
                'total': total,
                'passed': passed,
                'failed': failed,
                'pass_rate': f"{passed / total * 100:.2f}%",
                'duration': str(self.end_time - self.start_time),
                'start_time': self.start_time.isoformat(),
                'end_time': self.end_time.isoformat()
            },
            'details': self.results
        }
    
    def save(self, filepath: str):
        """保存报告"""
        report = self.generate()
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(report, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

# 使用
@pytest.fixture(scope='session')
def test_report():
    report = TestReport()
    report.start()
    yield report
    report.end()
    report.save('reports/test_report.json')

@pytest.hookimpl(hookwrapper=True)
def pytest_runtest_makereport(item, call):
    outcome = yield
    report = outcome.get_result()
    
    if call.when == 'call':
        test_report = item.funcargs.get('test_report')
        if test_report:
            test_report.add_result(
                test_name=item.name,
                status='passed' if report.passed else 'failed',
                duration=call.duration,
                error=str(call.excinfo) if call.excinfo else None
            )
```

最后，我会集成到CI/CD：

```yaml

- name: Generate Allure Report
  uses: simple-elf/allure-report-action@v1
  with:
    allure_results: reports/allure-results
    allure_report: reports/allure-report

- name: Deploy Report
  uses: peaceiris/actions-gh-pages@v3
  with:
    github_token: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    publish_dir: reports/allure-report
```

## 四、测试工具开发

### 4.1 测试平台开发

**Q8: 如何设计一个测试用例管理系统？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析系统需求：

**功能需求：**
- 测试用例管理：创建、编辑、删除、查询
- 测试计划管理：创建计划、分配用例、执行测试
- 测试报告管理：生成报告、查看历史
- 用户权限管理：角色权限、团队协作

**非功能需求：**
- 性能：支持大量用例（10000+）
- 可用性：界面友好、操作便捷
- 扩展性：支持插件、API接口

其次，我会设计系统架构：

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│           前端层 (Vue3)                  │
│  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌────────┐│
│  │用例管理  │  │计划管理  │  │报告管理││
│  └──────────┘  └──────────┘  └────────┘│
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
                    ↓ REST API
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│           后端层 (Django)                │
│  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌────────┐│
│  │用例服务  │  │计划服务  │  │报告服务││
│  └──────────┘  └──────────┘  └────────┘│
│  ┌──────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │         认证授权服务              │  │
│  └──────────────────────────────────┘  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
                    ↓ ORM
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│           数据层 (PostgreSQL)           │
│  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌────────┐│
│  │用例表    │  │计划表    │  │报告表  ││
│  └──────────┘  └──────────┘  └────────┘│
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

然后，我会设计数据模型：

```python
# models/testcase.py
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User

class Project(models.Model):
    """项目模型"""
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='active')
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'projects'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

class TestCase(models.Model):
    """测试用例模型"""
    PRIORITY_CHOICES = [
        ('P0', 'P0-最高'),
        ('P1', 'P1-高'),
        ('P2', 'P2-中'),
        ('P3', 'P3-低'),
    ]
    
    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ('draft', '草稿'),
        ('reviewing', '评审中'),
        ('approved', '已通过'),
        ('rejected', '已拒绝'),
    ]
    
    project = models.ForeignKey(Project, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    priority = models.CharField(max_length=2, choices=PRIORITY_CHOICES, default='P2')
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default='draft')
    tags = models.JSONField(default=list)
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'testcases'
        ordering = ['-priority', '-created_at']

class TestStep(models.Model):
    """测试步骤模型"""
    testcase = models.ForeignKey(TestCase, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='steps')
    step_number = models.IntegerField()
    action = models.TextField()
    expected_result = models.TextField()
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'test_steps'
        ordering = ['step_number']

class TestPlan(models.Model):
    """测试计划模型"""
    project = models.ForeignKey(Project, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    testcases = models.ManyToManyField(TestCase, through='PlanTestCase')
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='pending')
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'test_plans'

class PlanTestCase(models.Model):
    """计划-用例关联模型"""
    plan = models.ForeignKey(TestPlan, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    testcase = models.ForeignKey(TestCase, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='pending')
    executed_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True)
    executed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True)
    
    class Meta:
        db_table = 'plan_testcases'
```

最后，我会实现核心API：

```python
# views/testcase.py
from rest_framework import viewsets, status
from rest_framework.decorators import action
from rest_framework.response import Response
from django.db.models import Q
from ..models import TestCase, TestStep
from ..serializers import TestCaseSerializer, TestStepSerializer

class TestCaseViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    测试用例视图集
    """
    serializer_class = TestCaseSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    
    def get_queryset(self):
        """获取查询集"""
        queryset = TestCase.objects.select_related('project', 'created_by')
        
        # 过滤条件
        project_id = self.request.query_params.get('project')
        if project_id:
            queryset = queryset.filter(project_id=project_id)
        
        priority = self.request.query_params.get('priority')
        if priority:
            queryset = queryset.filter(priority=priority)
        
        status = self.request.query_params.get('status')
        if status:
            queryset = queryset.filter(status=status)
        
        # 搜索
        search = self.request.query_params.get('search')
        if search:
            queryset = queryset.filter(
                Q(title__icontains=search) |
                Q(description__icontains=search)
            )
        
        return queryset
    
    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        """创建时自动设置创建者"""
        serializer.save(created_by=self.request.user)
    
    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'])
    def duplicate(self, request, pk=None):
        """复制测试用例"""
        testcase = self.get_object()
        
        # 复制用例
        new_testcase = TestCase.objects.create(
            project=testcase.project,
            title=f"{testcase.title} (副本)",
            description=testcase.description,
            priority=testcase.priority,
            created_by=request.user
        )
        
        # 复制步骤
        for step in testcase.steps.all():
            TestStep.objects.create(
                testcase=new_testcase,
                step_number=step.step_number,
                action=step.action,
                expected_result=step.expected_result
            )
        
        serializer = self.get_serializer(new_testcase)
        return Response(serializer.data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
    
    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'])
    def batch_delete(self, request):
        """批量删除"""
        ids = request.data.get('ids', [])
        TestCase.objects.filter(id__in=ids).delete()
        return Response({'message': f'成功删除{len(ids)}条用例'})
    
    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'])
    def statistics(self, request):
        """统计信息"""
        project_id = request.query_params.get('project')
        queryset = self.get_queryset()
        
        if project_id:
            queryset = queryset.filter(project_id=project_id)
        
        return Response({
            'total': queryset.count(),
            'by_priority': {
                'P0': queryset.filter(priority='P0').count(),
                'P1': queryset.filter(priority='P1').count(),
                'P2': queryset.filter(priority='P2').count(),
                'P3': queryset.filter(priority='P3').count(),
            },
            'by_status': {
                'draft': queryset.filter(status='draft').count(),
                'reviewing': queryset.filter(status='reviewing').count(),
                'approved': queryset.filter(status='approved').count(),
                'rejected': queryset.filter(status='rejected').count(),
            }
        })
```

### 4.2 测试工具开发

**Q9: 如何开发一个接口Mock工具？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析Mock工具的需求：
- 支持自定义响应数据
- 支持延迟响应
- 支持条件匹配
- 支持动态数据生成

其次，我会设计Mock工具：

```python
# tools/mock_server.py
from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
import json
import time
import re
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

class MockServer:
    """
    Mock服务器
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.app = Flask(__name__)
        self.rules = {}
        self._setup_routes()
    
    def add_rule(
        self,
        path: str,
        method: str = 'GET',
        response: Any = None,
        delay: float = 0,
        condition: Optional[Dict] = None
    ):
        """
        添加Mock规则
        
        Args:
            path: API路径
            method: HTTP方法
            response: 响应数据
            delay: 延迟时间（秒）
            condition: 匹配条件
        """
        key = f"{method.upper()}:{path}"
        self.rules[key] = {
            'response': response,
            'delay': delay,
            'condition': condition
        }
    
    def _setup_routes(self):
        """设置路由"""
        @self.app.route('/<path:path>', methods=['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'DELETE'])
        def handle_request(path):
            method = request.method
            key = f"{method}:/{path}"
            
            # 查找匹配的规则
            rule = self._find_rule(key, request)
            
            if not rule:
                return jsonify({'error': 'No mock rule found'}), 404
            
            # 延迟响应
            if rule['delay'] > 0:
                time.sleep(rule['delay'])
            
            # 返回响应
            return jsonify(rule['response'])
        
        @self.app.route('/mock/rules', methods=['GET'])
        def get_rules():
            """获取所有规则"""
            return jsonify(self.rules)
        
        @self.app.route('/mock/rules', methods=['POST'])
        def add_rule():
            """添加规则"""
            data = request.json
            self.add_rule(
                path=data['path'],
                method=data.get('method', 'GET'),
                response=data.get('response'),
                delay=data.get('delay', 0),
                condition=data.get('condition')
            )
            return jsonify({'message': 'Rule added successfully'})
    
    def _find_rule(self, key: str, request) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """查找匹配的规则"""
        # 精确匹配
        if key in self.rules:
            rule = self.rules[key]
            if self._match_condition(rule, request):
                return rule
        
        # 正则匹配
        for pattern, rule in self.rules.items():
            if re.match(pattern.replace('*', '.*'), key):
                if self._match_condition(rule, request):
                    return rule
        
        return None
    
    def _match_condition(self, rule: Dict, request) -> bool:
        """匹配条件"""
        if not rule.get('condition'):
            return True
        
        condition = rule['condition']
        
        # 检查请求头
        if 'headers' in condition:
            for key, value in condition['headers'].items():
                if request.headers.get(key) != value:
                    return False
        
        # 检查请求参数
        if 'params' in condition:
            for key, value in condition['params'].items():
                if request.args.get(key) != value:
                    return False
        
        # 检查请求体
        if 'body' in condition:
            body = request.json or {}
            for key, value in condition['body'].items():
                if body.get(key) != value:
                    return False
        
        return True
    
    def run(self, host='0.0.0.0', port=5000):
        """启动服务器"""
        self.app.run(host=host, port=port)

# 使用示例
if __name__ == '__main__':
    mock = MockServer()
    
    # 添加规则
    mock.add_rule(
        path='/api/users',
        method='GET',
        response={
            'code': 200,
            'data': [
                {'id': 1, 'name': '张三'},
                {'id': 2, 'name': '李四'}
            ]
        }
    )
    
    # 添加带条件的规则
    mock.add_rule(
        path='/api/projects',
        method='GET',
        response={
            'code': 200,
            'data': {'name': '测试项目'}
        },
        condition={
            'headers': {'Authorization': 'Bearer test_token'}
        }
    )
    
    # 添加延迟响应
    mock.add_rule(
        path='/api/slow',
        method='GET',
        response={'code': 200, 'data': 'slow response'},
        delay=2  # 延迟2秒
    )
    
    mock.run(port=5001)
```

然后，我会实现动态数据生成：

```python
# tools/dynamic_response.py
import random
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from faker import Faker

fake = Faker('zh_CN')

class DynamicResponse:
    """
    动态响应生成器
    """
    
    @staticmethod
    def generate_user():
        """生成用户数据"""
        return {
            'id': random.randint(1, 10000),
            'username': fake.user_name(),
            'email': fake.email(),
            'phone': fake.phone_number(),
            'created_at': fake.date_time_this_year().isoformat()
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def generate_project():
        """生成项目数据"""
        return {
            'id': random.randint(1, 10000),
            'name': fake.company(),
            'description': fake.text(),
            'status': random.choice(['active', 'archived']),
            'created_at': fake.date_time_this_year().isoformat()
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def generate_testcase():
        """生成测试用例数据"""
        return {
            'id': random.randint(1, 10000),
            'title': fake.sentence(),
            'priority': random.choice(['P0', 'P1', 'P2', 'P3']),
            'status': random.choice(['draft', 'reviewing', 'approved']),
            'created_at': fake.date_time_this_year().isoformat()
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def generate_list(generator, count=10):
        """生成列表数据"""
        return [generator() for _ in range(count)]

# 在Mock中使用
mock.add_rule(
    path='/api/users',
    method='GET',
    response=lambda: {
        'code': 200,
        'data': DynamicResponse.generate_list(DynamicResponse.generate_user)
    }
)
```
