## 五、持续集成与持续交付

### 5.1 CI/CD流程设计

**Q10: 如何设计完整的CI/CD流程？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会设计CI/CD流程：

```
代码提交 → 代码检查 → 单元测试 → 构建 → 部署测试环境 → 自动化测试 → 部署生产环境
    ↓          ↓          ↓        ↓          ↓              ↓            ↓
  Git Hook  Lint/Sonar  Pytest  Docker   Kubernetes      API/UI      Blue-Green
```

其次，我会实现CI流程：

```yaml
# .github/workflows/ci.yml
name: CI Pipeline

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, develop]
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]

env:
  PYTHON_VERSION: '3.11'
  NODE_VERSION: '18'

jobs:
  # 代码检查
  lint:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
      
      - name: Setup Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v4
        with:
          python-version: ${{ env.PYTHON_VERSION }}
      
      - name: Install linters
        run: |
          pip install flake8 black isort mypy pylint
      
      - name: Run Black
        run: black --check .
      
      - name: Run isort
        run: isort --check-only .
      
      - name: Run Flake8
        run: flake8 . --max-line-length=100
      
      - name: Run MyPy
        run: mypy apps/ --ignore-missing-imports

  # 单元测试
  unit-test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: lint
    
    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:14
        env:
          POSTGRES_DB: test_db
          POSTGRES_USER: postgres
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
        ports:
          - 5432:5432
        options: --health-cmd pg_isready --health-interval 10s
    
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
      
      - name: Setup Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v4
        with:
          python-version: ${{ env.PYTHON_VERSION }}
          cache: 'pip'
      
      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          pip install -r requirements.txt
          pip install pytest pytest-cov pytest-django
      
      - name: Run unit tests
        env:
          DATABASE_URL: postgres://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/test_db
        run: |
          pytest tests/unit --cov=apps --cov-report=xml --cov-report=html
      
      - name: Upload coverage
        uses: codecov/codecov-action@v3
        with:
          file: ./coverage.xml

  # API测试
  api-test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: unit-test
    
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
      
      - name: Setup Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v4
        with:
          python-version: ${{ env.PYTHON_VERSION }}
          cache: 'pip'
      
      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          pip install -r requirements.txt
          pip install -r tests/api/requirements.txt
      
      - name: Start server
        run: |
          python manage.py migrate
          python manage.py runserver 0.0.0.0:8000 &
          sleep 5
      
      - name: Run API tests
        run: |
          cd tests/api
          pytest --alluredir=reports/allure-results
      
      - name: Upload Allure results
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v3
        with:
          name: allure-results
          path: reports/allure-results

  # UI测试
  ui-test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: unit-test
    
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
      
      - name: Setup Node
        uses: actions/setup-node@v3
        with:
          node-version: ${{ env.NODE_VERSION }}
          cache: 'npm'
          cache-dependency-path: vue3-frontend/package-lock.json
      
      - name: Setup Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v4
        with:
          python-version: ${{ env.PYTHON_VERSION }}
      
      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          cd vue3-frontend
          npm install
          pip install -r requirements.txt
          pip install playwright pytest-playwright
          playwright install chromium
      
      - name: Start services
        run: |
          python manage.py migrate
          python manage.py runserver 0.0.0.0:8000 &
          cd vue3-frontend && npm run dev &
          sleep 10
      
      - name: Run UI tests
        run: |
          cd tests/ui_py
          pytest --alluredir=reports/allure-results
      
      - name: Upload test results
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v3
        with:
          name: ui-test-results
          path: tests/ui_py/reports

  # 构建Docker镜像
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: [api-test, ui-test]
    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
    
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
      
      - name: Set up Docker Buildx
        uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v2
      
      - name: Login to Docker Hub
        uses: docker/login-action@v2
        with:
          username: ${{ secrets.DOCKER_USERNAME }}
          password: ${{ secrets.DOCKER_PASSWORD }}
      
      - name: Build and push
        uses: docker/build-push-action@v4
        with:
          context: .
          push: true
          tags: |
            myapp:${{ github.sha }}
            myapp:latest
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max

  # 部署到测试环境
  deploy-staging:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: build
    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/develop'
    
    steps:
      - name: Deploy to staging
        uses: appleboy/ssh-action@master
        with:
          host: ${{ secrets.STAGING_HOST }}
          username: ${{ secrets.SSH_USERNAME }}
          key: ${{ secrets.SSH_KEY }}
          script: |
            cd /app
            docker-compose pull
            docker-compose up -d
            docker-compose exec web python manage.py migrate

  # 部署到生产环境
  deploy-production:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: build
    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
    
    steps:
      - name: Deploy to production
        uses: appleboy/ssh-action@master
        with:
          host: ${{ secrets.PRODUCTION_HOST }}
          username: ${{ secrets.SSH_USERNAME }}
          key: ${{ secrets.SSH_KEY }}
          script: |
            cd /app
            # Blue-Green部署
            docker-compose -f docker-compose.blue.yml up -d
            sleep 10
            # 健康检查
            curl -f http://localhost:8000/health || exit 1
            # 切换流量
            docker-compose -f docker-compose.green.yml down
```

## 六、综合能力

### 6.1 问题解决能力

**Q11: 测试环境不稳定导致自动化测试频繁失败，如何解决？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析问题原因：

| 问题类型 | 具体表现 | 根本原因 |
|----------|----------|----------|
| 环境问题 | 服务启动慢、数据库连接失败 | 资源不足、配置错误 |
| 数据问题 | 测试数据冲突、数据被污染 | 数据隔离不足 |
| 网络问题 | 请求超时、连接断开 | 网络不稳定 |
| 时序问题 | 元素未加载、操作未完成 | 等待时间不够 |

其次，我会实施解决方案：

**方案1：环境健康检查**

```python
# utils/health_check.py
import requests
import time
from typing import List

class HealthChecker:
    """
    环境健康检查
    """
    def __init__(self, services: List[dict]):
        self.services = services
    
    def check_all(self, timeout: int = 60) -> bool:
        """检查所有服务"""
        start_time = time.time()
        
        while time.time() - start_time < timeout:
            all_healthy = True
            
            for service in self.services:
                if not self._check_service(service):
                    all_healthy = False
                    break
            
            if all_healthy:
                return True
            
            time.sleep(2)
        
        return False
    
    def _check_service(self, service: dict) -> bool:
        """检查单个服务"""
        try:
            response = requests.get(
                service['url'],
                timeout=5
            )
            return response.status_code == service.get('expected_status', 200)
        except:
            return False

# conftest.py
@pytest.fixture(scope='session', autouse=True)
def check_environment():
    """检查环境健康"""
    checker = HealthChecker([
        {'name': 'backend', 'url': 'http://localhost:8000/health'},
        {'name': 'frontend', 'url': 'http://localhost:3000'},
        {'name': 'database', 'url': 'http://localhost:8000/db-health'},
    ])
    
    if not checker.check_all():
        pytest.fail("环境健康检查失败，请检查服务状态")
```

**方案2：智能重试机制**

```python
# utils/retry.py
import functools
import time
from typing import Callable, List

def smart_retry(
    max_retries: int = 3,
    delay: float = 2,
    exceptions: List[Exception] = None
):
    """
    智能重试装饰器
    
    Args:
        max_retries: 最大重试次数
        delay: 重试延迟
        exceptions: 需要重试的异常类型
    """
    if exceptions is None:
        exceptions = [Exception]
    
    def decorator(func: Callable):
        @functools.wraps(func)
        def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
            last_exception = None
            
            for attempt in range(max_retries):
                try:
                    return func(*args, **kwargs)
                except tuple(exceptions) as e:
                    last_exception = e
                    
                    if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                        # 指数退避
                        wait_time = delay * (2 ** attempt)
                        time.sleep(wait_time)
            
            raise last_exception
        
        return wrapper
    
    return decorator

# 使用
@smart_retry(
    max_retries=3,
    delay=1,
    exceptions=[requests.exceptions.Timeout, requests.exceptions.ConnectionError]
)
def test_api_call():
    """测试API调用"""
    response = requests.get('http://localhost:8000/api/projects/')
    assert response.status_code == 200
```

**方案3：数据隔离**

```python
# utils/data_isolation.py
import uuid
from contextlib import contextmanager

class DataIsolation:
    """
    数据隔离管理
    """
    
    @staticmethod
    def generate_unique_id():
        """生成唯一ID"""
        return uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
    
    @staticmethod
    @contextmanager
    def isolated_data(db_session, model_class, data: dict):
        """
        隔离数据上下文管理器
        
        Args:
            db_session: 数据库会话
            model_class: 模型类
            data: 数据字典
        """
        # 添加唯一标识
        unique_id = DataIsolation.generate_unique_id()
        if 'name' in data:
            data['name'] = f"{data['name']}_{unique_id}"
        
        # 创建数据
        instance = model_class(**data)
        db_session.add(instance)
        db_session.commit()
        
        try:
            yield instance
        finally:
            # 清理数据
            db_session.delete(instance)
            db_session.commit()

# 使用
def test_create_project(db_session):
    """测试创建项目"""
    with DataIsolation.isolated_data(
        db_session,
        Project,
        {'name': '测试项目', 'description': '项目描述'}
    ) as project:
        # 测试逻辑
        assert project.name.startswith('测试项目_')
```

最后，我会建立监控告警：

```python
# utils/monitoring.py
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List

class TestMonitor:
    """
    测试监控
    """
    def __init__(self):
        self.failures = []
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    
    def record_failure(self, test_name: str, error: str):
        """记录失败"""
        self.failures.append({
            'test_name': test_name,
            'error': error,
            'timestamp': datetime.now()
        })
    
    def analyze_failures(self) -> Dict:
        """分析失败"""
        if not self.failures:
            return {'status': 'healthy'}
        
        # 统计失败类型
        error_types = {}
        for failure in self.failures:
            error_type = type(failure['error']).__name__
            error_types[error_type] = error_types.get(error_type, 0) + 1
        
        # 判断环境是否稳定
        total_tests = len(self.failures)
        if total_tests > 10:
            return {
                'status': 'unstable',
                'error_types': error_types,
                'recommendation': '环境不稳定，建议检查服务状态'
            }
        
        return {
            'status': 'normal',
            'error_types': error_types
        }
    
    def send_alert(self, message: str):
        """发送告警"""
        # 发送到钉钉/企业微信/邮件
        self.logger.warning(f"测试告警: {message}")
```

### 6.2 技术选型能力

**Q12: 如何选择合适的测试框架和工具？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析选型因素：

| 因素 | 说明 | 权重 |
|------|------|------|
| 团队技能 | 团队熟悉的技术栈 | ★★★★★ |
| 项目需求 | 功能需求、性能需求 | ★★★★★ |
| 社区支持 | 文档、社区活跃度 | ★★★★ |
| 学习成本 | 上手难度、培训成本 | ★★★★ |
| 扩展性 | 插件、二次开发 | ★★★ |
| 成本 | 开源/商业、维护成本 | ★★★ |

其次，我会对比主流测试框架：

**API测试框架对比：**

| 框架 | 优势 | 劣势 | 适用场景 |
|------|------|------|----------|
| Pytest | 简洁、插件丰富、Fixture强大 | 需要Python基础 | Python项目 |
| REST Assured | DSL语法、Java生态 | 只支持Java | Java项目 |
| Postman | 可视化、易上手 | 自动化能力弱 | 手工测试、快速验证 |
| Karate | BDD语法、功能全面 | 社区较小 | 全栈测试 |

**UI测试框架对比：**

| 框架 | 优势 | 劣势 | 适用场景 |
|------|------|------|----------|
| Playwright | 自动等待、多浏览器、调试工具 | 较新、社区小 | 现代Web应用 |
| Selenium | 成熟、社区大、支持多语言 | 需要显式等待、速度慢 | 传统Web应用 |
| Cypress | 快速、调试友好 | 只支持JS、跨域限制 | 前端团队 |
| Puppeteer | Chrome官方、功能强大 | 只支持Chrome | Chrome专项测试 |

**性能测试工具对比：**

| 工具 | 优势 | 劣势 | 适用场景 |
|------|------|------|----------|
| Locust | Python编写、分布式、可编程 | UI简单 | Python团队 |
| JMeter | 功能全面、插件丰富 | GUI重、脚本复杂 | 传统性能测试 |
| K6 | Go编写、JS脚本、云原生 | 社区较小 | 云原生项目 |
| Gatling | 高性能、Scala DSL | 需要Scala基础 | 高并发场景 |

最后，我会给出本项目的选择：

## 八、性能测试开发

### 8.1 性能测试框架

**Q15: 如何设计一个性能测试框架？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析性能测试框架的需求：
- **脚本编写**：支持多种协议（HTTP、WebSocket、gRPC）
- **场景设计**：支持并发、阶梯、峰值等场景
- **数据收集**：实时收集性能指标
- **报告生成**：生成详细的性能报告

其次，我会设计框架架构：

```python
# framework/performance_framework.py
import time
import threading
import statistics
from typing import Callable, Dict, List, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import requests

@dataclass
class PerformanceMetrics:
    """性能指标数据类"""
    total_requests: int = 0
    successful_requests: int = 0
    failed_requests: int = 0
    total_time: float = 0.0
    min_response_time: float = float('inf')
    max_response_time: float = 0.0
    response_times: List[float] = None
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        if self.response_times is None:
            self.response_times = []
    
    @property
    def avg_response_time(self) -> float:
        if not self.response_times:
            return 0
        return statistics.mean(self.response_times)
    
    @property
    def p50(self) -> float:
        return self._percentile(50)
    
    @property
    def p95(self) -> float:
        return self._percentile(95)
    
    @property
    def p99(self) -> float:
        return self._percentile(99)
    
    @property
    def rps(self) -> float:
        if self.total_time == 0:
            return 0
        return self.total_requests / self.total_time
    
    @property
    def error_rate(self) -> float:
        if self.total_requests == 0:
            return 0
        return self.failed_requests / self.total_requests * 100
    
    def _percentile(self, percentile: int) -> float:
        if not self.response_times:
            return 0
        sorted_times = sorted(self.response_times)
        index = int(len(sorted_times) * percentile / 100)
        return sorted_times[min(index, len(sorted_times) - 1)]

class PerformanceTestFramework:
    """
    性能测试框架
    """
    def __init__(self, base_url: str):
        self.base_url = base_url
        self.metrics = PerformanceMetrics()
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
    
    def run_test(
        self,
        test_func: Callable,
        concurrent_users: int = 10,
        duration: int = 60,
        ramp_up: int = 10
    ):
        """
        运行性能测试
        
        Args:
            test_func: 测试函数
            concurrent_users: 并发用户数
            duration: 测试持续时间（秒）
            ramp_up: 爬坡时间（秒）
        """
        start_time = time.time()
        
        # 计算每个用户的启动间隔
        interval = ramp_up / concurrent_users if concurrent_users > 0 else 0
        
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=concurrent_users) as executor:
            futures = []
            
            for i in range(concurrent_users):
                # 爬坡启动
                time.sleep(interval)
                
                # 提交任务
                future = executor.submit(
                    self._run_user_test,
                    test_func,
                    start_time,
                    duration
                )
                futures.append(future)
            
            # 等待所有任务完成
            for future in futures:
                future.result()
        
        self.metrics.total_time = time.time() - start_time
    
    def _run_user_test(
        self,
        test_func: Callable,
        start_time: float,
        duration: int
    ):
        """运行单个用户测试"""
        while time.time() - start_time < duration:
            try:
                request_start = time.time()
                test_func()
                request_time = time.time() - request_start
                
                with self.lock:
                    self.metrics.total_requests += 1
                    self.metrics.successful_requests += 1
                    self.metrics.response_times.append(request_time)
                    self.metrics.min_response_time = min(
                        self.metrics.min_response_time,
                        request_time
                    )
                    self.metrics.max_response_time = max(
                        self.metrics.max_response_time,
                        request_time
                    )
            except Exception as e:
                with self.lock:
                    self.metrics.total_requests += 1
                    self.metrics.failed_requests += 1
    
    def generate_report(self) -> Dict:
        """生成性能报告"""
        return {
            'summary': {
                'total_requests': self.metrics.total_requests,
                'successful_requests': self.metrics.successful_requests,
                'failed_requests': self.metrics.failed_requests,
                'error_rate': f"{self.metrics.error_rate:.2f}%",
                'total_time': f"{self.metrics.total_time:.2f}s",
                'rps': f"{self.metrics.rps:.2f}",
            },
            'response_time': {
                'min': f"{self.metrics.min_response_time:.2f}ms",
                'max': f"{self.metrics.max_response_time:.2f}ms",
                'avg': f"{self.metrics.avg_response_time:.2f}ms",
                'p50': f"{self.metrics.p50:.2f}ms",
                'p95': f"{self.metrics.p95:.2f}ms",
                'p99': f"{self.metrics.p99:.2f}ms",
            }
        }

# 使用示例
def test_api_performance():
    """测试API性能"""
    framework = PerformanceTestFramework('http://localhost:8000')
    
    def api_call():
        response = requests.get('http://localhost:8000/api/projects/')
        assert response.status_code == 200
    
    framework.run_test(
        test_func=api_call,
        concurrent_users=50,
        duration=60,
        ramp_up=10
    )
    
    report = framework.generate_report()
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
```

然后，我会实现场景设计：

```python
# framework/scenarios.py
from typing import List, Dict
from enum import Enum

class ScenarioType(Enum):
    """场景类型"""
    CONCURRENT = "concurrent"      # 并发测试
    STRESS = "stress"              # 压力测试
    SPIKE = "spike"                # 峰值测试
    ENDURANCE = "endurance"        # 耐久测试

class TestScenario:
    """
    测试场景
    """
    def __init__(self, name: str, scenario_type: ScenarioType):
        self.name = name
        self.scenario_type = scenario_type
        self.steps = []
    
    def add_step(
        self,
        users: int,
        duration: int,
        ramp_up: int = 0
    ):
        """添加测试步骤"""
        self.steps.append({
            'users': users,
            'duration': duration,
            'ramp_up': ramp_up
        })
        return self

class ScenarioBuilder:
    """
    场景构建器
    """
    
    @staticmethod
    def build_concurrent_scenario(users: int, duration: int) -> TestScenario:
        """构建并发测试场景"""
        return (TestScenario("并发测试", ScenarioType.CONCURRENT)
            .add_step(users=users, duration=duration, ramp_up=10))
    
    @staticmethod
    def build_stress_scenario(
        start_users: int,
        max_users: int,
        step: int,
        step_duration: int
    ) -> TestScenario:
        """构建压力测试场景"""
        scenario = TestScenario("压力测试", ScenarioType.STRESS)
        
        for users in range(start_users, max_users + 1, step):
            scenario.add_step(users=users, duration=step_duration, ramp_up=5)
        
        return scenario
    
    @staticmethod
    def build_spike_scenario(
        normal_users: int,
        peak_users: int,
        normal_duration: int,
        spike_duration: int
    ) -> TestScenario:
        """构建峰值测试场景"""
        return (TestScenario("峰值测试", ScenarioType.SPIKE)
            .add_step(users=normal_users, duration=normal_duration, ramp_up=10)
            .add_step(users=peak_users, duration=spike_duration, ramp_up=0)
            .add_step(users=normal_users, duration=normal_duration, ramp_up=0))
    
    @staticmethod
    def build_endurance_scenario(users: int, duration: int) -> TestScenario:
        """构建耐久测试场景"""
        return (TestScenario("耐久测试", ScenarioType.ENDURANCE)
            .add_step(users=users, duration=duration, ramp_up=30))

# 使用示例
# 压力测试场景：从10用户逐步增加到100用户
stress_scenario = ScenarioBuilder.build_stress_scenario(
    start_users=10,
    max_users=100,
    step=10,
    step_duration=60
)

# 峰值测试场景：正常50用户，峰值200用户
spike_scenario = ScenarioBuilder.build_spike_scenario(
    normal_users=50,
    peak_users=200,
    normal_duration=300,
    spike_duration=60
)
```

最后，我会实现实时监控：

```python
# framework/monitor.py
import time
import threading
from collections import deque
from typing import Callable, Optional

class RealTimeMonitor:
    """
    实时监控
    """
    def __init__(
        self,
        metrics: PerformanceMetrics,
        interval: int = 5,
        callback: Optional[Callable] = None
    ):
        self.metrics = metrics
        self.interval = interval
        self.callback = callback
        self.history = deque(maxlen=100)
        self._running = False
        self._thread = None
    
    def start(self):
        """启动监控"""
        self._running = True
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._monitor_loop)
        self._thread.daemon = True
        self._thread.start()
    
    def stop(self):
        """停止监控"""
        self._running = False
        if self._thread:
            self._thread.join()
    
    def _monitor_loop(self):
        """监控循环"""
        while self._running:
            snapshot = {
                'timestamp': time.time(),
                'total_requests': self.metrics.total_requests,
                'rps': self.metrics.rps,
                'avg_response_time': self.metrics.avg_response_time,
                'error_rate': self.metrics.error_rate
            }
            
            self.history.append(snapshot)
            
            if self.callback:
                self.callback(snapshot)
            
            time.sleep(self.interval)
    
    def get_trend(self) -> list:
        """获取趋势数据"""
        return list(self.history)
```

### 8.2 性能测试工具开发

**Q16: 如何开发一个性能测试分析工具？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析性能测试数据：

```python
# tools/performance_analyzer.py
import json
import statistics
from typing import Dict, List
from datetime import datetime

class PerformanceAnalyzer:
    """
    性能测试分析工具
    """
    
    def __init__(self, test_results: List[Dict]):
        self.results = test_results
    
    def analyze_response_time(self) -> Dict:
        """分析响应时间"""
        response_times = [r['response_time'] for r in self.results]
        
        return {
            'min': min(response_times),
            'max': max(response_times),
            'avg': statistics.mean(response_times),
            'median': statistics.median(response_times),
            'stdev': statistics.stdev(response_times) if len(response_times) > 1 else 0,
            'p50': self._percentile(response_times, 50),
            'p90': self._percentile(response_times, 90),
            'p95': self._percentile(response_times, 95),
            'p99': self._percentile(response_times, 99),
        }
    
    def analyze_throughput(self) -> Dict:
        """分析吞吐量"""
        total_requests = len(self.results)
        
        if not self.results:
            return {'rps': 0, 'total': 0}
        
        start_time = min(r['timestamp'] for r in self.results)
        end_time = max(r['timestamp'] for r in self.results)
        duration = end_time - start_time
        
        return {
            'rps': total_requests / duration if duration > 0 else 0,
            'total': total_requests,
            'duration': duration
        }
    
    def analyze_errors(self) -> Dict:
        """分析错误"""
        errors = [r for r in self.results if not r.get('success', True)]
        
        error_types = {}
        for error in errors:
            error_type = error.get('error_type', 'Unknown')
            error_types[error_type] = error_types.get(error_type, 0) + 1
        
        return {
            'total_errors': len(errors),
            'error_rate': len(errors) / len(self.results) * 100 if self.results else 0,
            'error_types': error_types
        }
    
    def analyze_trend(self, interval: int = 10) -> List[Dict]:
        """分析趋势"""
        if not self.results:
            return []
        
        # 按时间分组
        start_time = min(r['timestamp'] for r in self.results)
        end_time = max(r['timestamp'] for r in self.results)
        
        trends = []
        current_time = start_time
        
        while current_time < end_time:
            # 获取当前时间段的请求
            interval_results = [
                r for r in self.results
                if current_time <= r['timestamp'] < current_time + interval
            ]
            
            if interval_results:
                response_times = [r['response_time'] for r in interval_results]
                errors = [r for r in interval_results if not r.get('success', True)]
                
                trends.append({
                    'timestamp': current_time,
                    'requests': len(interval_results),
                    'rps': len(interval_results) / interval,
                    'avg_response_time': statistics.mean(response_times),
                    'error_rate': len(errors) / len(interval_results) * 100
                })
            
            current_time += interval
        
        return trends
    
    def detect_anomalies(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """检测异常"""
        anomalies = []
        response_times = [r['response_time'] for r in self.results]
        
        if len(response_times) < 10:
            return anomalies
        
        # 使用IQR方法检测异常值
        q1 = statistics.quantiles(response_times, n=4)[0]
        q3 = statistics.quantiles(response_times, n=4)[2]
        iqr = q3 - q1
        
        lower_bound = q1 - 1.5 * iqr
        upper_bound = q3 + 1.5 * iqr
        
        for i, result in enumerate(self.results):
            if result['response_time'] < lower_bound or result['response_time'] > upper_bound:
                anomalies.append({
                    'index': i,
                    'response_time': result['response_time'],
                    'type': 'slow' if result['response_time'] > upper_bound else 'fast'
                })
        
        return anomalies
    
    def generate_recommendations(self) -> List[str]:
        """生成优化建议"""
        recommendations = []
        
        response_analysis = self.analyze_response_time()
        error_analysis = self.analyze_errors()
        throughput_analysis = self.analyze_throughput()
        
        # 响应时间建议
        if response_analysis['p95'] > 1000:
            recommendations.append(
                f"P95响应时间({response_analysis['p95']:.0f}ms)过高，"
                "建议优化数据库查询或添加缓存"
            )
        
        if response_analysis['stdev'] > response_analysis['avg'] * 0.5:
            recommendations.append(
                "响应时间波动较大，建议检查是否存在资源竞争或GC问题"
            )
        
        # 错误率建议
        if error_analysis['error_rate'] > 1:
            recommendations.append(
                f"错误率({error_analysis['error_rate']:.2f}%)过高，"
                "建议检查服务稳定性和错误处理"
            )
        
        # 吞吐量建议
        if throughput_analysis['rps'] < 100:
            recommendations.append(
                f"吞吐量({throughput_analysis['rps']:.2f} RPS)较低，"
                "建议增加服务器资源或优化代码性能"
            )
        
        return recommendations
    
    def _percentile(self, data: List[float], percentile: int) -> float:
        """计算百分位数"""
        sorted_data = sorted(data)
        index = int(len(sorted_data) * percentile / 100)
        return sorted_data[min(index, len(sorted_data) - 1)]
    
    def generate_report(self) -> Dict:
        """生成完整报告"""
        return {
            'summary': {
                'total_requests': len(self.results),
                'response_time': self.analyze_response_time(),
                'throughput': self.analyze_throughput(),
                'errors': self.analyze_errors(),
            },
            'trend': self.analyze_trend(),
            'anomalies': self.detect_anomalies(),
            'recommendations': self.generate_recommendations()
        }
```

## 九、安全测试开发

### 9.1 安全测试框架

**Q17: 如何设计一个安全测试框架？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会设计安全测试框架架构：

```python
# framework/security_framework.py
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import requests

class Severity(Enum):
    """漏洞严重程度"""
    CRITICAL = "critical"
    HIGH = "high"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    LOW = "low"
    INFO = "info"

@dataclass
class Vulnerability:
    """漏洞数据类"""
    name: str
    severity: Severity
    description: str
    url: str
    payload: str
    response: str
    recommendation: str

class SecurityScanner(ABC):
    """安全扫描器基类"""
    
    def __init__(self, base_url: str):
        self.base_url = base_url
        self.vulnerabilities: List[Vulnerability] = []
    
    @abstractmethod
    def scan(self) -> List[Vulnerability]:
        """执行扫描"""
        pass
    
    def add_vulnerability(self, vuln: Vulnerability):
        """添加漏洞"""
        self.vulnerabilities.append(vuln)
    
    def get_report(self) -> Dict:
        """获取报告"""
        severity_count = {}
        for severity in Severity:
            severity_count[severity.value] = sum(
                1 for v in self.vulnerabilities if v.severity == severity
            )
        
        return {
            'total': len(self.vulnerabilities),
            'by_severity': severity_count,
            'vulnerabilities': [
                {
                    'name': v.name,
                    'severity': v.severity.value,
                    'description': v.description,
                    'url': v.url,
                    'recommendation': v.recommendation
                }
                for v in self.vulnerabilities
            ]
        }

class SQLInjectionScanner(SecurityScanner):
    """SQL注入扫描器"""
    
    PAYLOADS = [
        "' OR '1'='1",
        "' OR '1'='1' --",
        "' OR '1'='1' /*",
        "1' OR '1' = '1",
        "admin'--",
        "'; DROP TABLE users; --",
        "1; SELECT * FROM users",
    ]
    
    ERROR_PATTERNS = [
        'sql syntax',
        'mysql_fetch',
        'ORA-',
        'PostgreSQL',
        'SQLite3::SQLException',
        'Warning: mysql_',
    ]
    
    def scan(self) -> List[Vulnerability]:
        """扫描SQL注入"""
        # 扫描登录接口
        self._scan_login()
        
        # 扫描搜索接口
        self._scan_search()
        
        return self.vulnerabilities
    
    def _scan_login(self):
        """扫描登录接口"""
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/users/login/"
        
        for payload in self.PAYLOADS:
            try:
                response = requests.post(url, json={
                    'username': payload,
                    'password': 'test'
                }, timeout=10)
                
                # 检查是否成功绕过认证
                if response.status_code == 200:
                    data = response.json()
                    if 'token' in data.get('data', {}):
                        self.add_vulnerability(Vulnerability(
                            name='SQL Injection in Login',
                            severity=Severity.CRITICAL,
                            description='登录接口存在SQL注入漏洞，可绕过认证',
                            url=url,
                            payload=payload,
                            response=response.text[:500],
                            recommendation='使用参数化查询，避免直接拼接SQL'
                        ))
                
                # 检查是否有错误信息泄露
                for pattern in self.ERROR_PATTERNS:
                    if pattern.lower() in response.text.lower():
                        self.add_vulnerability(Vulnerability(
                            name='SQL Error Information Disclosure',
                            severity=Severity.MEDIUM,
                            description='SQL错误信息泄露，可能暴露数据库结构',
                            url=url,
                            payload=payload,
                            response=response.text[:500],
                            recommendation='捕获SQL异常，返回通用错误信息'
                        ))
                        break
                        
            except Exception as e:
                pass
    
    def _scan_search(self):
        """扫描搜索接口"""
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/projects/"
        
        for payload in self.PAYLOADS:
            try:
                response = requests.get(url, params={
                    'search': payload
                }, timeout=10)
                
                # 检查是否有异常数据返回
                if response.status_code == 200:
                    data = response.json()
                    # 如果返回的数据量异常大，可能存在注入
                    if isinstance(data.get('data'), list) and len(data['data']) > 100:
                        self.add_vulnerability(Vulnerability(
                            name='SQL Injection in Search',
                            severity=Severity.HIGH,
                            description='搜索接口存在SQL注入风险',
                            url=url,
                            payload=payload,
                            response=f"返回{len(data['data'])}条数据",
                            recommendation='对搜索参数进行严格验证和转义'
                        ))
                        
            except Exception as e:
                pass

class XSSScanner(SecurityScanner):
    """XSS扫描器"""
    
    PAYLOADS = [
        '<script>alert("XSS")</script>',
        '<img src=x onerror=alert("XSS")>',
        '<svg onload=alert("XSS")>',
        '"><script>alert("XSS")</script>',
        "javascript:alert('XSS')",
        '<body onload=alert("XSS")>',
    ]
    
    def scan(self) -> List[Vulnerability]:
        """扫描XSS漏洞"""
        # 扫描输入点
        self._scan_inputs()
        
        return self.vulnerabilities
    
    def _scan_inputs(self):
        """扫描输入点"""
        # 扫描项目创建接口
        url = f"{self.base_url}/api/projects/"
        
        for payload in self.PAYLOADS:
            try:
                # 创建包含XSS payload的项目
                response = requests.post(url, json={
                    'name': payload,
                    'description': payload
                }, timeout=10)
                
                if response.status_code in [200, 201]:
                    # 获取创建的项目
                    project_id = response.json().get('data', {}).get('id')
                    if project_id:
                        # 检查payload是否被原样返回
                        get_response = requests.get(
                            f"{url}{project_id}/",
                            timeout=10
                        )
                        
                        if payload in get_response.text:
                            self.add_vulnerability(Vulnerability(
                                name='Stored XSS',
                                severity=Severity.HIGH,
                                description='存储型XSS漏洞，用户输入未经过滤直接存储和显示',
                                url=url,
                                payload=payload,
                                response=get_response.text[:500],
                                recommendation='对用户输入进行HTML转义，使用Content-Security-Policy'
                            ))
                            
            except Exception as e:
                pass

class SecurityTestFramework:
    """
    安全测试框架
    """
    def __init__(self, base_url: str):
        self.base_url = base_url
        self.scanners: List[SecurityScanner] = []
    
    def add_scanner(self, scanner: SecurityScanner):
        """添加扫描器"""
        self.scanners.append(scanner)
    
    def run_all_scans(self) -> Dict:
        """运行所有扫描"""
        all_vulnerabilities = []
        
        for scanner in self.scanners:
            vulnerabilities = scanner.scan()
            all_vulnerabilities.extend(vulnerabilities)
        
        # 汇总报告
        severity_count = {}
        for severity in Severity:
            severity_count[severity.value] = sum(
                1 for v in all_vulnerabilities if v.severity == severity
            )
        
        return {
            'total': len(all_vulnerabilities),
            'by_severity': severity_count,
            'vulnerabilities': [
                {
                    'name': v.name,
                    'severity': v.severity.value,
                    'description': v.description,
                    'url': v.url,
                    'recommendation': v.recommendation
                }
                for v in all_vulnerabilities
            ]
        }

# 使用示例
def run_security_test():
    """运行安全测试"""
    framework = SecurityTestFramework('http://localhost:8000')
    
    # 添加扫描器
    framework.add_scanner(SQLInjectionScanner('http://localhost:8000'))
    framework.add_scanner(XSSScanner('http://localhost:8000'))
    
    # 运行扫描
    report = framework.run_all_scans()
    
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
```

## 十、测试平台开发进阶

### 10.1 测试数据平台

**Q18: 如何设计一个测试数据管理平台？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析测试数据平台的需求：
- **数据生成**：支持多种数据生成策略
- **数据管理**：数据的增删改查
- **数据隔离**：不同环境的数据隔离
- **数据版本**：支持数据版本管理

其次，我会设计数据平台架构：

```python
# platform/data_platform.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import json
import uuid
from datetime import datetime

class DataType(Enum):
    """数据类型"""
    USER = "user"
    PROJECT = "project"
    TESTCASE = "testcase"
    ORDER = "order"

class DataStatus(Enum):
    """数据状态"""
    ACTIVE = "active"
    ARCHIVED = "archived"
    DELETED = "deleted"

@dataclass
class TestData:
    """测试数据"""
    id: str
    data_type: DataType
    data: Dict
    status: DataStatus
    environment: str
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime
    tags: List[str]
    version: int

class DataGenerator:
    """
    数据生成器
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.generators = {}
        self._register_default_generators()
    
    def _register_default_generators(self):
        """注册默认生成器"""
        self.register_generator(DataType.USER, self._generate_user)
        self.register_generator(DataType.PROJECT, self._generate_project)
        self.register_generator(DataType.TESTCASE, self._generate_testcase)
    
    def register_generator(self, data_type: DataType, generator_func):
        """注册生成器"""
        self.generators[data_type] = generator_func
    
    def generate(
        self,
        data_type: DataType,
        count: int = 1,
        **kwargs
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """生成数据"""
        generator = self.generators.get(data_type)
        if not generator:
            raise ValueError(f"No generator for {data_type}")
        
        return [generator(**kwargs) for _ in range(count)]
    
    def _generate_user(self, **kwargs) -> Dict:
        """生成用户数据"""
        from faker import Faker
        fake = Faker('zh_CN')
        
        return {
            'username': kwargs.get('username') or f'user_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}',
            'email': kwargs.get('email') or fake.email(),
            'phone': kwargs.get('phone') or fake.phone_number(),
            'password': kwargs.get('password') or 'Test@123456',
            'role': kwargs.get('role') or 'user',
        }
    
    def _generate_project(self, **kwargs) -> Dict:
        """生成项目数据"""
        from faker import Faker
        fake = Faker('zh_CN')
        
        return {
            'name': kwargs.get('name') or f'项目_{fake.company()}',
            'description': kwargs.get('description') or fake.text(),
            'status': kwargs.get('status') or 'active',
        }
    
    def _generate_testcase(self, **kwargs) -> Dict:
        """生成测试用例数据"""
        from faker import Faker
        fake = Faker('zh_CN')
        
        return {
            'title': kwargs.get('title') or f'测试用例_{fake.sentence()}',
            'priority': kwargs.get('priority') or 'P2',
            'status': kwargs.get('status') or 'draft',
            'steps': kwargs.get('steps') or [
                {'action': '步骤1', 'expected': '预期结果1'},
                {'action': '步骤2', 'expected': '预期结果2'},
            ]
        }

class DataPool:
    """
    数据池
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.data_store: Dict[str, TestData] = {}
        self.index_by_type: Dict[DataType, List[str]] = {}
        self.index_by_env: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
    
    def add(self, test_data: TestData) -> str:
        """添加数据"""
        self.data_store[test_data.id] = test_data
        
        # 更新索引
        if test_data.data_type not in self.index_by_type:
            self.index_by_type[test_data.data_type] = []
        self.index_by_type[test_data.data_type].append(test_data.id)
        
        if test_data.environment not in self.index_by_env:
            self.index_by_env[test_data.environment] = []
        self.index_by_env[test_data.environment].append(test_data.id)
        
        return test_data.id
    
    def get(self, data_id: str) -> Optional[TestData]:
        """获取数据"""
        return self.data_store.get(data_id)
    
    def query(
        self,
        data_type: Optional[DataType] = None,
        environment: Optional[str] = None,
        status: Optional[DataStatus] = None,
        tags: Optional[List[str]] = None
    ) -> List[TestData]:
        """查询数据"""
        results = list(self.data_store.values())
        
        if data_type:
            results = [d for d in results if d.data_type == data_type]
        
        if environment:
            results = [d for d in results if d.environment == environment]
        
        if status:
            results = [d for d in results if d.status == status]
        
        if tags:
            results = [d for d in results if any(tag in d.tags for tag in tags)]
        
        return results
    
    def delete(self, data_id: str) -> bool:
        """删除数据"""
        if data_id in self.data_store:
            test_data = self.data_store[data_id]
            test_data.status = DataStatus.DELETED
            return True
        return False
    
    def archive(self, data_id: str) -> bool:
        """归档数据"""
        if data_id in self.data_store:
            self.data_store[data_id].status = DataStatus.ARCHIVED
            return True
        return False

class DataVersionManager:
    """
    数据版本管理
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.versions: Dict[str, List[Dict]] = {}
    
    def create_version(
        self,
        data_id: str,
        data: Dict,
        description: str = ""
    ) -> str:
        """创建版本"""
        version_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        
        if data_id not in self.versions:
            self.versions[data_id] = []
        
        version = {
            'version_id': version_id,
            'data': data.copy(),
            'description': description,
            'created_at': datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        self.versions[data_id].append(version)
        return version_id
    
    def get_version(self, data_id: str, version_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """获取指定版本"""
        versions = self.versions.get(data_id, [])
        for version in versions:
            if version['version_id'] == version_id:
                return version
        return None
    
    def list_versions(self, data_id: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """列出所有版本"""
        return self.versions.get(data_id, [])
    
    def rollback(self, data_id: str, version_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """回滚到指定版本"""
        version = self.get_version(data_id, version_id)
        if version:
            return version['data'].copy()
        return None

class TestDataPlatform:
    """
    测试数据管理平台
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.generator = DataGenerator()
        self.pool = DataPool()
        self.version_manager = DataVersionManager()
    
    def generate_and_store(
        self,
        data_type: DataType,
        environment: str,
        count: int = 1,
        tags: List[str] = None,
        **kwargs
    ) -> List[str]:
        """生成并存储数据"""
        data_list = self.generator.generate(data_type, count, **kwargs)
        data_ids = []
        
        for data in data_list:
            test_data = TestData(
                id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                data_type=data_type,
                data=data,
                status=DataStatus.ACTIVE,
                environment=environment,
                created_at=datetime.now(),
                updated_at=datetime.now(),
                tags=tags or [],
                version=1
            )
            
            data_id = self.pool.add(test_data)
            self.version_manager.create_version(
                data_id,
                data,
                "Initial version"
            )
            
            data_ids.append(data_id)
        
        return data_ids
    
    def get_data(self, data_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """获取数据"""
        test_data = self.pool.get(data_id)
        return test_data.data if test_data else None
    
    def update_data(
        self,
        data_id: str,
        new_data: Dict,
        description: str = ""
    ) -> bool:
        """更新数据"""
        test_data = self.pool.get(data_id)
        if not test_data:
            return False
        
        # 创建新版本
        test_data.data = new_data
        test_data.updated_at = datetime.now()
        test_data.version += 1
        
        self.version_manager.create_version(data_id, new_data, description)
        
        return True
    
    def query_data(
        self,
        data_type: Optional[DataType] = None,
        environment: Optional[str] = None,
        status: Optional[DataStatus] = None,
        tags: Optional[List[str]] = None
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """查询数据"""
        test_data_list = self.pool.query(
            data_type=data_type,
            environment=environment,
            status=status,
            tags=tags
        )
        return [td.data for td in test_data_list]
    
    def rollback_data(self, data_id: str, version_id: str) -> bool:
        """回滚数据"""
        old_data = self.version_manager.rollback(data_id, version_id)
        if old_data:
            return self.update_data(
                data_id,
                old_data,
                f"Rollback to version {version_id}"
            )
        return False

# 使用示例
platform = TestDataPlatform()

# 生成并存储用户数据
user_ids = platform.generate_and_store(
    data_type=DataType.USER,
    environment='test',
    count=5,
    tags=['smoke', 'regression']
)

# 查询数据
users = platform.query_data(
    data_type=DataType.USER,
    environment='test'
)

# 更新数据
platform.update_data(
    user_ids[0],
    {'username': 'updated_user', 'email': 'updated@test.com'},
    "Update user info"
)

# 回滚数据
versions = platform.version_manager.list_versions(user_ids[0])
platform.rollback_data(user_ids[0], versions[0]['version_id'])
```

### 10.2 测试环境管理

**Q19: 如何设计一个测试环境管理平台？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会设计环境管理平台：

```python
# platform/environment_platform.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import subprocess
import docker
import time
from datetime import datetime

class EnvironmentStatus(Enum):
    """环境状态"""
    CREATING = "creating"
    RUNNING = "running"
    STOPPED = "stopped"
    ERROR = "error"
    DESTROYED = "destroyed"

class EnvironmentType(Enum):
    """环境类型"""
    DEVELOPMENT = "development"
    TESTING = "testing"
    STAGING = "staging"
    PERFORMANCE = "performance"

@dataclass
class Environment:
    """环境"""
    id: str
    name: str
    env_type: EnvironmentType
    status: EnvironmentStatus
    config: Dict
    services: Dict[str, str]
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime
    owner: str
    locked: bool = False
    locked_by: Optional[str] = None

class DockerManager:
    """
    Docker管理器
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.client = docker.from_env()
    
    def create_container(
        self,
        image: str,
        name: str,
        ports: Dict[str, int],
        environment: Dict[str, str] = None,
        volumes: Dict[str, str] = None
    ) -> str:
        """创建容器"""
        container = self.client.containers.run(
            image,
            name=name,
            ports=ports,
            environment=environment or {},
            volumes=volumes or {},
            detach=True
        )
        return container.id
    
    def stop_container(self, container_id: str):
        """停止容器"""
        container = self.client.containers.get(container_id)
        container.stop()
    
    def start_container(self, container_id: str):
        """启动容器"""
        container = self.client.containers.get(container_id)
        container.start()
    
    def remove_container(self, container_id: str):
        """删除容器"""
        container = self.client.containers.get(container_id)
        container.remove(force=True)
    
    def get_container_status(self, container_id: str) -> str:
        """获取容器状态"""
        container = self.client.containers.get(container_id)
        return container.status
    
    def get_container_logs(self, container_id: str) -> str:
        """获取容器日志"""
        container = self.client.containers.get(container_id)
        return container.logs().decode('utf-8')

class ServiceManager:
    """
    服务管理器
    """
    
    def __init__(self, docker_manager: DockerManager):
        self.docker = docker_manager
    
    def start_database(self, name: str, port: int = 5432) -> str:
        """启动数据库服务"""
        return self.docker.create_container(
            image='postgres:14',
            name=name,
            ports={'5432/tcp': port},
            environment={
                'POSTGRES_USER': 'test',
                'POSTGRES_PASSWORD': 'test',
                'POSTGRES_DB': 'testdb'
            }
        )
    
    def start_redis(self, name: str, port: int = 6379) -> str:
        """启动Redis服务"""
        return self.docker.create_container(
            image='redis:7-alpine',
            name=name,
            ports={'6379/tcp': port}
        )
    
    def start_backend(
        self,
        name: str,
        port: int,
        db_host: str,
        redis_host: str
    ) -> str:
        """启动后端服务"""
        return self.docker.create_container(
            image='myapp-backend:latest',
            name=name,
            ports={'8000/tcp': port},
            environment={
                'DATABASE_URL': f'postgres://test:test@{db_host}:5432/testdb',
                'REDIS_URL': f'redis://{redis_host}:6379',
                'DEBUG': 'True'
            }
        )
    
    def start_frontend(self, name: str, port: int, backend_url: str) -> str:
        """启动前端服务"""
        return self.docker.create_container(
            image='myapp-frontend:latest',
            name=name,
            ports={'3000/tcp': port},
            environment={
                'VITE_API_BASE_URL': backend_url
            }
        )

class EnvironmentManager:
    """
    环境管理器
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.environments: Dict[str, Environment] = {}
        self.docker = DockerManager()
        self.service_manager = ServiceManager(self.docker)
    
    def create_environment(
        self,
        name: str,
        env_type: EnvironmentType,
        config: Dict,
        owner: str
    ) -> str:
        """创建环境"""
        import uuid
        
        env_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        
        environment = Environment(
            id=env_id,
            name=name,
            env_type=env_type,
            status=EnvironmentStatus.CREATING,
            config=config,
            services={},
            created_at=datetime.now(),
            updated_at=datetime.now(),
            owner=owner
        )
        
        self.environments[env_id] = environment
        
        try:
            # 启动服务
            self._start_services(environment)
            environment.status = EnvironmentStatus.RUNNING
        except Exception as e:
            environment.status = EnvironmentStatus.ERROR
            raise
        
        return env_id
    
    def _start_services(self, environment: Environment):
        """启动服务"""
        config = environment.config
        
        # 启动数据库
        db_container = self.service_manager.start_database(
            f"{environment.name}-db",
            config.get('db_port', 5432)
        )
        environment.services['database'] = db_container
        
        # 启动Redis
        redis_container = self.service_manager.start_redis(
            f"{environment.name}-redis",
            config.get('redis_port', 6379)
        )
        environment.services['redis'] = redis_container
        
        # 等待服务启动
        time.sleep(5)
        
        # 启动后端
        backend_container = self.service_manager.start_backend(
            f"{environment.name}-backend",
            config.get('backend_port', 8000),
            'localhost',
            'localhost'
        )
        environment.services['backend'] = backend_container
        
        # 启动前端
        frontend_container = self.service_manager.start_frontend(
            f"{environment.name}-frontend",
            config.get('frontend_port', 3000),
            f"http://localhost:{config.get('backend_port', 8000)}"
        )
        environment.services['frontend'] = frontend_container
    
    def stop_environment(self, env_id: str):
        """停止环境"""
        environment = self.environments.get(env_id)
        if not environment:
            return
        
        for service_name, container_id in environment.services.items():
            try:
                self.docker.stop_container(container_id)
            except:
                pass
        
        environment.status = EnvironmentStatus.STOPPED
        environment.updated_at = datetime.now()
    
    def start_environment(self, env_id: str):
        """启动环境"""
        environment = self.environments.get(env_id)
        if not environment:
            return
        
        for service_name, container_id in environment.services.items():
            try:
                self.docker.start_container(container_id)
            except:
                pass
        
        environment.status = EnvironmentStatus.RUNNING
        environment.updated_at = datetime.now()
    
    def destroy_environment(self, env_id: str):
        """销毁环境"""
        environment = self.environments.get(env_id)
        if not environment:
            return
        
        for service_name, container_id in environment.services.items():
            try:
                self.docker.remove_container(container_id)
            except:
                pass
        
        environment.status = EnvironmentStatus.DESTROYED
        environment.updated_at = datetime.now()
    
    def lock_environment(self, env_id: str, user: str):
        """锁定环境"""
        environment = self.environments.get(env_id)
        if environment:
            environment.locked = True
            environment.locked_by = user
            environment.updated_at = datetime.now()
    
    def unlock_environment(self, env_id: str):
        """解锁环境"""
        environment = self.environments.get(env_id)
        if environment:
            environment.locked = False
            environment.locked_by = None
            environment.updated_at = datetime.now()
    
    def get_environment_status(self, env_id: str) -> Dict:
        """获取环境状态"""
        environment = self.environments.get(env_id)
        if not environment:
            return {}
        
        service_status = {}
        for service_name, container_id in environment.services.items():
            try:
                status = self.docker.get_container_status(container_id)
                service_status[service_name] = status
            except:
                service_status[service_name] = 'unknown'
        
        return {
            'id': environment.id,
            'name': environment.name,
            'status': environment.status.value,
            'services': service_status,
            'locked': environment.locked,
            'locked_by': environment.locked_by,
            'created_at': environment.created_at.isoformat(),
            'updated_at': environment.updated_at.isoformat()
        }
    
    def list_environments(
        self,
        env_type: Optional[EnvironmentType] = None,
        status: Optional[EnvironmentStatus] = None
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """列出环境"""
        environments = list(self.environments.values())
        
        if env_type:
            environments = [e for e in environments if e.env_type == env_type]
        
        if status:
            environments = [e for e in environments if e.status == status]
        
        return [
            {
                'id': e.id,
                'name': e.name,
                'type': e.env_type.value,
                'status': e.status.value,
                'locked': e.locked,
                'owner': e.owner
            }
            for e in environments
        ]

# 使用示例
manager = EnvironmentManager()

# 创建测试环境
env_id = manager.create_environment(
    name='test-env-001',
    env_type=EnvironmentType.TESTING,
    config={
        'db_port': 5433,
        'redis_port': 6380,
        'backend_port': 8001,
        'frontend_port': 3001
    },
    owner='test_user'
)

# 查看环境状态
status = manager.get_environment_status(env_id)
print(json.dumps(status, indent=2))

# 锁定环境
manager.lock_environment(env_id, 'test_user')

# 停止环境
manager.stop_environment(env_id)

# 销毁环境
manager.destroy_environment(env_id)
```

## 十一、测试效能度量

### 11.1 测试度量指标

**Q20: 如何设计测试效能度量体系？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会定义测试度量指标：

```python
# metrics/test_metrics.py
from typing import Dict, List
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import statistics

@dataclass
class TestMetrics:
    """测试度量指标"""
    # 测试覆盖率
    code_coverage: float = 0.0
    api_coverage: float = 0.0
    ui_coverage: float = 0.0
    
    # 测试效率
    automation_rate: float = 0.0
    test_execution_time: float = 0.0
    test_case_count: int = 0
    
    # 测试质量
    defect_detection_rate: float = 0.0
    test_pass_rate: float = 0.0
    false_positive_rate: float = 0.0
    
    # 测试稳定性
    test_stability: float = 0.0
    flaky_test_rate: float = 0.0

class MetricsCollector:
    """
    指标收集器
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.test_results: List[Dict] = []
        self.defects: List[Dict] = []
        self.coverage_data: Dict = {}
    
    def collect_test_result(self, result: Dict):
        """收集测试结果"""
        self.test_results.append({
            'test_name': result['test_name'],
            'status': result['status'],
            'duration': result['duration'],
            'timestamp': datetime.now(),
            'error': result.get('error')
        })
    
    def collect_defect(self, defect: Dict):
        """收集缺陷"""
        self.defects.append({
            'defect_id': defect['defect_id'],
            'severity': defect['severity'],
            'found_by': defect['found_by'],
            'found_at': datetime.now(),
            'status': defect['status']
        })
    
    def collect_coverage(self, coverage_type: str, data: Dict):
        """收集覆盖率数据"""
        self.coverage_data[coverage_type] = data

class MetricsCalculator:
    """
    指标计算器
    """
    
    def __init__(self, collector: MetricsCollector):
        self.collector = collector
    
    def calculate_coverage_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        """计算覆盖率指标"""
        coverage = {}
        
        # 代码覆盖率
        if 'code' in self.collector.coverage_data:
            code_data = self.collector.coverage_data['code']
            coverage['code_coverage'] = code_data.get('line_coverage', 0)
        
        # API覆盖率
        if 'api' in self.collector.coverage_data:
            api_data = self.collector.coverage_data['api']
            total_apis = api_data.get('total', 0)
            tested_apis = api_data.get('tested', 0)
            coverage['api_coverage'] = (
                tested_apis / total_apis * 100 if total_apis > 0 else 0
            )
        
        # UI覆盖率
        if 'ui' in self.collector.coverage_data:
            ui_data = self.collector.coverage_data['ui']
            total_pages = ui_data.get('total_pages', 0)
            tested_pages = ui_data.get('tested_pages', 0)
            coverage['ui_coverage'] = (
                tested_pages / total_pages * 100 if total_pages > 0 else 0
            )
        
        return coverage
    
    def calculate_efficiency_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        """计算效率指标"""
        results = self.collector.test_results
        
        if not results:
            return {
                'automation_rate': 0,
                'avg_execution_time': 0,
                'test_count': 0
            }
        
        # 自动化率（假设所有收集的都是自动化测试）
        automation_rate = 100.0
        
        # 平均执行时间
        durations = [r['duration'] for r in results]
        avg_execution_time = statistics.mean(durations)
        
        return {
            'automation_rate': automation_rate,
            'avg_execution_time': avg_execution_time,
            'test_count': len(results)
        }
    
    def calculate_quality_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        """计算质量指标"""
        results = self.collector.test_results
        defects = self.collector.defects
        
        # 测试通过率
        passed = sum(1 for r in results if r['status'] == 'passed')
        pass_rate = passed / len(results) * 100 if results else 0
        
        # 缺陷检测率（测试发现的缺陷 / 总缺陷）
        test_found_defects = sum(
            1 for d in defects if d['found_by'] == 'test'
        )
        total_defects = len(defects)
        defect_detection_rate = (
            test_found_defects / total_defects * 100 if total_defects > 0 else 0
        )
        
        # 误报率（测试失败但不是真实缺陷）
        failed_tests = [r for r in results if r['status'] == 'failed']
        false_positives = sum(
            1 for f in failed_tests 
            if f.get('error') and 'flaky' in str(f.get('error')).lower()
        )
        false_positive_rate = (
            false_positives / len(failed_tests) * 100 if failed_tests else 0
        )
        
        return {
            'pass_rate': pass_rate,
            'defect_detection_rate': defect_detection_rate,
            'false_positive_rate': false_positive_rate
        }
    
    def calculate_stability_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        """计算稳定性指标"""
        results = self.collector.test_results
        
        if not results:
            return {
                'stability': 0,
                'flaky_rate': 0
            }
        
        # 按测试名称分组
        test_groups = {}
        for r in results:
            test_name = r['test_name']
            if test_name not in test_groups:
                test_groups[test_name] = []
            test_groups[test_name].append(r['status'])
        
        # 计算不稳定测试
        flaky_count = 0
        for test_name, statuses in test_groups.items():
            if len(set(statuses)) > 1:  # 状态不一致
                flaky_count += 1
        
        flaky_rate = flaky_count / len(test_groups) * 100 if test_groups else 0
        stability = 100 - flaky_rate
        
        return {
            'stability': stability,
            'flaky_rate': flaky_rate
        }
    
    def generate_metrics_report(self) -> Dict:
        """生成度量报告"""
        return {
            'coverage': self.calculate_coverage_metrics(),
            'efficiency': self.calculate_efficiency_metrics(),
            'quality': self.calculate_quality_metrics(),
            'stability': self.calculate_stability_metrics(),
            'generated_at': datetime.now().isoformat()
        }

class MetricsDashboard:
    """
    指标仪表板
    """
    
    def __init__(self, calculator: MetricsCalculator):
        self.calculator = calculator
        self.history: List[Dict] = []
    
    def update(self):
        """更新仪表板"""
        report = self.calculator.generate_metrics_report()
        self.history.append(report)
        return report
    
    def get_trend(self, days: int = 7) -> Dict:
        """获取趋势"""
        if not self.history:
            return {}
        
        # 获取最近N天的数据
        recent = self.history[-days:] if len(self.history) >= days else self.history
        
        return {
            'pass_rate_trend': [
                r['quality']['pass_rate'] for r in recent
            ],
            'coverage_trend': [
                r['coverage'].get('code_coverage', 0) for r in recent
            ],
            'stability_trend': [
                r['stability']['stability'] for r in recent
            ]
        }
    
    def get_summary(self) -> Dict:
        """获取摘要"""
        if not self.history:
            return {}
        
        latest = self.history[-1]
        
        return {
            'code_coverage': f"{latest['coverage'].get('code_coverage', 0):.1f}%",
            'pass_rate': f"{latest['quality']['pass_rate']:.1f}%",
            'stability': f"{latest['stability']['stability']:.1f}%",
            'test_count': latest['efficiency']['test_count'],
            'avg_execution_time': f"{latest['efficiency']['avg_execution_time']:.2f}s"
        }
```

## 十二、测试左移实践

### 12.1 测试左移策略

**Q21: 如何在项目中实施测试左移？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会解释测试左移的概念：

测试左移是指在软件开发生命周期的早期阶段就引入测试活动，尽早发现和修复缺陷，降低修复成本。

```
传统模式：
需求 → 设计 → 开发 → 测试 → 发布
                    ↑ 测试介入晚

测试左移：
需求 → 设计 → 开发 → 测试 → 发布
  ↑      ↑      ↑      ↑
测试参与全流程
```

其次，我会实施测试左移策略：

```python
# shift_left/quality_gate.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum

class GateStatus(Enum):
    """门禁状态"""
    PASSED = "passed"
    FAILED = "failed"
    WARNING = "warning"

@dataclass
class QualityGate:
    """质量门禁"""
    name: str
    condition: str
    threshold: float
    actual_value: float
    status: GateStatus

class QualityGateChecker:
    """
    质量门禁检查器
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.gates: List[QualityGate] = []
    
    def add_gate(
        self,
        name: str,
        condition: str,
        threshold: float,
        actual_value: float
    ):
        """添加门禁"""
        if condition == '>=':
            status = GateStatus.PASSED if actual_value >= threshold else GateStatus.FAILED
        elif condition == '<=':
            status = GateStatus.PASSED if actual_value <= threshold else GateStatus.FAILED
        else:
            status = GateStatus.WARNING
        
        self.gates.append(QualityGate(
            name=name,
            condition=condition,
            threshold=threshold,
            actual_value=actual_value,
            status=status
        ))
    
    def check_all(self) -> Dict:
        """检查所有门禁"""
        passed = sum(1 for g in self.gates if g.status == GateStatus.PASSED)
        failed = sum(1 for g in self.gates if g.status == GateStatus.FAILED)
        
        return {
            'overall_status': GateStatus.PASSED.value if failed == 0 else GateStatus.FAILED.value,
            'passed_count': passed,
            'failed_count': failed,
            'total_count': len(self.gates),
            'gates': [
                {
                    'name': g.name,
                    'condition': g.condition,
                    'threshold': g.threshold,
                    'actual_value': g.actual_value,
                    'status': g.status.value
                }
                for g in self.gates
            ]
        }

class RequirementReview:
    """
    需求评审
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.review_items: List[Dict] = []
    
    def add_review_item(
        self,
        requirement_id: str,
        requirement_text: str,
        testability: str,
        questions: List[str]
    ):
        """添加评审项"""
        self.review_items.append({
            'requirement_id': requirement_id,
            'requirement_text': requirement_text,
            'testability': testability,
            'questions': questions,
            'status': 'pending'
        })
    
    def evaluate_testability(self, requirement_text: str) -> str:
        """评估可测试性"""
        # 简单的可测试性评估规则
        if any(word in requirement_text.lower() for word in ['应该', '必须', '需要']):
            return 'high'
        elif any(word in requirement_text.lower() for word in ['可能', '也许']):
            return 'low'
        else:
            return 'medium'
    
    def generate_test_scenarios(self, requirement_text: str) -> List[str]:
        """生成测试场景"""
        scenarios = []
        
        # 基于需求生成测试场景
        scenarios.append(f"正向场景：验证{requirement_text}正常工作")
        scenarios.append(f"异常场景：验证{requirement_text}异常处理")
        scenarios.append(f"边界场景：验证{requirement_text}边界条件")
        
        return scenarios

class DesignReview:
    """
    设计评审
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.design_issues: List[Dict] = []
    
    def review_api_design(self, api_spec: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """评审API设计"""
        issues = []
        
        # 检查是否有认证
        if not api_spec.get('authentication'):
            issues.append({
                'type': 'security',
                'severity': 'high',
                'message': 'API缺少认证机制'
            })
        
        # 检查是否有错误处理
        if 'error_responses' not in api_spec:
            issues.append({
                'type': 'reliability',
                'severity': 'medium',
                'message': 'API缺少错误响应定义'
            })
        
        # 检查是否有输入验证
        if 'input_validation' not in api_spec:
            issues.append({
                'type': 'security',
                'severity': 'high',
                'message': 'API缺少输入验证'
            })
        
        self.design_issues.extend(issues)
        return issues
    
    def review_database_design(self, db_schema: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """评审数据库设计"""
        issues = []
        
        # 检查是否有索引
        for table_name, table_def in db_schema.items():
            if 'indexes' not in table_def or not table_def['indexes']:
                issues.append({
                    'type': 'performance',
                    'severity': 'medium',
                    'message': f'表{table_name}缺少索引定义'
                })
        
        self.design_issues.extend(issues)
        return issues

class CodeReviewAssistant:
    """
    代码评审助手
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.review_comments: List[Dict] = []
    
    def analyze_code(self, code: str, file_path: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """分析代码"""
        comments = []
        
        # 检查是否有单元测试
        if 'def test_' not in code and 'test/' not in file_path:
            comments.append({
                'type': 'testing',
                'severity': 'medium',
                'line': None,
                'message': '代码缺少对应的单元测试'
            })
        
        # 检查是否有异常处理
        if 'try:' not in code and 'except' not in code:
            comments.append({
                'type': 'reliability',
                'severity': 'low',
                'line': None,
                'message': '建议添加异常处理'
            })
        
        # 检查是否有日志
        if 'logging' not in code and 'logger' not in code:
            comments.append({
                'type': 'observability',
                'severity': 'low',
                'line': None,
                'message': '建议添加日志记录'
            })
        
        self.review_comments.extend(comments)
        return comments
    
    def check_test_coverage(self, file_path: str, coverage: float) -> Dict:
        """检查测试覆盖率"""
        threshold = 80.0
        
        return {
            'file': file_path,
            'coverage': coverage,
            'threshold': threshold,
            'passed': coverage >= threshold,
            'message': f"覆盖率{coverage:.1f}%，{'达到' if coverage >= threshold else '未达到'}阈值{threshold}%"
        }

# 使用示例
# 需求评审
review = RequirementReview()
review.add_review_item(
    requirement_id='REQ-001',
    requirement_text='用户登录功能应该验证用户名和密码',
    testability='high',
    questions=['密码强度要求是什么？', '登录失败次数限制？']
)

# 设计评审
design_review = DesignReview()
api_issues = design_review.review_api_design({
    'path': '/api/login',
    'method': 'POST',
    'authentication': False  # 缺少认证
})

# 代码评审
code_review = CodeReviewAssistant()
comments = code_review.analyze_code(
    'def login(username, password):\n    return authenticate(username, password)',
    'auth.py'
)

# 质量门禁
checker = QualityGateChecker()
checker.add_gate('代码覆盖率', '>=', 80.0, 75.0)
checker.add_gate('单元测试通过率', '>=', 100.0, 95.0)
checker.add_gate('代码复杂度', '<=', 10.0, 8.0)

result = checker.check_all()
print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
```
