### 16.2 智能测试推荐

**Q29: 如何实现测试用例的智能推荐？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析智能推荐的需求：
- **代码变更分析**：分析代码变更影响范围
- **历史数据分析**：基于历史执行数据推荐
- **风险预测**：预测高风险测试用例
- **优先级排序**：智能排序测试用例

其次，我会设计智能推荐系统：

```python
# platform/smart_recommendation.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import json
from collections import defaultdict
import re

@dataclass
class CodeChange:
    """代码变更"""
    file_path: str
    change_type: str  # add, modify, delete
    lines_added: int
    lines_deleted: int
    author: str
    timestamp: datetime

@dataclass
class TestCaseInfo:
    """测试用例信息"""
    id: str
    name: str
    file_path: str
    covered_files: Set[str]
    last_run: Optional[datetime] = None
    last_result: Optional[str] = None
    failure_count: int = 0
    total_runs: int = 0
    avg_duration: float = 0

@dataclass
class Recommendation:
    """推荐结果"""
    test_id: str
    test_name: str
    priority: float
    reasons: List[str]
    risk_level: str

class CodeChangeAnalyzer:
    """代码变更分析器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.file_dependencies: Dict[str, Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    
    def analyze_changes(
        self,
        changes: List[CodeChange]
    ) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
        """分析代码变更"""
        impact_map = {}
        
        for change in changes:
            affected_files = self._get_affected_files(change.file_path)
            impact_map[change.file_path] = list(affected_files)
        
        return impact_map
    
    def _get_affected_files(self, file_path: str) -> Set[str]:
        """获取受影响的文件"""
        affected = {file_path}
        
        for dep_file, dependencies in self.file_dependencies.items():
            if file_path in dependencies:
                affected.add(dep_file)
        
        return affected
    
    def build_dependency_graph(self, project_path: str):
        """构建依赖图"""
        import os
        
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(project_path):
            for file in files:
                if file.endswith('.py'):
                    file_path = os.path.join(root, file)
                    self._analyze_imports(file_path)
    
    def _analyze_imports(self, file_path: str):
        """分析导入关系"""
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                content = f.read()
            
            imports = re.findall(
                r'^(?:from|import)\s+([^\s]+)',
                content,
                re.MULTILINE
            )
            
            for imp in imports:
                self.file_dependencies[file_path].add(imp)
        except:
            pass

class HistoryAnalyzer:
    """历史数据分析器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.test_history: Dict[str, List[Dict]] = defaultdict(list)
    
    def record_execution(
        self,
        test_id: str,
        result: str,
        duration: float,
        timestamp: datetime = None
    ):
        """记录执行历史"""
        self.test_history[test_id].append({
            'result': result,
            'duration': duration,
            'timestamp': timestamp or datetime.now()
        })
    
    def get_failure_rate(self, test_id: str) -> float:
        """获取失败率"""
        history = self.test_history.get(test_id, [])
        if not history:
            return 0.0
        
        failures = sum(1 for h in history if h['result'] == 'failed')
        return failures / len(history)
    
    def get_recent_failures(
        self,
        test_id: str,
        days: int = 7
    ) -> int:
        """获取最近失败次数"""
        history = self.test_history.get(test_id, [])
        cutoff = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=days)
        
        return sum(
            1 for h in history
            if h['result'] == 'failed' and h['timestamp'] > cutoff
        )
    
    def get_flakiness_score(self, test_id: str) -> float:
        """获取不稳定分数"""
        history = self.test_history.get(test_id, [])
        if len(history) < 3:
            return 0.0
        
        results = [h['result'] for h in history[-10:]]
        
        changes = sum(
            1 for i in range(1, len(results))
            if results[i] != results[i-1]
        )
        
        return changes / (len(results) - 1) if len(results) > 1 else 0
    
    def get_avg_duration(self, test_id: str) -> float:
        """获取平均执行时间"""
        history = self.test_history.get(test_id, [])
        if not history:
            return 0.0
        
        durations = [h['duration'] for h in history]
        return sum(durations) / len(durations)

class RiskPredictor:
    """风险预测器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.risk_factors = {
            'high_failure_rate': 0.3,
            'recent_failure': 0.25,
            'code_change_impact': 0.25,
            'flakiness': 0.2
        }
    
    def predict_risk(
        self,
        test_info: TestCaseInfo,
        history_analyzer: HistoryAnalyzer,
        change_impact: float
    ) -> Tuple[float, str]:
        """预测风险"""
        failure_rate = history_analyzer.get_failure_rate(test_info.id)
        recent_failures = history_analyzer.get_recent_failures(test_info.id)
        flakiness = history_analyzer.get_flakiness_score(test_info.id)
        
        risk_score = (
            failure_rate * self.risk_factors['high_failure_rate'] +
            min(recent_failures / 5, 1.0) * self.risk_factors['recent_failure'] +
            change_impact * self.risk_factors['code_change_impact'] +
            flakiness * self.risk_factors['flakiness']
        )
        
        if risk_score >= 0.7:
            risk_level = 'high'
        elif risk_score >= 0.4:
            risk_level = 'medium'
        else:
            risk_level = 'low'
        
        return risk_score, risk_level

class SmartRecommender:
    """智能推荐器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.code_analyzer = CodeChangeAnalyzer()
        self.history_analyzer = HistoryAnalyzer()
        self.risk_predictor = RiskPredictor()
        self.test_cases: Dict[str, TestCaseInfo] = {}
    
    def register_test_case(self, test_info: TestCaseInfo):
        """注册测试用例"""
        self.test_cases[test_info.id] = test_info
    
    def recommend(
        self,
        code_changes: List[CodeChange] = None,
        time_limit: int = None,
        max_tests: int = None
    ) -> List[Recommendation]:
        """智能推荐测试用例"""
        recommendations = []
        
        change_impact = {}
        if code_changes:
            change_impact = self.code_analyzer.analyze_changes(code_changes)
        
        for test_id, test_info in self.test_cases.items():
            priority = 0.0
            reasons = []
            
            impact_score = self._calculate_impact_score(
                test_info,
                change_impact
            )
            if impact_score > 0:
                priority += impact_score * 0.4
                reasons.append(f"代码变更影响({impact_score:.2f})")
            
            risk_score, risk_level = self.risk_predictor.predict_risk(
                test_info,
                self.history_analyzer,
                impact_score
            )
            if risk_score > 0:
                priority += risk_score * 0.3
                reasons.append(f"风险评分({risk_score:.2f})")
            
            if test_info.last_run:
                days_since_run = (datetime.now() - test_info.last_run).days
                if days_since_run > 7:
                    priority += 0.2
                    reasons.append(f"未执行{days_since_run}天")
            
            flakiness = self.history_analyzer.get_flakiness_score(test_id)
            if flakiness > 0.3:
                priority += flakiness * 0.1
                reasons.append(f"不稳定测试({flakiness:.2f})")
            
            recommendations.append(Recommendation(
                test_id=test_id,
                test_name=test_info.name,
                priority=priority,
                reasons=reasons,
                risk_level=risk_level
            ))
        
        recommendations.sort(key=lambda r: r.priority, reverse=True)
        
        if time_limit:
            recommendations = self._filter_by_time(
                recommendations,
                time_limit
            )
        
        if max_tests:
            recommendations = recommendations[:max_tests]
        
        return recommendations
    
    def _calculate_impact_score(
        self,
        test_info: TestCaseInfo,
        change_impact: Dict[str, List[str]]
    ) -> float:
        """计算影响分数"""
        if not change_impact:
            return 0.0
        
        affected_files = set()
        for files in change_impact.values():
            affected_files.update(files)
        
        overlap = len(test_info.covered_files & affected_files)
        total = len(affected_files)
        
        return overlap / total if total > 0 else 0.0
    
    def _filter_by_time(
        self,
        recommendations: List[Recommendation],
        time_limit: int
    ) -> List[Recommendation]:
        """按时间过滤"""
        filtered = []
        total_time = 0
        
        for rec in recommendations:
            test_info = self.test_cases[rec.test_id]
            duration = self.history_analyzer.get_avg_duration(rec.test_id)
            
            if total_time + duration <= time_limit:
                filtered.append(rec)
                total_time += duration
        
        return filtered

# 使用示例
recommender = SmartRecommender()

# 注册测试用例
recommender.register_test_case(TestCaseInfo(
    id='TC001',
    name='登录测试',
    file_path='tests/test_login.py',
    covered_files={'auth/login.py', 'auth/utils.py'}
))

recommender.register_test_case(TestCaseInfo(
    id='TC002',
    name='订单测试',
    file_path='tests/test_order.py',
    covered_files={'order/models.py', 'order/views.py'}
))

# 记录执行历史
recommender.history_analyzer.record_execution('TC001', 'passed', 1.5)
recommender.history_analyzer.record_execution('TC001', 'failed', 2.0)
recommender.history_analyzer.record_execution('TC002', 'passed', 3.0)

# 代码变更
changes = [
    CodeChange(
        file_path='auth/login.py',
        change_type='modify',
        lines_added=10,
        lines_deleted=5,
        author='developer',
        timestamp=datetime.now()
    )
]

# 获取推荐
recommendations = recommender.recommend(
    code_changes=changes,
    time_limit=300,
    max_tests=10
)

for rec in recommendations:
    print(f"推荐: {rec.test_name}, 优先级: {rec.priority:.2f}, 原因: {rec.reasons}")
```

## 十七、AI在测试中的应用

### 17.1 AI辅助测试

**Q30: 如何利用AI技术提升测试效率？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析AI在测试中的应用场景：
- **测试用例生成**：基于需求自动生成测试用例
- **缺陷预测**：预测高风险代码区域
- **测试数据生成**：智能生成测试数据
- **自动化维护**：自动修复失败的测试用例

其次，我会设计AI辅助测试系统：

```python
# platform/ai_testing.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
import json
import re
from collections import defaultdict

@dataclass
class Requirement:
    """需求"""
    id: str
    title: str
    description: str
    acceptance_criteria: List[str]
    priority: str

@dataclass
class GeneratedTestCase:
    """生成的测试用例"""
    id: str
    title: str
    preconditions: List[str]
    steps: List[Dict]
    expected_results: List[str]
    priority: str
    tags: List[str]
    confidence: float

class TestCaseGenerator:
    """测试用例生成器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.templates = self._load_templates()
    
    def _load_templates(self) -> Dict:
        """加载模板"""
        return {
            'login': {
                'positive': [
                    {
                        'title': '使用有效凭据登录成功',
                        'steps': [
                            {'action': '打开登录页面', 'expected': '显示登录表单'},
                            {'action': '输入有效的用户名', 'expected': '用户名显示正确'},
                            {'action': '输入有效的密码', 'expected': '密码显示为掩码'},
                            {'action': '点击登录按钮', 'expected': '登录成功，跳转到首页'}
                        ]
                    }
                ],
                'negative': [
                    {
                        'title': '使用无效凭据登录失败',
                        'steps': [
                            {'action': '打开登录页面', 'expected': '显示登录表单'},
                            {'action': '输入无效的用户名', 'expected': '用户名显示正确'},
                            {'action': '输入任意密码', 'expected': '密码显示为掩码'},
                            {'action': '点击登录按钮', 'expected': '显示错误提示'}
                        ]
                    }
                ]
            },
            'crud': {
                'create': [
                    {
                        'title': '创建新记录成功',
                        'steps': [
                            {'action': '打开创建页面', 'expected': '显示创建表单'},
                            {'action': '填写所有必填字段', 'expected': '字段值显示正确'},
                            {'action': '点击保存按钮', 'expected': '创建成功，显示成功提示'}
                        ]
                    }
                ],
                'read': [
                    {
                        'title': '查看记录详情',
                        'steps': [
                            {'action': '打开列表页面', 'expected': '显示记录列表'},
                            {'action': '点击查看按钮', 'expected': '显示记录详情'}
                        ]
                    }
                ],
                'update': [
                    {
                        'title': '更新记录成功',
                        'steps': [
                            {'action': '打开编辑页面', 'expected': '显示编辑表单'},
                            {'action': '修改字段值', 'expected': '字段值更新正确'},
                            {'action': '点击保存按钮', 'expected': '更新成功，显示成功提示'}
                        ]
                    }
                ],
                'delete': [
                    {
                        'title': '删除记录成功',
                        'steps': [
                            {'action': '选择要删除的记录', 'expected': '记录被选中'},
                            {'action': '点击删除按钮', 'expected': '显示确认对话框'},
                            {'action': '确认删除', 'expected': '删除成功，记录从列表移除'}
                        ]
                    }
                ]
            }
        }
    
    def generate_from_requirement(
        self,
        requirement: Requirement
    ) -> List[GeneratedTestCase]:
        """从需求生成测试用例"""
        test_cases = []
        
        keywords = self._extract_keywords(requirement.description)
        
        for criteria in requirement.acceptance_criteria:
            test_case = self._generate_from_criteria(
                criteria,
                keywords,
                requirement.priority
            )
            if test_case:
                test_cases.append(test_case)
        
        test_cases.extend(self._generate_edge_cases(keywords, requirement.priority))
        
        test_cases.extend(self._generate_negative_cases(keywords, requirement.priority))
        
        return test_cases
    
    def _extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> List[str]:
        """提取关键词"""
        keywords = []
        
        patterns = {
            'login': r'登录|认证|验证',
            'crud': r'创建|读取|更新|删除|增删改查',
            'search': r'搜索|查询|过滤',
            'export': r'导出|下载',
            'import': r'导入|上传'
        }
        
        for keyword, pattern in patterns.items():
            if re.search(pattern, text):
                keywords.append(keyword)
        
        return keywords
    
    def _generate_from_criteria(
        self,
        criteria: str,
        keywords: List[str],
        priority: str
    ) -> Optional[GeneratedTestCase]:
        """从验收标准生成测试用例"""
        steps = []
        
        if 'login' in keywords:
            steps = self._generate_login_steps(criteria)
        elif 'crud' in keywords:
            steps = self._generate_crud_steps(criteria)
        else:
            steps = self._generate_generic_steps(criteria)
        
        if not steps:
            return None
        
        return GeneratedTestCase(
            id=f"TC-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}",
            title=f"验证: {criteria[:50]}",
            preconditions=['用户已登录系统'],
            steps=steps,
            expected_results=[step['expected'] for step in steps],
            priority=priority,
            tags=keywords,
            confidence=0.8
        )
    
    def _generate_login_steps(self, criteria: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """生成登录相关步骤"""
        return [
            {'action': '打开登录页面', 'expected': '显示登录表单'},
            {'action': '输入用户名和密码', 'expected': '凭据输入正确'},
            {'action': '点击登录按钮', 'expected': criteria}
        ]
    
    def _generate_crud_steps(self, criteria: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """生成CRUD相关步骤"""
        return [
            {'action': '导航到功能模块', 'expected': '显示功能页面'},
            {'action': '执行操作', 'expected': criteria}
        ]
    
    def _generate_generic_steps(self, criteria: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """生成通用步骤"""
        return [
            {'action': '执行测试前置条件', 'expected': '前置条件满足'},
            {'action': '执行主要操作', 'expected': criteria}
        ]
    
    def _generate_edge_cases(
        self,
        keywords: List[str],
        priority: str
    ) -> List[GeneratedTestCase]:
        """生成边界用例"""
        edge_cases = []
        
        edge_scenarios = [
            ('空值输入', '验证系统对空值的处理'),
            ('超长输入', '验证系统对超长输入的处理'),
            ('特殊字符', '验证系统对特殊字符的处理'),
            ('边界值', '验证系统对边界值的处理')
        ]
        
        for name, description in edge_scenarios:
            edge_cases.append(GeneratedTestCase(
                id=f"TC-EDGE-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}-{name}",
                title=f"边界测试: {name}",
                preconditions=['用户已登录系统'],
                steps=[
                    {'action': f'输入{name}数据', 'expected': '系统正确处理'},
                    {'action': '提交表单', 'expected': '显示适当的验证信息'}
                ],
                expected_results=['系统正确处理边界情况'],
                priority='P2',
                tags=keywords + ['edge-case'],
                confidence=0.7
            ))
        
        return edge_cases
    
    def _generate_negative_cases(
        self,
        keywords: List[str],
        priority: str
    ) -> List[GeneratedTestCase]:
        """生成负面用例"""
        negative_cases = []
        
        negative_scenarios = [
            ('权限不足', '验证无权限用户的操作被拒绝'),
            ('并发操作', '验证并发操作的数据一致性'),
            ('网络异常', '验证网络异常时的错误处理'),
            ('数据不存在', '验证操作不存在数据时的处理')
        ]
        
        for name, description in negative_scenarios:
            negative_cases.append(GeneratedTestCase(
                id=f"TC-NEG-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}-{name}",
                title=f"负面测试: {name}",
                preconditions=['准备测试环境'],
                steps=[
                    {'action': f'模拟{name}场景', 'expected': '系统进入异常状态'},
                    {'action': '执行操作', 'expected': description}
                ],
                expected_results=[description],
                priority='P2',
                tags=keywords + ['negative'],
                confidence=0.7
            ))
        
        return negative_cases

class DefectPredictor:
    """缺陷预测器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.historical_data: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
        self.code_metrics: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
    
    def train(self, historical_defects: List[Dict]):
        """训练模型"""
        for defect in historical_defects:
            file_path = defect['file_path']
            
            if file_path not in self.historical_data:
                self.historical_data[file_path] = {
                    'defect_count': 0,
                    'severity_sum': 0,
                    'files_modified': defaultdict(int)
                }
            
            self.historical_data[file_path]['defect_count'] += 1
            self.historical_data[file_path]['severity_sum'] += self._severity_score(
                defect['severity']
            )
            
            for modified_file in defect.get('modified_files', []):
                self.historical_data[file_path]['files_modified'][modified_file] += 1
    
    def _severity_score(self, severity: str) -> int:
        """严重程度分数"""
        scores = {
            'critical': 10,
            'high': 7,
            'medium': 4,
            'low': 1
        }
        return scores.get(severity.lower(), 1)
    
    def predict_risk(self, file_path: str) -> Dict:
        """预测风险"""
        if file_path not in self.historical_data:
            return {
                'risk_level': 'low',
                'risk_score': 0,
                'factors': []
            }
        
        data = self.historical_data[file_path]
        
        defect_score = min(data['defect_count'] / 10, 1.0) * 40
        severity_score = min(data['severity_sum'] / 50, 1.0) * 30
        
        related_files = len(data['files_modified'])
        coupling_score = min(related_files / 10, 1.0) * 30
        
        total_score = defect_score + severity_score + coupling_score
        
        if total_score >= 70:
            risk_level = 'high'
        elif total_score >= 40:
            risk_level = 'medium'
        else:
            risk_level = 'low'
        
        factors = []
        if data['defect_count'] > 5:
            factors.append(f"历史缺陷数: {data['defect_count']}")
        if data['severity_sum'] > 20:
            factors.append(f"严重程度累计: {data['severity_sum']}")
        if related_files > 5:
            factors.append(f"耦合文件数: {related_files}")
        
        return {
            'risk_level': risk_level,
            'risk_score': total_score,
            'factors': factors
        }
    
    def get_high_risk_files(self, threshold: float = 50) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取高风险文件"""
        high_risk = []
        
        for file_path in self.historical_data:
            risk = self.predict_risk(file_path)
            if risk['risk_score'] >= threshold:
                high_risk.append({
                    'file_path': file_path,
                    **risk
                })
        
        return sorted(high_risk, key=lambda x: x['risk_score'], reverse=True)

class TestDataGenerator:
    """测试数据生成器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.generators = {
            'email': self._generate_email,
            'phone': self._generate_phone,
            'name': self._generate_name,
            'address': self._generate_address,
            'date': self._generate_date,
            'number': self._generate_number,
            'id_card': self._generate_id_card,
        }
    
    def generate(
        self,
        field_type: str,
        count: int = 1,
        **kwargs
    ) -> List[str]:
        """生成测试数据"""
        generator = self.generators.get(field_type)
        if not generator:
            return [f"unknown_{i}" for i in range(count)]
        
        return [generator(**kwargs) for _ in range(count)]
    
    def _generate_email(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        """生成邮箱"""
        import random
        import string
        
        domains = ['gmail.com', 'outlook.com', 'qq.com', '163.com']
        username = ''.join(random.choices(string.ascii_lowercase, k=8))
        domain = random.choice(domains)
        return f"{username}@{domain}"
    
    def _generate_phone(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        """生成手机号"""
        import random
        
        prefixes = ['138', '139', '150', '151', '186', '187', '188']
        prefix = random.choice(prefixes)
        suffix = ''.join([str(random.randint(0, 9)) for _ in range(8)])
        return prefix + suffix
    
    def _generate_name(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        """生成姓名"""
        import random
        
        surnames = ['张', '李', '王', '刘', '陈', '杨', '赵', '黄', '周', '吴']
        names = ['伟', '芳', '娜', '敏', '静', '丽', '强', '磊', '军', '洋']
        
        surname = random.choice(surnames)
        name = random.choice(names)
        return surname + name
    
    def _generate_address(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        """生成地址"""
        import random
        
        provinces = ['北京市', '上海市', '广东省', '江苏省', '浙江省']
        cities = ['朝阳区', '海淀区', '浦东新区', '天河区', '南山区']
        streets = ['科技路', '建设路', '人民路', '解放路', '中山路']
        
        province = random.choice(provinces)
        city = random.choice(cities)
        street = random.choice(streets)
        number = random.randint(1, 100)
        
        return f"{province}{city}{street}{number}号"
    
    def _generate_date(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        """生成日期"""
        import random
        from datetime import datetime, timedelta
        
        start = datetime(2020, 1, 1)
        end = datetime.now()
        
        delta = end - start
        random_days = random.randint(0, delta.days)
        random_date = start + timedelta(days=random_days)
        
        return random_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
    
    def _generate_number(self, min_val: int = 1, max_val: int = 100, **kwargs) -> str:
        """生成数字"""
        import random
        return str(random.randint(min_val, max_val))
    
    def _generate_id_card(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        """生成身份证号"""
        import random
        
        area_codes = ['110101', '310101', '440106', '320102', '330102']
        area_code = random.choice(area_codes)
        
        year = random.randint(1970, 2000)
        month = random.randint(1, 12)
        day = random.randint(1, 28)
        
        sequence = random.randint(1, 999)
        
        id_17 = f"{area_code}{year}{month:02d}{day:02d}{sequence:03d}"
        
        weights = [7, 9, 10, 5, 8, 4, 2, 1, 6, 3, 7, 9, 10, 5, 8, 4, 2]
        check_codes = '10X98765432'
        
        total = sum(int(id_17[i]) * weights[i] for i in range(17))
        check_code = check_codes[total % 11]
        
        return id_17 + check_code

class TestMaintenanceAssistant:
    """测试维护助手"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.failure_patterns: Dict[str, List[Dict]] = defaultdict(list)
    
    def analyze_failure(
        self,
        test_name: str,
        error_message: str,
        error_traceback: str
    ) -> Dict:
        """分析失败原因"""
        analysis = {
            'test_name': test_name,
            'error_type': self._classify_error(error_message),
            'root_cause': self._identify_root_cause(error_message, error_traceback),
            'suggestions': [],
            'auto_fix_available': False
        }
        
        analysis['suggestions'] = self._generate_suggestions(analysis)
        analysis['auto_fix_available'] = self._check_auto_fix(analysis)
        
        self.failure_patterns[test_name].append({
            'error_message': error_message,
            'error_type': analysis['error_type'],
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        
        return analysis
    
    def _classify_error(self, error_message: str) -> str:
        """分类错误"""
        patterns = {
            'element_not_found': r'NoSuchElementException|元素未找到|Unable to locate element',
            'timeout': r'TimeoutException|超时|timeout',
            'assertion': r'AssertionError|断言失败|assert',
            'connection': r'ConnectionError|连接|connection refused',
            'permission': r'PermissionError|权限|permission denied'
        }
        
        for error_type, pattern in patterns.items():
            if re.search(pattern, error_message, re.IGNORECASE):
                return error_type
        
        return 'unknown'
    
    def _identify_root_cause(
        self,
        error_message: str,
        error_traceback: str
    ) -> str:
        """识别根因"""
        error_type = self._classify_error(error_message)
        
        causes = {
            'element_not_found': '页面元素定位器可能已变更，或页面结构发生变化',
            'timeout': '页面加载时间过长，或异步操作未完成',
            'assertion': '预期结果与实际结果不符',
            'connection': '网络连接问题或服务不可用',
            'permission': '用户权限不足或认证失败'
        }
        
        return causes.get(error_type, '未知原因')
    
    def _generate_suggestions(self, analysis: Dict) -> List[str]:
        """生成建议"""
        suggestions = []
        
        error_type = analysis['error_type']
        
        if error_type == 'element_not_found':
            suggestions.extend([
                '检查元素定位器是否正确',
                '验证页面是否完全加载',
                '检查是否有iframe需要切换',
                '考虑使用更稳定的定位策略'
            ])
        elif error_type == 'timeout':
            suggestions.extend([
                '增加等待时间',
                '添加显式等待条件',
                '检查网络连接状态',
                '优化页面加载性能'
            ])
        elif error_type == 'assertion':
            suggestions.extend([
                '检查预期结果是否正确',
                '验证测试数据是否有效',
                '检查业务逻辑是否变更'
            ])
        
        return suggestions
    
    def _check_auto_fix(self, analysis: Dict) -> bool:
        """检查是否可以自动修复"""
        auto_fixable = ['element_not_found', 'timeout']
        return analysis['error_type'] in auto_fixable
    
    def suggest_locator_update(
        self,
        old_locator: str,
        page_html: str
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """建议定位器更新"""
        suggestions = []
        
        old_id = re.search(r'id[=:]\s*["\']([^"\']+)["\']', old_locator)
        if old_id:
            old_id_value = old_id.group(1)
            similar_elements = re.findall(
                rf'<[^>]+(?:id|data-testid)[=:]\s*["\'][^"\']*{old_id_value[:5]}[^"\']*["\'][^>]*>',
                page_html,
                re.IGNORECASE
            )
            
            for element in similar_elements:
                new_id = re.search(r'(?:id|data-testid)[=:]\s*["\']([^"\']+)["\']', element)
                if new_id:
                    suggestions.append({
                        'old_locator': old_locator,
                        'new_locator': f"id={new_id.group(1)}",
                        'confidence': 0.8,
                        'element_html': element[:100]
                    })
        
        return suggestions

# 使用示例
# 测试用例生成
generator = TestCaseGenerator()
requirement = Requirement(
    id='REQ-001',
    title='用户登录功能',
    description='用户可以通过用户名和密码登录系统',
    acceptance_criteria=[
        '使用正确的用户名和密码可以成功登录',
        '使用错误的密码应该显示错误提示'
    ],
    priority='P0'
)

test_cases = generator.generate_from_requirement(requirement)
for tc in test_cases:
    print(f"用例: {tc.title}, 置信度: {tc.confidence}")

# 缺陷预测
predictor = DefectPredictor()
predictor.train([
    {'file_path': 'auth/login.py', 'severity': 'high', 'modified_files': ['auth/utils.py']},
    {'file_path': 'auth/login.py', 'severity': 'medium', 'modified_files': ['models/user.py']},
])

risk = predictor.predict_risk('auth/login.py')
print(f"风险等级: {risk['risk_level']}, 风险分数: {risk['risk_score']}")

# 测试数据生成
data_gen = TestDataGenerator()
emails = data_gen.generate('email', count=5)
phones = data_gen.generate('phone', count=5)
print(f"生成的邮箱: {emails}")
print(f"生成的手机号: {phones}")

# 测试维护助手
assistant = TestMaintenanceAssistant()
analysis = assistant.analyze_failure(
    'test_login',
    'NoSuchElementException: Unable to locate element: username',
    'Traceback...'
)
print(f"错误类型: {analysis['error_type']}")
print(f"建议: {analysis['suggestions']}")
```

## 十八、容器化与云原生测试

### 18.1 Docker测试环境

**Q31: 如何使用Docker构建测试环境？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析Docker测试环境的需求：
- **环境一致性**：确保测试环境与生产环境一致
- **快速部署**：快速创建和销毁测试环境
- **资源隔离**：测试环境之间相互隔离
- **版本管理**：支持多版本环境并行

其次，我会设计Docker测试环境方案：

```python
# platform/docker_testing.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
import subprocess
import json
import time
import docker
from docker.models.containers import Container
from docker.models.networks import Network

class ContainerStatus(Enum):
    """容器状态"""
    RUNNING = "running"
    STOPPED = "stopped"
    PAUSED = "paused"
    ERROR = "error"

@dataclass
class ServiceConfig:
    """服务配置"""
    name: str
    image: str
    ports: Dict[str, int]
    environment: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    volumes: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    depends_on: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    health_check: Optional[Dict] = None
    command: Optional[str] = None

@dataclass
class TestEnvironment:
    """测试环境"""
    name: str
    services: Dict[str, Container] = field(default_factory=dict)
    network: Optional[Network] = None
    status: ContainerStatus = ContainerStatus.STOPPED
    created_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)

class DockerComposeBuilder:
    """Docker Compose构建器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.services: Dict[str, ServiceConfig] = {}
    
    def add_service(self, config: ServiceConfig):
        """添加服务"""
        self.services[config.name] = config
    
    def generate_compose_file(self) -> str:
        """生成docker-compose.yml"""
        compose = {
            'version': '3.8',
            'services': {}
        }
        
        for name, config in self.services.items():
            service = {
                'image': config.image,
                'ports': [f"{v}:{k}" for k, v in config.ports.items()],
                'environment': config.environment,
                'restart': 'unless-stopped'
            }
            
            if config.volumes:
                service['volumes'] = [
                    f"{v}:{k}" for k, v in config.volumes.items()
                ]
            
            if config.depends_on:
                service['depends_on'] = config.depends_on
            
            if config.health_check:
                service['healthcheck'] = config.health_check
            
            if config.command:
                service['command'] = config.command
            
            compose['services'][name] = service
        
        import yaml
        return yaml.dump(compose, default_flow_style=False)

class DockerEnvironmentManager:
    """Docker环境管理器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.client = docker.from_env()
        self.environments: Dict[str, TestEnvironment] = {}
    
    def create_environment(
        self,
        name: str,
        services: List[ServiceConfig]
    ) -> TestEnvironment:
        """创建测试环境"""
        network = self.client.networks.create(
            f"{name}_network",
            driver="bridge"
        )
        
        env = TestEnvironment(
            name=name,
            network=network,
            status=ContainerStatus.RUNNING
        )
        
        sorted_services = self._sort_by_dependencies(services)
        
        for config in sorted_services:
            container = self._create_container(config, network)
            env.services[config.name] = container
        
        self.environments[name] = env
        return env
    
    def _sort_by_dependencies(
        self,
        services: List[ServiceConfig]
    ) -> List[ServiceConfig]:
        """按依赖排序"""
        service_map = {s.name: s for s in services}
        visited = set()
        result = []
        
        def visit(name: str):
            if name in visited:
                return
            visited.add(name)
            
            service = service_map.get(name)
            if service:
                for dep in service.depends_on:
                    visit(dep)
                result.append(service)
        
        for service in services:
            visit(service.name)
        
        return result
    
    def _create_container(
        self,
        config: ServiceConfig,
        network: Network
    ) -> Container:
        """创建容器"""
        container = self.client.containers.run(
            config.image,
            name=config.name,
            ports=config.ports,
            environment=config.environment,
            volumes=config.volumes,
            network=network.name,
            detach=True,
            command=config.command
        )
        
        if config.health_check:
            self._wait_for_healthy(container, config.health_check)
        
        return container
    
    def _wait_for_healthy(
        self,
        container: Container,
        health_check: Dict,
        timeout: int = 60
    ):
        """等待容器健康"""
        start_time = time.time()
        
        while time.time() - start_time < timeout:
            container.reload()
            
            status = container.attrs.get('State', {}).get('Health', {}).get('Status')
            if status == 'healthy':
                return True
            
            time.sleep(1)
        
        raise Exception(f"容器 {container.name} 健康检查超时")
    
    def stop_environment(self, name: str):
        """停止环境"""
        env = self.environments.get(name)
        if not env:
            return
        
        for container in env.services.values():
            try:
                container.stop()
            except:
                pass
        
        env.status = ContainerStatus.STOPPED
    
    def start_environment(self, name: str):
        """启动环境"""
        env = self.environments.get(name)
        if not env:
            return
        
        for container in env.services.values():
            try:
                container.start()
            except:
                pass
        
        env.status = ContainerStatus.RUNNING
    
    def destroy_environment(self, name: str):
        """销毁环境"""
        env = self.environments.get(name)
        if not env:
            return
        
        for container in env.services.values():
            try:
                container.remove(force=True)
            except:
                pass
        
        if env.network:
            try:
                env.network.remove()
            except:
                pass
        
        del self.environments[name]
    
    def get_environment_status(self, name: str) -> Dict:
        """获取环境状态"""
        env = self.environments.get(name)
        if not env:
            return {}
        
        services_status = {}
        for name, container in env.services.items():
            container.reload()
            services_status[name] = {
                'status': container.status,
                'ip': container.attrs['NetworkSettings']['IPAddress']
            }
        
        return {
            'name': env.name,
            'status': env.status.value,
            'services': services_status,
            'created_at': env.created_at
        }
    
    def execute_in_container(
        self,
        env_name: str,
        service_name: str,
        command: str
    ) -> str:
        """在容器中执行命令"""
        env = self.environments.get(env_name)
        if not env:
            raise Exception(f"环境 {env_name} 不存在")
        
        container = env.services.get(service_name)
        if not container:
            raise Exception(f"服务 {service_name} 不存在")
        
        exit_code, output = container.exec_run(command)
        return output.decode()

class DatabaseTestContainer:
    """数据库测试容器"""
    
    def __init__(self, db_type: str = 'postgres'):
        self.db_type = db_type
        self.container: Optional[Container] = None
        self.client = docker.from_env()
    
    def start(self, port: int = 5432) -> Dict:
        """启动数据库容器"""
        configs = {
            'postgres': {
                'image': 'postgres:14',
                'environment': {
                    'POSTGRES_USER': 'test',
                    'POSTGRES_PASSWORD': 'test',
                    'POSTGRES_DB': 'testdb'
                },
                'port': 5432
            },
            'mysql': {
                'image': 'mysql:8',
                'environment': {
                    'MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD': 'test',
                    'MYSQL_DATABASE': 'testdb'
                },
                'port': 3306
            },
            'redis': {
                'image': 'redis:7-alpine',
                'environment': {},
                'port': 6379
            },
            'mongodb': {
                'image': 'mongo:6',
                'environment': {
                    'MONGO_INITDB_ROOT_USERNAME': 'test',
                    'MONGO_INITDB_ROOT_PASSWORD': 'test'
                },
                'port': 27017
            }
        }
        
        config = configs.get(self.db_type)
        if not config:
            raise ValueError(f"不支持的数据库类型: {self.db_type}")
        
        self.container = self.client.containers.run(
            config['image'],
            environment=config['environment'],
            ports={f"{config['port']}/tcp": port},
            detach=True
        )
        
        time.sleep(5)
        
        return {
            'container_id': self.container.id,
            'port': port,
            'connection_string': self._get_connection_string(port)
        }
    
    def _get_connection_string(self, port: int) -> str:
        """获取连接字符串"""
        strings = {
            'postgres': f"postgresql://test:test@localhost:{port}/testdb",
            'mysql': f"mysql://root:test@localhost:{port}/testdb",
            'redis': f"redis://localhost:{port}",
            'mongodb': f"mongodb://test:test@localhost:{port}"
        }
        return strings.get(self.db_type, '')
    
    def stop(self):
        """停止容器"""
        if self.container:
            self.container.stop()
            self.container.remove()
            self.container = None
    
    def reset_data(self):
        """重置数据"""
        if not self.container:
            return
        
        reset_commands = {
            'postgres': 'psql -U test -d testdb -c "DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE; CREATE SCHEMA public;"',
            'mysql': 'mysql -uroot -ptest -e "DROP DATABASE testdb; CREATE DATABASE testdb;"',
            'redis': 'redis-cli FLUSHALL',
            'mongodb': 'mongosh -u test -p test --eval "db.dropDatabase()"'
        }
        
        command = reset_commands.get(self.db_type)
        if command:
            self.container.exec_run(command)

class SeleniumGridManager:
    """Selenium Grid管理器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.client = docker.from_env()
        self.hub: Optional[Container] = None
        self.nodes: List[Container] = []
    
    def start_grid(self, port: int = 4444):
        """启动Selenium Grid"""
        self.hub = self.client.containers.run(
            'selenium/hub:4',
            name='selenium-hub',
            ports={'4444/tcp': port},
            environment={
                'GRID_MAX_SESSION': '10',
                'GRID_BROWSER_TIMEOUT': '300',
                'GRID_TIMEOUT': '300'
            },
            detach=True
        )
        
        time.sleep(5)
    
    def add_chrome_node(self, count: int = 1):
        """添加Chrome节点"""
        for i in range(count):
            node = self.client.containers.run(
                'selenium/node-chrome:4',
                name=f'selenium-chrome-{i}',
                environment={
                    'SE_EVENT_BUS_HOST': 'selenium-hub',
                    'SE_EVENT_BUS_PUBLISH_PORT': '4442',
                    'SE_EVENT_BUS_SUBSCRIBE_PORT': '4443',
                    'SE_NODE_MAX_SESSIONS': '5'
                },
                links={'selenium-hub': 'selenium-hub'},
                detach=True
            )
            self.nodes.append(node)
    
    def add_firefox_node(self, count: int = 1):
        """添加Firefox节点"""
        for i in range(count):
            node = self.client.containers.run(
                'selenium/node-firefox:4',
                name=f'selenium-firefox-{i}',
                environment={
                    'SE_EVENT_BUS_HOST': 'selenium-hub',
                    'SE_EVENT_BUS_PUBLISH_PORT': '4442',
                    'SE_EVENT_BUS_SUBSCRIBE_PORT': '4443',
                    'SE_NODE_MAX_SESSIONS': '5'
                },
                links={'selenium-hub': 'selenium-hub'},
                detach=True
            )
            self.nodes.append(node)
    
    def stop_grid(self):
        """停止Grid"""
        for node in self.nodes:
            try:
                node.stop()
                node.remove()
            except:
                pass
        
        if self.hub:
            self.hub.stop()
            self.hub.remove()
        
        self.nodes.clear()
        self.hub = None
    
    def get_grid_status(self) -> Dict:
        """获取Grid状态"""
        import requests
        
        try:
            response = requests.get('http://localhost:4444/status')
            return response.json()
        except:
            return {'status': 'unavailable'}

# 使用示例
# 创建测试环境
manager = DockerEnvironmentManager()

# 定义服务
services = [
    ServiceConfig(
        name='postgres',
        image='postgres:14',
        ports={'5432': 5433},
        environment={
            'POSTGRES_USER': 'test',
            'POSTGRES_PASSWORD': 'test',
            'POSTGRES_DB': 'testdb'
        },
        health_check={
            'test': ['CMD', 'pg_isready', '-U', 'test'],
            'interval': '5s',

        }
    ),
    ServiceConfig(
        name='redis',
        image='redis:7-alpine',
        ports={'6379': 6380}
    ),
    ServiceConfig(
        name='backend',
        image='myapp-backend:latest',
        ports={'8000': 8001},
        environment={
            'DATABASE_URL': 'postgresql://test:test@postgres:5432/testdb',
            'REDIS_URL': 'redis://redis:6379'
        },
        depends_on=['postgres', 'redis']
    )
]

# 创建环境

# 查看状态

print(json.dumps(status, indent=2))

# 数据库测试容器
```

### 18.2 Kubernetes测试环境

**Q32: 如何在Kubernetes中部署测试环境？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析Kubernetes测试环境的需求：
- **资源调度**：自动调度测试资源
- **弹性伸缩**：根据负载自动扩缩容
- **服务发现**：自动服务注册和发现
- **配置管理**：ConfigMap和Secret管理

其次，我会设计Kubernetes测试环境方案：

```python
# platform/k8s_testing.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
import yaml
import time

class ResourceType(Enum):
    """资源类型"""
    DEPLOYMENT = "Deployment"
    SERVICE = "Service"
    CONFIGMAP = "ConfigMap"
    SECRET = "Secret"
    POD = "Pod"
    NAMESPACE = "Namespace"
    JOB = "Job"

@dataclass
class K8sResource:
    """K8s资源"""
    name: str
    resource_type: ResourceType
    manifest: Dict
    namespace: str = "default"

@dataclass
class TestNamespace:
    """测试命名空间"""
    name: str
    resources: Dict[str, K8sResource] = field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)

class K8sManifestBuilder:
    """K8s清单构建器"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def build_deployment(
        name: str,
        image: str,
        replicas: int = 1,
        ports: List[int] = None,
        env: Dict[str, str] = None,
        resources: Dict = None,
        liveness_probe: Dict = None,
        readiness_probe: Dict = None
    ) -> Dict:
        """构建Deployment"""
        container = {
            'name': name,
            'image': image,
            'ports': [{'containerPort': p} for p in (ports or [])],
            'env': [{'name': k, 'value': v} for k, v in (env or {}).items()]
        }
        
        if resources:
            container['resources'] = resources
        
        if liveness_probe:
            container['livenessProbe'] = liveness_probe
        
        if readiness_probe:
            container['readinessProbe'] = readiness_probe
        
        return {
            'apiVersion': 'apps/v1',
            'kind': 'Deployment',
            'metadata': {
                'name': name,
                'labels': {'app': name}
            },
            'spec': {
                'replicas': replicas,
                'selector': {
                    'matchLabels': {'app': name}
                },
                'template': {
                    'metadata': {
                        'labels': {'app': name}
                    },
                    'spec': {
                        'containers': [container]
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def build_service(
        name: str,
        port: int,
        target_port: int,
        service_type: str = "ClusterIP",
        selector: Dict[str, str] = None
    ) -> Dict:
        """构建Service"""
        return {
            'apiVersion': 'v1',
            'kind': 'Service',
            'metadata': {
                'name': name
            },
            'spec': {
                'type': service_type,
                'selector': selector or {'app': name},
                'ports': [{
                    'port': port,
                    'targetPort': target_port
                }]
            }
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def build_configmap(
        name: str,
        data: Dict[str, str]
    ) -> Dict:
        """构建ConfigMap"""
        return {
            'apiVersion': 'v1',
            'kind': 'ConfigMap',
            'metadata': {
                'name': name
            },
            'data': data
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def build_secret(
        name: str,
        data: Dict[str, str],
        secret_type: str = "Opaque"
    ) -> Dict:
        """构建Secret"""
        import base64
        
        encoded_data = {
            k: base64.b64encode(v.encode()).decode()
            for k, v in data.items()
        }
        
        return {
            'apiVersion': 'v1',
            'kind': 'Secret',
            'metadata': {
                'name': name
            },
            'type': secret_type,
            'data': encoded_data
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def build_job(
        name: str,
        image: str,
        command: List[str],
        env: Dict[str, str] = None,
        backoff_limit: int = 0
    ) -> Dict:
        """构建Job"""
        return {
            'apiVersion': 'batch/v1',
            'kind': 'Job',
            'metadata': {
                'name': name
            },
            'spec': {
                'backoffLimit': backoff_limit,
                'template': {
                    'spec': {
                        'restartPolicy': 'Never',
                        'containers': [{
                            'name': name,
                            'image': image,
                            'command': command,
                            'env': [{'name': k, 'value': v} for k, v in (env or {}).items()]
                        }]
                    }
                }
            }
        }

class K8sTestEnvironment:
    """K8s测试环境"""
    
    def __init__(self, namespace_prefix: str = "test"):
        self.namespace_prefix = namespace_prefix
        self.namespaces: Dict[str, TestNamespace] = {}
        self._init_client()
    
    def _init_client(self):
        """初始化K8s客户端"""
        try:
            from kubernetes import client, config
            config.load_kube_config()
            self.api = client.CoreV1Api()
            self.apps_api = client.AppsV1Api()
            self.batch_api = client.BatchV1Api()
            self.k8s_client = client
        except ImportError:
            self.api = None
            self.apps_api = None
            self.batch_api = None
            self.k8s_client = None
    
    def create_namespace(self, name: str) -> TestNamespace:
        """创建命名空间"""
        full_name = f"{self.namespace_prefix}-{name}"
        
        namespace = TestNamespace(name=full_name)
        self.namespaces[full_name] = namespace
        
        if self.api:
            from kubernetes import client
            ns = client.V1Namespace(
                metadata=client.V1ObjectMeta(name=full_name)
            )
            self.api.create_namespace(ns)
        
        return namespace
    
    def apply_resource(self, namespace: str, resource: K8sResource):
        """应用资源"""
        ns = self.namespaces.get(namespace)
        if not ns:
            return
        
        ns.resources[resource.name] = resource
        
        if not self.api:
            return
        
        manifest = resource.manifest
        
        if resource.resource_type == ResourceType.DEPLOYMENT:
            self.apps_api.create_namespaced_deployment(
                namespace=namespace,
                body=manifest
            )
        elif resource.resource_type == ResourceType.SERVICE:
            self.api.create_namespaced_service(
                namespace=namespace,
                body=manifest
            )
        elif resource.resource_type == ResourceType.CONFIGMAP:
            self.api.create_namespaced_config_map(
                namespace=namespace,
                body=manifest
            )
        elif resource.resource_type == ResourceType.SECRET:
            self.api.create_namespaced_secret(
                namespace=namespace,
                body=manifest
            )
        elif resource.resource_type == ResourceType.JOB:
            self.batch_api.create_namespaced_job(
                namespace=namespace,
                body=manifest
            )
    
    def delete_namespace(self, name: str):
        """删除命名空间"""
        ns = self.namespaces.get(name)
        if not ns:
            return
        
        if self.api:
            self.api.delete_namespace(name)
        
        del self.namespaces[name]
    
    def get_pod_status(self, namespace: str, label_selector: str = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取Pod状态"""
        if not self.api:
            return []
        
        pods = self.api.list_namespaced_pod(
            namespace=namespace,
            label_selector=label_selector
        )
        
        return [
            {
                'name': pod.metadata.name,
                'status': pod.status.phase,
                'pod_ip': pod.status.pod_ip,
                'containers': [
                    {
                        'name': c.name,
                        'ready': c.ready,
                        'image': c.image
                    }
                    for c in pod.status.container_statuses or []
                ]
            }
            for pod in pods.items
        ]
    
    def get_logs(self, namespace: str, pod_name: str, container: str = None) -> str:
        """获取日志"""
        if not self.api:
            return ""
        
        return self.api.read_namespaced_pod_log(
            name=pod_name,
            namespace=namespace,
            container=container
        )
    
    def wait_for_deployment_ready(
        self,
        namespace: str,
        deployment_name: str,
        timeout: int = 300
    ) -> bool:
        """等待Deployment就绪"""
        start_time = time.time()
        
        while time.time() - start_time < timeout:
            if not self.apps_api:
                time.sleep(5)
                continue
            
            deployment = self.apps_api.read_namespaced_deployment(
                name=deployment_name,
                namespace=namespace
            )
            
            if deployment.status.ready_replicas == deployment.spec.replicas:
                return True
            
            time.sleep(5)
        
        return False
    
    def scale_deployment(
        self,
        namespace: str,
        deployment_name: str,
        replicas: int
    ):
        """扩缩容Deployment"""
        if not self.apps_api:
            return
        
        from kubernetes import client
        
        body = client.V1Scale(
            spec=client.V1ScaleSpec(replicas=replicas)
        )
        
        self.apps_api.patch_namespaced_deployment_scale(
            name=deployment_name,
            namespace=namespace,
            body=body
        )

class K8sTestRunner:
    """K8s测试运行器"""
    
    def __init__(self, k8s_env: K8sTestEnvironment):
        self.env = k8s_env
        self.builder = K8sManifestBuilder()
    
    def setup_test_environment(
        self,
        env_name: str,
        services: List[Dict]
    ) -> str:
        """设置测试环境"""
        namespace = self.env.create_namespace(env_name)
        
        for service in services:
            deployment = self.builder.build_deployment(
                name=service['name'],
                image=service['image'],
                replicas=service.get('replicas', 1),
                ports=service.get('ports', []),
                env=service.get('env', {}),
                resources=service.get('resources'),
                liveness_probe=service.get('liveness_probe'),
                readiness_probe=service.get('readiness_probe')
            )
            
            self.env.apply_resource(
                namespace.name,
                K8sResource(
                    name=service['name'],
                    resource_type=ResourceType.DEPLOYMENT,
                    manifest=deployment,
                    namespace=namespace.name
                )
            )
            
            if service.get('ports'):
                svc = self.builder.build_service(
                    name=service['name'],
                    port=service['ports'][0],
                    target_port=service['ports'][0],
                    service_type=service.get('service_type', 'ClusterIP')
                )
                
                self.env.apply_resource(
                    namespace.name,
                    K8sResource(
                        name=f"{service['name']}-svc",
                        resource_type=ResourceType.SERVICE,
                        manifest=svc,
                        namespace=namespace.name
                    )
                )
        
        return namespace.name
    
    def run_test_job(
        self,
        namespace: str,
        test_name: str,
        image: str,
        command: List[str],
        env: Dict[str, str] = None
    ) -> str:
        """运行测试Job"""
        job = self.builder.build_job(
            name=test_name,
            image=image,
            command=command,
            env=env
        )
        
        self.env.apply_resource(
            namespace,
            K8sResource(
                name=test_name,
                resource_type=ResourceType.JOB,
                manifest=job,
                namespace=namespace
            )
        )
        
        return test_name
    
    def wait_for_job_completion(
        self,
        namespace: str,
        job_name: str,
        timeout: int = 600
    ) -> Dict:
        """等待Job完成"""
        start_time = time.time()
        
        while time.time() - start_time < timeout:
            if not self.env.batch_api:
                time.sleep(5)
                continue
            
            job = self.env.batch_api.read_namespaced_job(
                name=job_name,
                namespace=namespace
            )
            
            if job.status.succeeded:
                pods = self.env.api.list_namespaced_pod(
                    namespace=namespace,
                    label_selector=f"job-name={job_name}"
                )
                
                if pods.items:
                    logs = self.env.get_logs(
                        namespace,
                        pods.items[0].metadata.name
                    )
                    return {
                        'status': 'succeeded',
                        'logs': logs
                    }
            
            if job.status.failed:
                return {
                    'status': 'failed',
                    'message': 'Job执行失败'
                }
            
            time.sleep(5)
        
        return {
            'status': 'timeout',
            'message': 'Job执行超时'
        }
    
    def cleanup(self, namespace: str):
        """清理环境"""
        self.env.delete_namespace(namespace)

# 使用示例
k8s_env = K8sTestEnvironment("test-auto")
runner = K8sTestRunner(k8s_env)

# 定义测试服务
services = [
    {
        'name': 'backend',
        'image': 'myapp-backend:latest',
        'replicas': 2,
        'ports': [8000],
        'env': {
            'DEBUG': 'True',
            'DATABASE_URL': 'postgresql://test:test@postgres:5432/testdb'
        },
        'resources': {
            'requests': {'cpu': '100m', 'memory': '128Mi'},
            'limits': {'cpu': '500m', 'memory': '512Mi'}
        },
        'liveness_probe': {
            'httpGet': {'path': '/health', 'port': 8000},
            'initialDelaySeconds': 10,
            'periodSeconds': 10
        },
        'readiness_probe': {
            'httpGet': {'path': '/ready', 'port': 8000},
            'initialDelaySeconds': 5,
            'periodSeconds': 5
        }
    },
    {
        'name': 'postgres',
        'image': 'postgres:14',
        'ports': [5432],
        'env': {
            'POSTGRES_USER': 'test',
            'POSTGRES_PASSWORD': 'test',
            'POSTGRES_DB': 'testdb'
        }
    }
]

# 创建测试环境
namespace = runner.setup_test_environment('api-test', services)

# 运行测试Job
job_name = runner.run_test_job(
    namespace=namespace,
    test_name='api-test-job',
    image='myapp-test:latest',
    command=['pytest', 'tests/', '-v', '--junitxml=/results/report.xml'],
    env={'API_URL': 'http://backend:8000'}
)

# 等待完成
result = runner.wait_for_job_completion(namespace, job_name)
print(f"测试结果: {result['status']}")

# 清理
runner.cleanup(namespace)
```

## 十九、微服务测试

### 19.1 服务虚拟化

**Q33: 如何实现微服务测试中的服务虚拟化？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析服务虚拟化的需求：
- **依赖隔离**：隔离被测服务的外部依赖
- **契约测试**：验证服务间契约
- **场景模拟**：模拟各种响应场景
- **延迟注入**：模拟网络延迟和故障

其次，我会设计服务虚拟化方案：

```python
# platform/service_virtualization.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Callable, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime
import json
import re
import time
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

class ScenarioType(Enum):
    """场景类型"""
    HAPPY_PATH = "happy_path"
    ERROR = "error"
    TIMEOUT = "timeout"
    SLOW = "slow"
    FAULT = "fault"

@dataclass
class VirtualService:
    """虚拟服务"""
    name: str
    base_url: str
    endpoints: List[Dict]
    scenarios: Dict[str, Dict] = field(default_factory=dict)
    call_history: List[Dict] = field(default_factory=list)

@dataclass
class ServiceContract:
    """服务契约"""
    service_name: str
    version: str
    endpoints: List[Dict]
    schemas: Dict[str, Dict] = field(default_factory=dict)

class ContractValidator:
    """契约验证器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.contracts: Dict[str, ServiceContract] = {}
    
    def register_contract(self, contract: ServiceContract):
        """注册契约"""
        self.contracts[contract.service_name] = contract
    
    def validate_request(
        self,
        service_name: str,
        endpoint: str,
        method: str,
        request_data: Dict
    ) -> Dict:
        """验证请求"""
        contract = self.contracts.get(service_name)
        if not contract:
            return {'valid': False, 'errors': ['契约未找到']}
        
        endpoint_contract = self._find_endpoint_contract(
            contract, endpoint, method
        )
        
        if not endpoint_contract:
            return {'valid': False, 'errors': ['端点契约未找到']}
        
        errors = []
        
        request_schema = endpoint_contract.get('request_schema')
        if request_schema:
            schema_errors = self._validate_schema(
                request_data,
                request_schema
            )
            errors.extend(schema_errors)
        
        return {
            'valid': len(errors) == 0,
            'errors': errors
        }
    
    def validate_response(
        self,
        service_name: str,
        endpoint: str,
        method: str,
        response_data: Dict
    ) -> Dict:
        """验证响应"""
        contract = self.contracts.get(service_name)
        if not contract:
            return {'valid': False, 'errors': ['契约未找到']}
        
        endpoint_contract = self._find_endpoint_contract(
            contract, endpoint, method
        )
        
        if not endpoint_contract:
            return {'valid': False, 'errors': ['端点契约未找到']}
        
        errors = []
        
        response_schema = endpoint_contract.get('response_schema')
        if response_schema:
            schema_errors = self._validate_schema(
                response_data,
                response_schema
            )
            errors.extend(schema_errors)
        
        return {
            'valid': len(errors) == 0,
            'errors': errors
        }
    
    def _find_endpoint_contract(
        self,
        contract: ServiceContract,
        endpoint: str,
        method: str
    ) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """查找端点契约"""
        for ep in contract.endpoints:
            if ep['path'] == endpoint and ep['method'] == method:
                return ep
        return None
    
    def _validate_schema(
        self,
        data: Dict,
        schema: Dict
    ) -> List[str]:
        """验证Schema"""
        errors = []
        
        required_fields = schema.get('required', [])
        properties = schema.get('properties', {})
        
        for field in required_fields:
            if field not in data:
                errors.append(f"缺少必填字段: {field}")
        
        for field, field_schema in properties.items():
            if field in data:
                value = data[field]
                expected_type = field_schema.get('type')
                
                if expected_type == 'string' and not isinstance(value, str):
                    errors.append(f"字段 {field} 类型错误，期望 string")
                elif expected_type == 'number' and not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
                    errors.append(f"字段 {field} 类型错误，期望 number")
                elif expected_type == 'boolean' and not isinstance(value, bool):
                    errors.append(f"字段 {field} 类型错误，期望 boolean")
                elif expected_type == 'array' and not isinstance(value, list):
                    errors.append(f"字段 {field} 类型错误，期望 array")
                elif expected_type == 'object' and not isinstance(value, dict):
                    errors.append(f"字段 {field} 类型错误，期望 object")
        
        return errors

class ServiceVirtualizer:
    """服务虚拟化器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.virtual_services: Dict[str, VirtualService] = {}
        self.contract_validator = ContractValidator()
    
    def create_virtual_service(
        self,
        name: str,
        base_url: str,
        endpoints: List[Dict]
    ) -> VirtualService:
        """创建虚拟服务"""
        service = VirtualService(
            name=name,
            base_url=base_url,
            endpoints=endpoints
        )
        
        self._setup_default_scenarios(service)
        self.virtual_services[name] = service
        
        return service
    
    def _setup_default_scenarios(self, service: VirtualService):
        """设置默认场景"""
        for endpoint in service.endpoints:
            scenario_key = f"{endpoint['method']}:{endpoint['path']}"
            
            service.scenarios[scenario_key] = {
                'happy_path': {
                    'status_code': endpoint.get('default_status', 200),
                    'response': endpoint.get('default_response', {}),
                    'delay': 0
                },
                'error': {
                    'status_code': 500,
                    'response': {'error': 'Internal Server Error'},
                    'delay': 0
                },
                'timeout': {
                    'status_code': None,
                    'response': None,
                    'delay': 30
                },
                'slow': {
                    'status_code': endpoint.get('default_status', 200),
                    'response': endpoint.get('default_response', {}),
                    'delay': 5
                }
            }
    
    def add_custom_scenario(
        self,
        service_name: str,
        endpoint: str,
        method: str,
        scenario_name: str,
        response: Dict,
        status_code: int = 200,
        delay: float = 0
    ):
        """添加自定义场景"""
        service = self.virtual_services.get(service_name)
        if not service:
            return
        
        scenario_key = f"{method}:{endpoint}"
        
        if scenario_key not in service.scenarios:
            service.scenarios[scenario_key] = {}
        
        service.scenarios[scenario_key][scenario_name] = {
            'status_code': status_code,
            'response': response,
            'delay': delay
        }
    
    def handle_request(
        self,
        service_name: str,
        endpoint: str,
        method: str,
        request_data: Dict = None,
        headers: Dict = None,
        active_scenario: str = 'happy_path'
    ) -> Dict:
        """处理请求"""
        service = self.virtual_services.get(service_name)
        if not service:
            return {
                'status_code': 404,
                'response': {'error': f'服务 {service_name} 不存在'}
            }
        
        scenario_key = f"{method}:{endpoint}"
        scenarios = service.scenarios.get(scenario_key, {})
        scenario = scenarios.get(active_scenario, scenarios.get('happy_path', {}))
        
        service.call_history.append({
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
            'endpoint': endpoint,
            'method': method,
            'request': request_data,
            'headers': headers,
            'scenario': active_scenario
        })
        
        if scenario.get('delay', 0) > 0:
            time.sleep(scenario['delay'])
        
        return {
            'status_code': scenario.get('status_code', 200),
            'response': scenario.get('response', {})
        }
    
    def get_call_history(
        self,
        service_name: str,
        endpoint: str = None
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取调用历史"""
        service = self.virtual_services.get(service_name)
        if not service:
            return []
        
        if endpoint:
            return [
                call for call in service.call_history
                if call['endpoint'] == endpoint
            ]
        
        return service.call_history
    
    def verify_calls(
        self,
        service_name: str,
        expected_calls: List[Dict]
    ) -> Dict:
        """验证调用"""
        service = self.virtual_services.get(service_name)
        if not service:
            return {'verified': False, 'errors': ['服务不存在']}
        
        errors = []
        
        for expected in expected_calls:
            matching_calls = [
                call for call in service.call_history
                if call['endpoint'] == expected['endpoint']
                and call['method'] == expected['method']
            ]
            
            if expected.get('times'):
                if len(matching_calls) != expected['times']:
                    errors.append(
                        f"{expected['method']} {expected['endpoint']} "
                        f"调用次数不匹配: 期望 {expected['times']}, "
                        f"实际 {len(matching_calls)}"
                    )
            
            if expected.get('request'):
                for call in matching_calls:
                    for key, value in expected['request'].items():
                        if call.get('request', {}).get(key) != value:
                            errors.append(
                                f"请求参数 {key} 不匹配: "
                                f"期望 {value}, "
                                f"实际 {call.get('request', {}).get(key)}"
                            )
        
        return {
            'verified': len(errors) == 0,
            'errors': errors
        }
    
    def reset(self, service_name: str = None):
        """重置"""
        if service_name:
            service = self.virtual_services.get(service_name)
            if service:
                service.call_history.clear()
        else:
            for service in self.virtual_services.values():
                service.call_history.clear()

class ChaosInjector:
    """混沌注入器"""
    
    def __init__(self, virtualizer: ServiceVirtualizer):
        self.virtualizer = virtualizer
        self.active_faults: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
    
    def inject_latency(
        self,
        service_name: str,
        endpoint: str,
        method: str,
        latency_ms: int,
        probability: float = 1.0
    ):
        """注入延迟"""
        fault_id = f"{service_name}-{method}-{endpoint}-latency"
        
        self.active_faults[fault_id] = {
            'type': 'latency',
            'service_name': service_name,
            'endpoint': endpoint,
            'method': method,
            'latency_ms': latency_ms,
            'probability': probability
        }
    
    def inject_error(
        self,
        service_name: str,
        endpoint: str,
        method: str,
        error_rate: float,
        error_status: int = 500
    ):
        """注入错误"""
        fault_id = f"{service_name}-{method}-{endpoint}-error"
        
        self.active_faults[fault_id] = {
            'type': 'error',
            'service_name': service_name,
            'endpoint': endpoint,
            'method': method,
            'error_rate': error_rate,
            'error_status': error_status
        }
    
    def inject_timeout(
        self,
        service_name: str,
        endpoint: str,
        method: str,
        timeout_seconds: int,
        probability: float = 1.0
    ):
        """注入超时"""
        fault_id = f"{service_name}-{method}-{endpoint}-timeout"
        
        self.active_faults[fault_id] = {
            'type': 'timeout',
            'service_name': service_name,
            'endpoint': endpoint,
            'method': method,
            'timeout_seconds': timeout_seconds,
            'probability': probability
        }
    
    def apply_fault(
        self,
        service_name: str,
        endpoint: str,
        method: str
    ) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """应用故障"""
        import random
        
        for fault_id, fault in self.active_faults.items():
            if (fault['service_name'] == service_name and
                fault['endpoint'] == endpoint and
                fault['method'] == method):
                
                if random.random() > fault.get('probability', 1.0):
                    continue
                
                if fault['type'] == 'latency':
                    return {
                        'delay': fault['latency_ms'] / 1000
                    }
                elif fault['type'] == 'error':
                    return {
                        'status_code': fault['error_status'],
                        'response': {'error': 'Injected error'}
                    }
                elif fault['type'] == 'timeout':
                    return {
                        'delay': fault['timeout_seconds']
                    }
        
        return None
    
    def remove_fault(self, fault_id: str):
        """移除故障"""
        if fault_id in self.active_faults:
            del self.active_faults[fault_id]
    
    def clear_all_faults(self):
        """清除所有故障"""
        self.active_faults.clear()

# 使用示例
virtualizer = ServiceVirtualizer()

# 创建虚拟服务
user_service = virtualizer.create_virtual_service(
    name='user-service',
    base_url='http://user-service:8000',
    endpoints=[
        {
            'path': '/api/users/{id}',
            'method': 'GET',
            'default_status': 200,
            'default_response': {
                'id': 1,
                'username': 'testuser',
                'email': 'test@example.com'
            }
        },
        {
            'path': '/api/users/',
            'method': 'POST',
            'default_status': 201,
            'default_response': {
                'id': 1,
                'message': '用户创建成功'
            }
        }
    ]
)

# 添加自定义场景
virtualizer.add_custom_scenario(
    service_name='user-service',
    endpoint='/api/users/{id}',
    method='GET',
    scenario_name='user_not_found',
    response={'error': '用户不存在'},
    status_code=404
)

# 处理请求
response = virtualizer.handle_request(
    service_name='user-service',
    endpoint='/api/users/1',
    method='GET',
    active_scenario='happy_path'
)
print(f"响应: {response}")

# 混沌注入
chaos = ChaosInjector(virtualizer)
chaos.inject_latency('user-service', '/api/users/{id}', 'GET', 1000, 0.5)
chaos.inject_error('user-service', '/api/users/{id}', 'GET', 0.3, 503)

# 验证调用
verification = virtualizer.verify_calls('user-service', [
    {'endpoint': '/api/users/1', 'method': 'GET', 'times': 1}
])
print(f"验证结果: {verification}")
```
