## 二十三、移动端测试

### 23.1 App自动化测试

**Q39: 如何设计移动端App自动化测试框架？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析移动端测试的需求：
- **跨平台支持**：支持iOS和Android
- **设备管理**：管理多设备和模拟器
- **元素定位**：支持多种定位策略
- **手势操作**：支持复杂手势操作

其次，我会设计移动端测试框架：

```python
# framework/mobile_testing.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime
import time
import base64
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

class Platform(Enum):
    """平台类型"""
    ANDROID = "android"
    IOS = "ios"

class DeviceType(Enum):
    """设备类型"""
    REAL = "real"
    EMULATOR = "emulator"
    SIMULATOR = "simulator"

@dataclass
class DeviceInfo:
    """设备信息"""
    device_id: str
    name: str
    platform: Platform
    device_type: DeviceType
    os_version: str
    screen_size: Tuple[int, int]
    is_available: bool = True

@dataclass
class MobileElement:
    """移动端元素"""
    locator_type: str
    locator_value: str
    element_id: Optional[str] = None
    text: Optional[str] = None
    bounds: Optional[Dict] = None
    is_displayed: bool = False
    is_enabled: bool = False
    is_selected: bool = False

class DeviceManager:
    """设备管理器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.devices: Dict[str, DeviceInfo] = {}
        self.connected_devices: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    
    def discover_devices(self) -> List[DeviceInfo]:
        """发现设备"""
        import subprocess
        
        devices = []
        
        # Android设备
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(
                ['adb', 'devices', '-l'],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True
            )
            
            lines = result.stdout.strip().split('\n')[1:]
            for line in lines:
                if line.strip():
                    parts = line.split()
                    if len(parts) >= 2:
                        device_id = parts[0]
                        device_info = DeviceInfo(
                            device_id=device_id,
                            name=parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else device_id,
                            platform=Platform.ANDROID,
                            device_type=DeviceType.REAL if ':' not in device_id else DeviceType.EMULATOR,
                            os_version=self._get_android_version(device_id),
                            screen_size=self._get_screen_size(device_id)
                        )
                        devices.append(device_info)
                        self.devices[device_id] = device_info
        except Exception:
            pass
        
        # iOS设备
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(
                ['xcrun', 'xctrace', 'list', 'devices'],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True
            )
            
            # 解析iOS设备列表
            # 简化处理
        except Exception:
            pass
        
        return devices
    
    def _get_android_version(self, device_id: str) -> str:
        """获取Android版本"""
        import subprocess
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(
                ['adb', '-s', device_id, 'shell', 'getprop', 'ro.build.version.release'],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True
            )
            return result.stdout.strip()
        except:
            return "unknown"
    
    def _get_screen_size(self, device_id: str) -> Tuple[int, int]:
        """获取屏幕尺寸"""
        import subprocess
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(
                ['adb', '-s', device_id, 'shell', 'wm', 'size'],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True
            )
            size_str = result.stdout.strip()
            if 'x' in size_str:
                parts = size_str.split(':')[-1].strip().split('x')
                return (int(parts[0]), int(parts[1]))
        except:
            pass
        return (1080, 1920)
    
    def connect_device(self, device_id: str) -> Any:
        """连接设备"""
        from appium import webdriver
        
        device = self.devices.get(device_id)
        if not device:
            raise ValueError(f"设备 {device_id} 不存在")
        
        if device.platform == Platform.ANDROID:
            capabilities = {
                'platformName': 'Android',
                'platformVersion': device.os_version,
                'deviceName': device.name,
                'automationName': 'UiAutomator2',
                'noReset': True
            }
        else:
            capabilities = {
                'platformName': 'iOS',
                'platformVersion': device.os_version,
                'deviceName': device.name,
                'automationName': 'XCUITest',
                'noReset': True
            }
        
        driver = webdriver.Remote(
            'http://localhost:4723/wd/hub',
            capabilities
        )
        
        self.connected_devices[device_id] = driver
        return driver
    
    def disconnect_device(self, device_id: str):
        """断开设备"""
        driver = self.connected_devices.get(device_id)
        if driver:
            driver.quit()
            del self.connected_devices[device_id]

class MobileElementLocator:
    """移动端元素定位器"""
    
    LOCATOR_STRATEGIES = {
        'id': 'id',
        'class': 'class name',
        'xpath': 'xpath',
        'accessibility_id': 'accessibility id',
        'android_uiautomator': 'android uiautomator',
        'ios_predicate': 'ios predicate',
        'ios_class_chain': 'ios class chain'
    }
    
    def __init__(self, driver):
        self.driver = driver
    
    def find_element(
        self,
        locator_type: str,
        locator_value: str,
        timeout: int = 10
    ) -> MobileElement:
        """查找元素"""
        from selenium.webdriver.support.ui import WebDriverWait
        from selenium.webdriver.support import expected_conditions as EC
        from selenium.webdriver.common.by import By
        
        strategy = self.LOCATOR_STRATEGIES.get(locator_type)
        if not strategy:
            raise ValueError(f"不支持的定位策略: {locator_type}")
        
        by = By(strategy, locator_value)
        
        element = WebDriverWait(self.driver, timeout).until(
            EC.presence_of_element_located(by)
        )
        
        return MobileElement(
            locator_type=locator_type,
            locator_value=locator_value,
            element_id=element.id,
            text=element.text,
            is_displayed=element.is_displayed(),
            is_enabled=element.is_enabled(),
            is_selected=element.is_selected()
        )
    
    def find_elements(
        self,
        locator_type: str,
        locator_value: str
    ) -> List[MobileElement]:
        """查找多个元素"""
        from selenium.webdriver.common.by import By
        
        strategy = self.LOCATOR_STRATEGIES.get(locator_type)
        by = By(strategy, locator_value)
        
        elements = self.driver.find_elements(by)
        
        return [
            MobileElement(
                locator_type=locator_type,
                locator_value=locator_value,
                element_id=e.id,
                text=e.text,
                is_displayed=e.is_displayed(),
                is_enabled=e.is_enabled(),
                is_selected=e.is_selected()
            )
            for e in elements
        ]

class GestureActions:
    """手势操作"""
    
    def __init__(self, driver):
        self.driver = driver
    
    def tap(self, x: int, y: int, duration: int = 100):
        """点击"""
        from appium.webdriver.common.touch_action import TouchAction
        
        action = TouchAction(self.driver)
        action.tap(x=x, y=y).perform()
    
    def double_tap(self, x: int, y: int):
        """双击"""
        from appium.webdriver.common.touch_action import TouchAction
        
        action = TouchAction(self.driver)
        action.tap(x=x, y=y, count=2).perform()
    
    def long_press(self, x: int, y: int, duration: int = 1000):
        """长按"""
        from appium.webdriver.common.touch_action import TouchAction
        
        action = TouchAction(self.driver)
        action.long_press(x=x, y=y, duration=duration).perform()
    
    def swipe(
        self,
        start_x: int,
        start_y: int,
        end_x: int,
        end_y: int,
        duration: int = 500
    ):
        """滑动"""
        from appium.webdriver.common.touch_action import TouchAction
        
        action = TouchAction(self.driver)
        action.press(x=start_x, y=start_y).wait(duration).move_to(x=end_x, y=end_y).release().perform()
    
    def scroll_to_element(self, locator_type: str, locator_value: str, max_swipes: int = 10):
        """滚动到元素"""
        from appium.webdriver.common.touch_action import TouchAction
        
        screen_size = self.driver.get_window_size()
        width = screen_size['width']
        height = screen_size['height']
        
        locator = MobileElementLocator(self.driver)
        
        for _ in range(max_swipes):
            try:
                element = locator.find_element(locator_type, locator_value, timeout=1)
                if element.is_displayed:
                    return element
            except:
                pass
            
            action = TouchAction(self.driver)
            action.press(x=width // 2, y=height * 3 // 4).wait(500).move_to(x=width // 2, y=height // 4).release().perform()
        
        return None
    
    def pinch(self, x: int, y: int, scale: float = 0.5):
        """捏合"""
        from appium.webdriver.common.multi_action import MultiAction
        from appium.webdriver.common.touch_action import TouchAction
        
        screen_size = self.driver.get_window_size()
        
        finger1 = TouchAction(self.driver)
        finger2 = TouchAction(self.driver)
        
        offset = 100
        
        finger1.press(x=x - offset, y=y).wait(500).move_to(x=x - int(offset * scale), y=y).release()
        finger2.press(x=x + offset, y=y).wait(500).move_to(x=x + int(offset * scale), y=y).release()
        
        multi_action = MultiAction(self.driver)
        multi_action.add(finger1, finger2)
        multi_action.perform()
    
    def zoom(self, x: int, y: int, scale: float = 2.0):
        """放大"""
        from appium.webdriver.common.multi_action import MultiAction
        from appium.webdriver.common.touch_action import TouchAction
        
        offset = 50
        
        finger1 = TouchAction(self.driver)
        finger2 = TouchAction(self.driver)
        
        finger1.press(x=x - offset, y=y).wait(500).move_to(x=x - int(offset * scale), y=y).release()
        finger2.press(x=x + offset, y=y).wait(500).move_to(x=x + int(offset * scale), y=y).release()
        
        multi_action = MultiAction(self.driver)
        multi_action.add(finger1, finger2)
        multi_action.perform()

class MobileTestFramework:
    """移动端测试框架"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.device_manager = DeviceManager()
        self.current_driver = None
        self.locator = None
        self.gestures = None
    
    def setup(self, device_id: str):
        """初始化"""
        self.current_driver = self.device_manager.connect_device(device_id)
        self.locator = MobileElementLocator(self.current_driver)
        self.gestures = GestureActions(self.current_driver)
    
    def teardown(self):
        """清理"""
        if self.current_driver:
            self.current_driver.quit()
            self.current_driver = None
    
    def take_screenshot(self, filepath: str):
        """截图"""
        if self.current_driver:
            screenshot = self.current_driver.get_screenshot_as_png()
            with open(filepath, 'wb') as f:
                f.write(screenshot)
    
    def get_page_source(self) -> str:
        """获取页面源码"""
        if self.current_driver:
            return self.current_driver.page_source
        return ""
    
    def wait_for_element(
        self,
        locator_type: str,
        locator_value: str,
        timeout: int = 10
    ) -> MobileElement:
        """等待元素"""
        return self.locator.find_element(locator_type, locator_value, timeout)
    
    def click(self, locator_type: str, locator_value: str):
        """点击元素"""
        element = self.wait_for_element(locator_type, locator_value)
        self.current_driver.find_element(
            MobileElementLocator.LOCATOR_STRATEGIES[locator_type],
            locator_value
        ).click()
    
    def input_text(self, locator_type: str, locator_value: str, text: str):
        """输入文本"""
        element = self.wait_for_element(locator_type, locator_value)
        self.current_driver.find_element(
            MobileElementLocator.LOCATOR_STRATEGIES[locator_type],
            locator_value
        ).send_keys(text)
    
    def hide_keyboard(self):
        """隐藏键盘"""
        if self.current_driver:
            self.current_driver.hide_keyboard()
    
    def rotate_screen(self, orientation: str):
        """旋转屏幕"""
        if self.current_driver:
            self.current_driver.orientation = orientation
    
    def install_app(self, app_path: str):
        """安装应用"""
        if self.current_driver:
            self.current_driver.install_app(app_path)
    
    def remove_app(self, app_id: str):
        """卸载应用"""
        if self.current_driver:
            self.current_driver.remove_app(app_id)
    
    def launch_app(self):
        """启动应用"""
        if self.current_driver:
            self.current_driver.launch_app()
    
    def close_app(self):
        """关闭应用"""
        if self.current_driver:
            self.current_driver.close_app()
    
    def reset_app(self):
        """重置应用"""
        if self.current_driver:
            self.current_driver.reset()

# 使用示例
framework = MobileTestFramework()

# 发现设备
devices = framework.device_manager.discover_devices()
for device in devices:
    print(f"设备: {device.name}, 平台: {device.platform.value}")

# 初始化测试
framework.setup(devices[0].device_id)

# 执行测试操作
framework.click('id', 'login_button')
framework.input_text('id', 'username', 'testuser')
framework.input_text('id', 'password', 'password123')
framework.click('id', 'submit')

# 手势操作
framework.gestures.swipe(500, 1500, 500, 500)
framework.gestures.scroll_to_element('xpath', '//android.widget.TextView[@text="Settings"]')

# 截图
framework.take_screenshot('screenshot.png')

# 清理
framework.teardown()
```

## 二十四、数据库测试

### 24.1 数据库自动化测试

**Q40: 如何设计数据库自动化测试框架？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析数据库测试的需求：
- **数据验证**：验证数据完整性和正确性
- **存储过程测试**：测试存储过程和函数
- **性能测试**：测试SQL查询性能
- **数据迁移测试**：验证数据迁移正确性

其次，我会设计数据库测试框架：

```python
# framework/database_testing.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime
import time
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

class DatabaseType(Enum):
    """数据库类型"""
    POSTGRESQL = "postgresql"
    MYSQL = "mysql"
    SQLITE = "sqlite"
    ORACLE = "oracle"
    SQLSERVER = "sqlserver"

@dataclass
class DatabaseConfig:
    """数据库配置"""
    db_type: DatabaseType
    host: str
    port: int
    database: str
    username: str
    password: str

@dataclass
class QueryResult:
    """查询结果"""
    query: str
    rows: List[Tuple]
    columns: List[str]
    row_count: int
    execution_time: float
    error: Optional[str] = None

@dataclass
class StoredProcedureResult:
    """存储过程结果"""
    name: str
    parameters: Dict[str, Any]
    return_value: Any
    output_parameters: Dict[str, Any]
    result_sets: List[List[Tuple]]
    execution_time: float
    success: bool
    error: Optional[str] = None

class DatabaseConnection(ABC):
    """数据库连接基类"""
    
    @abstractmethod
    def connect(self):
        """连接数据库"""
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    def disconnect(self):
        """断开连接"""
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    def execute_query(self, query: str, params: Tuple = None) -> QueryResult:
        """执行查询"""
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    def execute_update(self, sql: str, params: Tuple = None) -> int:
        """执行更新"""
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    def execute_stored_procedure(
        self,
        name: str,
        params: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> StoredProcedureResult:
        """执行存储过程"""
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    def get_tables(self) -> List[str]:
        """获取所有表"""
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    def get_table_schema(self, table_name: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取表结构"""
        pass

class PostgreSQLConnection(DatabaseConnection):
    """PostgreSQL连接"""
    
    def __init__(self, config: DatabaseConfig):
        self.config = config
        self.connection = None
    
    def connect(self):
        """连接数据库"""
        import psycopg2
        self.connection = psycopg2.connect(
            host=self.config.host,
            port=self.config.port,
            database=self.config.database,
            user=self.config.username,
            password=self.config.password
        )
    
    def disconnect(self):
        """断开连接"""
        if self.connection:
            self.connection.close()
            self.connection = None
    
    def execute_query(self, query: str, params: Tuple = None) -> QueryResult:
        """执行查询"""
        start_time = time.time()
        
        try:
            cursor = self.connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute(query, params)
            
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            columns = [desc[0] for desc in cursor.description] if cursor.description else []
            
            cursor.close()
            
            execution_time = time.time() - start_time
            
            return QueryResult(
                query=query,
                rows=rows,
                columns=columns,
                row_count=len(rows),
                execution_time=execution_time
            )
        
        except Exception as e:
            return QueryResult(
                query=query,
                rows=[],
                columns=[],
                row_count=0,
                execution_time=time.time() - start_time,
                error=str(e)
            )
    
    def execute_update(self, sql: str, params: Tuple = None) -> int:
        """执行更新"""
        cursor = self.connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(sql, params)
        row_count = cursor.rowcount
        self.connection.commit()
        cursor.close()
        return row_count
    
    def execute_stored_procedure(
        self,
        name: str,
        params: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> StoredProcedureResult:
        """执行存储过程"""
        start_time = time.time()
        
        try:
            cursor = self.connection.cursor()
            
            placeholders = ', '.join(['%s'] * len(params))
            cursor.callproc(name, list(params.values()))
            
            result_sets = []
            while True:
                try:
                    rows = cursor.fetchall()
                    result_sets.append(rows)
                except:
                    break
                
                if not cursor.nextset():
                    break
            
            cursor.close()
            
            return StoredProcedureResult(
                name=name,
                parameters=params,
                return_value=None,
                output_parameters={},
                result_sets=result_sets,
                execution_time=time.time() - start_time,
                success=True
            )
        
        except Exception as e:
            return StoredProcedureResult(
                name=name,
                parameters=params,
                return_value=None,
                output_parameters={},
                result_sets=[],
                execution_time=time.time() - start_time,
                success=False,
                error=str(e)
            )
    
    def get_tables(self) -> List[str]:
        """获取所有表"""
        result = self.execute_query(
            "SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = 'public'"
        )
        return [row[0] for row in result.rows]
    
    def get_table_schema(self, table_name: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取表结构"""
        result = self.execute_query("""
            SELECT column_name, data_type, is_nullable, column_default
            FROM information_schema.columns
            WHERE table_name = %s
            ORDER BY ordinal_position
        """, (table_name,))
        
        return [
            {
                'column_name': row[0],
                'data_type': row[1],
                'is_nullable': row[2] == 'YES',
                'default': row[3]
            }
            for row in result.rows
        ]

class MySQLConnection(DatabaseConnection):
    """MySQL连接"""
    
    def __init__(self, config: DatabaseConfig):
        self.config = config
        self.connection = None
    
    def connect(self):
        """连接数据库"""
        import mysql.connector
        self.connection = mysql.connector.connect(
            host=self.config.host,
            port=self.config.port,
            database=self.config.database,
            user=self.config.username,
            password=self.config.password
        )
    
    def disconnect(self):
        """断开连接"""
        if self.connection:
            self.connection.close()
            self.connection = None
    
    def execute_query(self, query: str, params: Tuple = None) -> QueryResult:
        """执行查询"""
        start_time = time.time()
        
        try:
            cursor = self.connection.cursor()
            cursor.execute(query, params)
            
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            columns = [desc[0] for desc in cursor.description] if cursor.description else []
            
            cursor.close()
            
            return QueryResult(
                query=query,
                rows=rows,
                columns=columns,
                row_count=len(rows),
                execution_time=time.time() - start_time
            )
        
        except Exception as e:
            return QueryResult(
                query=query,
                rows=[],
                columns=[],
                row_count=0,
                execution_time=time.time() - start_time,
                error=str(e)
            )
    
    def execute_update(self, sql: str, params: Tuple = None) -> int:
        """执行更新"""
        cursor = self.connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute(sql, params)
        row_count = cursor.rowcount
        self.connection.commit()
        cursor.close()
        return row_count
    
    def execute_stored_procedure(
        self,
        name: str,
        params: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> StoredProcedureResult:
        """执行存储过程"""
        start_time = time.time()
        
        try:
            cursor = self.connection.cursor()
            cursor.callproc(name, list(params.values()))
            
            result_sets = []
            for result in cursor.stored_results():
                result_sets.append(result.fetchall())
            
            cursor.close()
            
            return StoredProcedureResult(
                name=name,
                parameters=params,
                return_value=None,
                output_parameters={},
                result_sets=result_sets,
                execution_time=time.time() - start_time,
                success=True
            )
        
        except Exception as e:
            return StoredProcedureResult(
                name=name,
                parameters=params,
                return_value=None,
                output_parameters={},
                result_sets=[],
                execution_time=time.time() - start_time,
                success=False,
                error=str(e)
            )
    
    def get_tables(self) -> List[str]:
        """获取所有表"""
        result = self.execute_query("SHOW TABLES")
        return [row[0] for row in result.rows]
    
    def get_table_schema(self, table_name: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取表结构"""
        result = self.execute_query(f"DESCRIBE {table_name}")
        
        return [
            {
                'column_name': row[0],
                'data_type': row[1],
                'is_nullable': row[2] == 'YES',
                'default': row[4]
            }
            for row in result.rows
        ]

class DatabaseTestFramework:
    """数据库测试框架"""
    
    def __init__(self, config: DatabaseConfig):
        self.config = config
        self.connection = self._create_connection()
    
    def _create_connection(self) -> DatabaseConnection:
        """创建连接"""
        if self.config.db_type == DatabaseType.POSTGRESQL:
            return PostgreSQLConnection(self.config)
        elif self.config.db_type == DatabaseType.MYSQL:
            return MySQLConnection(self.config)
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"不支持的数据库类型: {self.config.db_type}")
    
    def connect(self):
        """连接数据库"""
        self.connection.connect()
    
    def disconnect(self):
        """断开连接"""
        self.connection.disconnect()
    
    def verify_table_exists(self, table_name: str) -> bool:
        """验证表是否存在"""
        tables = self.connection.get_tables()
        return table_name in tables
    
    def verify_table_structure(
        self,
        table_name: str,
        expected_columns: Dict[str, Dict]
    ) -> Dict:
        """验证表结构"""
        schema = self.connection.get_table_schema(table_name)
        
        errors = []
        
        for col in schema:
            col_name = col['column_name']
            if col_name in expected_columns:
                expected = expected_columns[col_name]
                
                if expected.get('data_type') and col['data_type'] != expected['data_type']:
                    errors.append(
                        f"列 {col_name} 类型不匹配: 期望 {expected['data_type']}, "
                        f"实际 {col['data_type']}"
                    )
                
                if expected.get('nullable') is not None:
                    if col['is_nullable'] != expected['nullable']:
                        errors.append(
                            f"列 {col_name} 可空性不匹配"
                        )
        
        for col_name in expected_columns:
            if not any(c['column_name'] == col_name for c in schema):
                errors.append(f"缺少列: {col_name}")
        
        return {
            'valid': len(errors) == 0,
            'errors': errors
        }
    
    def verify_data_count(
        self,
        table_name: str,
        expected_count: int,
        where_clause: str = None
    ) -> Dict:
        """验证数据数量"""
        query = f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table_name}"
        if where_clause:
            query += f" WHERE {where_clause}"
        
        result = self.connection.execute_query(query)
        
        if result.error:
            return {
                'valid': False,
                'error': result.error
            }
        
        actual_count = result.rows[0][0]
        
        return {
            'valid': actual_count == expected_count,
            'expected': expected_count,
            'actual': actual_count
        }
    
    def verify_data_exists(
        self,
        table_name: str,
        conditions: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Dict:
        """验证数据存在"""
        where_clause = ' AND '.join([f"{k} = %s" for k in conditions.keys()])
        query = f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table_name} WHERE {where_clause}"
        
        result = self.connection.execute_query(query, tuple(conditions.values()))
        
        if result.error:
            return {
                'exists': False,
                'error': result.error
            }
        
        count = result.rows[0][0]
        
        return {
            'exists': count > 0,
            'count': count
        }
    
    def verify_data_integrity(
        self,
        table_name: str,
        primary_key: str
    ) -> Dict:
        """验证数据完整性"""
        query = f"""
            SELECT {primary_key}, COUNT(*) as cnt
            FROM {table_name}
            GROUP BY {primary_key}
            HAVING COUNT(*) > 1
        """
        
        result = self.connection.execute_query(query)
        
        duplicates = result.rows
        
        return {
            'valid': len(duplicates) == 0,
            'duplicates': duplicates
        }
    
    def verify_foreign_key(
        self,
        table_name: str,
        column_name: str,
        referenced_table: str,
        referenced_column: str
    ) -> Dict:
        """验证外键约束"""
        query = f"""
            SELECT a.{column_name}
            FROM {table_name} a
            LEFT JOIN {referenced_table} b ON a.{column_name} = b.{referenced_column}
            WHERE a.{column_name} IS NOT NULL AND b.{referenced_column} IS NULL
        """
        
        result = self.connection.execute_query(query)
        
        orphaned = result.rows
        
        return {
            'valid': len(orphaned) == 0,
            'orphaned_records': orphaned
        }
    
    def test_stored_procedure(
        self,
        name: str,
        params: Dict[str, Any],
        expected_result: Any = None,
        expected_output: Dict = None
    ) -> Dict:
        """测试存储过程"""
        result = self.connection.execute_stored_procedure(name, params)
        
        errors = []
        
        if not result.success:
            errors.append(f"执行失败: {result.error}")
        
        if expected_result is not None:
            if result.return_value != expected_result:
                errors.append(
                    f"返回值不匹配: 期望 {expected_result}, "
                    f"实际 {result.return_value}"
                )
        
        if expected_output:
            for key, value in expected_output.items():
                if result.output_parameters.get(key) != value:
                    errors.append(
                        f"输出参数 {key} 不匹配: 期望 {value}, "
                        f"实际 {result.output_parameters.get(key)}"
                    )
        
        return {
            'passed': len(errors) == 0,
            'errors': errors,
            'execution_time': result.execution_time
        }
    
    def measure_query_performance(
        self,
        query: str,
        params: Tuple = None,
        iterations: int = 10
    ) -> Dict:
        """测量查询性能"""
        times = []
        
        for _ in range(iterations):
            result = self.connection.execute_query(query, params)
            if result.error:
                return {
                    'error': result.error,
                    'success': False
                }
            times.append(result.execution_time)
        
        import statistics
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'avg_time': statistics.mean(times),
            'min_time': min(times),
            'max_time': max(times),
            'median_time': statistics.median(times),
            'std_dev': statistics.stdev(times) if len(times) > 1 else 0
        }
    
    def compare_query_plans(
        self,
        query1: str,
        query2: str
    ) -> Dict:
        """比较查询计划"""
        plan1 = self._get_query_plan(query1)
        plan2 = self._get_query_plan(query2)
        
        return {
            'query1_plan': plan1,
            'query2_plan': plan2
        }
    
    def _get_query_plan(self, query: str) -> str:
        """获取查询计划"""
        if self.config.db_type == DatabaseType.POSTGRESQL:
            result = self.connection.execute_query(f"EXPLAIN {query}")
            return '\n'.join([row[0] for row in result.rows])
        elif self.config.db_type == DatabaseType.MYSQL:
            result = self.connection.execute_query(f"EXPLAIN {query}")
            return str(result.rows)
        return ""
    
    def backup_table(self, table_name: str, backup_name: str):
        """备份表"""
        self.connection.execute_update(f"CREATE TABLE {backup_name} AS SELECT * FROM {table_name}")
    
    def restore_table(self, table_name: str, backup_name: str):
        """恢复表"""
        self.connection.execute_update(f"DROP TABLE IF EXISTS {table_name}")
        self.connection.execute_update(f"ALTER TABLE {backup_name} RENAME TO {table_name}")
    
    def truncate_table(self, table_name: str):
        """清空表"""
        self.connection.execute_update(f"TRUNCATE TABLE {table_name}")

# 使用示例
config = DatabaseConfig(
    db_type=DatabaseType.POSTGRESQL,
    host="localhost",
    port=5432,
    database="testdb",
    username="test",
    password="test"
)

framework = DatabaseTestFramework(config)
framework.connect()

# 验证表存在
exists = framework.verify_table_exists("users")
print(f"表存在: {exists}")

# 验证表结构
structure_result = framework.verify_table_structure("users", {
    "id": {"data_type": "integer", "nullable": False},
    "username": {"data_type": "character varying", "nullable": False},
    "email": {"data_type": "character varying", "nullable": True}
})
print(f"结构验证: {structure_result}")

# 验证数据数量
count_result = framework.verify_data_count("users", 100)
print(f"数量验证: {count_result}")

# 验证数据存在
exists_result = framework.verify_data_exists("users", {"username": "admin"})
print(f"数据存在: {exists_result}")

# 测试存储过程
sp_result = framework.test_stored_procedure(
    "get_user_by_id",
    {"user_id": 1},
    expected_result=None
)
print(f"存储过程测试: {sp_result}")

# 测量查询性能
perf_result = framework.measure_query_performance("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = %s", (1,))
print(f"性能测试: {perf_result}")

framework.disconnect()
```

## 二十五、测试质量度量

### 25.1 测试质量指标体系

**Q41: 如何建立测试质量度量体系？**

**参考答案：**

首先，我会分析测试质量度量的需求：
- **过程度量**：衡量测试过程的质量
- **结果度量**：衡量测试结果的质量
- **效率度量**：衡量测试效率
- **价值度量**：衡量测试价值

其次，我会设计测试质量度量体系：

```python
# framework/test_metrics.py
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import statistics
from collections import defaultdict

class MetricCategory(Enum):
    """指标类别"""
    PROCESS = "process"
    RESULT = "result"
    EFFICIENCY = "efficiency"
    VALUE = "value"

@dataclass
class Metric:
    """度量指标"""
    name: str
    category: MetricCategory
    value: float
    unit: str
    target: Optional[float] = None
    threshold: Optional[float] = None
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    trend: Optional[str] = None

@dataclass
class TestExecution:
    """测试执行记录"""
    execution_id: str
    test_suite: str
    start_time: datetime
    end_time: datetime
    total_cases: int
    passed: int
    failed: int
    skipped: int
    errors: int
    duration: float

@dataclass
class Defect:
    """缺陷记录"""
    defect_id: str
    severity: str
    priority: str
    status: str
    found_in_phase: str
    found_by: str
    created_at: datetime
    resolved_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    root_cause: Optional[str] = None

class MetricsCollector:
    """指标收集器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.executions: List[TestExecution] = []
        self.defects: List[Defect] = []
        self.metrics: Dict[str, List[Metric]] = defaultdict(list)
    
    def record_execution(self, execution: TestExecution):
        """记录执行"""
        self.executions.append(execution)
    
    def record_defect(self, defect: Defect):
        """记录缺陷"""
        self.defects.append(defect)
    
    def record_metric(self, metric: Metric):
        """记录指标"""
        self.metrics[metric.name].append(metric)

class ProcessMetrics:
    """过程度量"""
    
    def __init__(self, collector: MetricsCollector):
        self.collector = collector
    
    def calculate_test_coverage(self) -> Metric:
        """计算测试覆盖率"""
        # 这里需要从代码覆盖率工具获取数据
        # 简化示例
        coverage = 0.0
        if self.collector.metrics.get('code_coverage'):
            coverage = self.collector.metrics['code_coverage'][-1].value
        
        return Metric(
            name="测试覆盖率",
            category=MetricCategory.PROCESS,
            value=coverage,
            unit="%",
            target=80.0,
            threshold=60.0
        )
    
    def calculate_requirement_coverage(self) -> Metric:
        """计算需求覆盖率"""
        # 简化示例
        total_requirements = 100
        covered_requirements = 85
        
        coverage = (covered_requirements / total_requirements) * 100
        
        return Metric(
            name="需求覆盖率",
            category=MetricCategory.PROCESS,
            value=coverage,
            unit="%",
            target=100.0,
            threshold=90.0
        )
    
    def calculate_test_case_productivity(self) -> Metric:
        """计算测试用例产出率"""
        if not self.collector.executions:
            return Metric(
                name="测试用例产出率",
                category=MetricCategory.PROCESS,
                value=0,
                unit="用例/人天"
            )
        
        total_cases = sum(e.total_cases for e in self.collector.executions)
        
        # 假设统计周期为30天
        days = 30
        productivity = total_cases / days
        
        return Metric(
            name="测试用例产出率",
            category=MetricCategory.PROCESS,
            value=productivity,
            unit="用例/人天",
            target=10.0,
            threshold=5.0
        )
    
    def calculate_test_preparation_ratio(self) -> Metric:
        """计算测试准备率"""
        # 测试用例准备完成数 / 需求总数
        prepared_cases = 450
        total_requirements = 500
        
        ratio = (prepared_cases / total_requirements) * 100
        
        return Metric(
            name="测试准备率",
            category=MetricCategory.PROCESS,
            value=ratio,
            unit="%",
            target=100.0,
            threshold=80.0
        )

class ResultMetrics:
    """结果度量"""
    
    def __init__(self, collector: MetricsCollector):
        self.collector = collector
    
    def calculate_pass_rate(self) -> Metric:
        """计算通过率"""
        if not self.collector.executions:
            return Metric(
                name="测试通过率",
                category=MetricCategory.RESULT,
                value=0,
                unit="%"
            )
        
        total = sum(e.total_cases for e in self.collector.executions)
        passed = sum(e.passed for e in self.collector.executions)
        
        rate = (passed / total * 100) if total > 0 else 0
        
        return Metric(
            name="测试通过率",
            category=MetricCategory.RESULT,
            value=rate,
            unit="%",
            target=95.0,
            threshold=80.0
        )
    
    def calculate_defect_density(self) -> Metric:
        """计算缺陷密度"""
        if not self.collector.defects:
            return Metric(
                name="缺陷密度",
                category=MetricCategory.RESULT,
                value=0,
                unit="缺陷/KLOC"
            )
        
        total_defects = len(self.collector.defects)
        # 假设代码行数为10000行
        kloc = 10
        
        density = total_defects / kloc
        
        return Metric(
            name="缺陷密度",
            category=MetricCategory.RESULT,
            value=density,
            unit="缺陷/KLOC",
            target=5.0,
            threshold=10.0
        )
    
    def calculate_defect_detection_rate(self) -> Metric:
        """计算缺陷检出率"""
        # 测试发现的缺陷数 / 总缺陷数
        test_found = len([d for d in self.collector.defects if d.found_in_phase == 'testing'])
        total_defects = len(self.collector.defects)
        
        rate = (test_found / total_defects * 100) if total_defects > 0 else 0
        
        return Metric(
            name="缺陷检出率",
            category=MetricCategory.RESULT,
            value=rate,
            unit="%",
            target=80.0,
            threshold=60.0
        )
    
    def calculate_defect_removal_efficiency(self) -> Metric:
        """计算缺陷移除效率"""
        # 发布前发现的缺陷数 / 总缺陷数
        pre_release_defects = len([
            d for d in self.collector.defects
            if d.found_in_phase in ['testing', 'review']
        ])
        total_defects = len(self.collector.defects)
        
        efficiency = (pre_release_defects / total_defects * 100) if total_defects > 0 else 0
        
        return Metric(
            name="缺陷移除效率",
            category=MetricCategory.RESULT,
            value=efficiency,
            unit="%",
            target=95.0,
            threshold=85.0
        )
    
    def calculate_severity_distribution(self) -> Dict[str, int]:
        """计算严重程度分布"""
        distribution = defaultdict(int)
        
        for defect in self.collector.defects:
            distribution[defect.severity] += 1
        
        return dict(distribution)

class EfficiencyMetrics:
    """效率度量"""
    
    def __init__(self, collector: MetricsCollector):
        self.collector = collector
    
    def calculate_execution_efficiency(self) -> Metric:
        """计算执行效率"""
        if not self.collector.executions:
            return Metric(
                name="执行效率",
                category=MetricCategory.EFFICIENCY,
                value=0,
                unit="用例/小时"
            )
        
        total_cases = sum(e.total_cases for e in self.collector.executions)
        total_hours = sum(e.duration for e in self.collector.executions) / 3600
        
        efficiency = total_cases / total_hours if total_hours > 0 else 0
        
        return Metric(
            name="执行效率",
            category=MetricCategory.EFFICIENCY,
            value=efficiency,
            unit="用例/小时",
            target=20.0,
            threshold=10.0
        )
    
    def calculate_automation_rate(self) -> Metric:
        """计算自动化率"""
        automated_cases = 800
        total_cases = 1000
        
        rate = (automated_cases / total_cases) * 100
        
        return Metric(
            name="自动化率",
            category=MetricCategory.EFFICIENCY,
            value=rate,
            unit="%",
            target=80.0,
            threshold=50.0
        )
    
    def calculate_defect_fix_time(self) -> Metric:
        """计算缺陷修复时间"""
        resolved_defects = [
            d for d in self.collector.defects
            if d.resolved_at
        ]
        
        if not resolved_defects:
            return Metric(
                name="平均缺陷修复时间",
                category=MetricCategory.EFFICIENCY,
                value=0,
                unit="小时"
            )
        
        fix_times = [
            (d.resolved_at - d.created_at).total_seconds() / 3600
            for d in resolved_defects
        ]
        
        avg_time = statistics.mean(fix_times)
        
        return Metric(
            name="平均缺陷修复时间",
            category=MetricCategory.EFFICIENCY,
            value=avg_time,
            unit="小时",
            target=24.0,
            threshold=72.0
        )
    
    def calculate_test_cycle_time(self) -> Metric:
        """计算测试周期时间"""
        if not self.collector.executions:
            return Metric(
                name="测试周期时间",
                category=MetricCategory.EFFICIENCY,
                value=0,
                unit="天"
            )
        
        # 计算最近一次测试周期
        latest = max(self.collector.executions, key=lambda e: e.start_time)
        cycle_time = (latest.end_time - latest.start_time).days
        
        return Metric(
            name="测试周期时间",
            category=MetricCategory.EFFICIENCY,
            value=cycle_time,
            unit="天",
            target=5.0,
            threshold=10.0
        )

class ValueMetrics:
    """价值度量"""
    
    def __init__(self, collector: MetricsCollector):
        self.collector = collector
    
    def calculate_cost_of_quality(self) -> Metric:
        """计算质量成本"""
        # 预防成本 + 评估成本 + 失败成本
        prevention_cost = 50000
        appraisal_cost = 30000
        failure_cost = 20000
        
        total_cost = prevention_cost + appraisal_cost + failure_cost
        
        return Metric(
            name="质量成本",
            category=MetricCategory.VALUE,
            value=total_cost,
            unit="元",
            target=80000.0,
            threshold=150000.0
        )
    
    def calculate_roi(self) -> Metric:
        """计算投资回报率"""
        # (收益 - 成本) / 成本 * 100
        benefits = 500000
        costs = 100000
        
        roi = ((benefits - costs) / costs) * 100
        
        return Metric(
            name="测试投资回报率",
            category=MetricCategory.VALUE,
            value=roi,
            unit="%",
            target=200.0,
            threshold=100.0
        )
    
    def calculate_defect_escape_rate(self) -> Metric:
        """计算缺陷逃逸率"""
        # 生产环境发现的缺陷数 / 总缺陷数
        production_defects = len([
            d for d in self.collector.defects
            if d.found_in_phase == 'production'
        ])
        total_defects = len(self.collector.defects)
        
        rate = (production_defects / total_defects * 100) if total_defects > 0 else 0
        
        return Metric(
            name="缺陷逃逸率",
            category=MetricCategory.VALUE,
            value=rate,
            unit="%",
            target=5.0,
            threshold=10.0
        )

class MetricsReporter:
    """指标报告器"""
    
    def __init__(self, collector: MetricsCollector):
        self.collector = collector
        self.process_metrics = ProcessMetrics(collector)
        self.result_metrics = ResultMetrics(collector)
        self.efficiency_metrics = EfficiencyMetrics(collector)
        self.value_metrics = ValueMetrics(collector)
    
    def generate_dashboard(self) -> Dict:
        """生成仪表盘"""
        return {
            'process': {
                'test_coverage': self.process_metrics.calculate_test_coverage().__dict__,
                'requirement_coverage': self.process_metrics.calculate_requirement_coverage().__dict__,
                'productivity': self.process_metrics.calculate_test_case_productivity().__dict__,
                'preparation_ratio': self.process_metrics.calculate_test_preparation_ratio().__dict__
            },
            'result': {
                'pass_rate': self.result_metrics.calculate_pass_rate().__dict__,
                'defect_density': self.result_metrics.calculate_defect_density().__dict__,
                'detection_rate': self.result_metrics.calculate_defect_detection_rate().__dict__,
                'removal_efficiency': self.result_metrics.calculate_defect_removal_efficiency().__dict__
            },
            'efficiency': {
                'execution_efficiency': self.efficiency_metrics.calculate_execution_efficiency().__dict__,
                'automation_rate': self.efficiency_metrics.calculate_automation_rate().__dict__,
                'fix_time': self.efficiency_metrics.calculate_defect_fix_time().__dict__,
                'cycle_time': self.efficiency_metrics.calculate_test_cycle_time().__dict__
            },
            'value': {
                'cost_of_quality': self.value_metrics.calculate_cost_of_quality().__dict__,
                'roi': self.value_metrics.calculate_roi().__dict__,
                'escape_rate': self.value_metrics.calculate_defect_escape_rate().__dict__
            }
        }
    
    def generate_report(self) -> str:
        """生成报告"""
        dashboard = self.generate_dashboard()
        
        report = """
# 测试质量度量报告

## 过程度量
"""
        
        for name, metric in dashboard['process'].items():
            status = "✓" if metric['value'] >= metric.get('target', 0) else "✗"
            report += f"- {metric['name']}: {metric['value']:.2f}{metric['unit']} {status}\n"
        
        report += "\n## 结果度量\n"
        
        for name, metric in dashboard['result'].items():
            status = "✓" if metric['value'] >= metric.get('target', 0) else "✗"
            report += f"- {metric['name']}: {metric['value']:.2f}{metric['unit']} {status}\n"
        
        report += "\n## 效率度量\n"
        
        for name, metric in dashboard['efficiency'].items():
            status = "✓" if metric['value'] >= metric.get('target', 0) else "✗"
            report += f"- {metric['name']}: {metric['value']:.2f}{metric['unit']} {status}\n"
        
        report += "\n## 价值度量\n"
        
        for name, metric in dashboard['value'].items():
            status = "✓" if metric['value'] >= metric.get('target', 0) else "✗"
            report += f"- {metric['name']}: {metric['value']:.2f}{metric['unit']} {status}\n"
        
        return report

# 使用示例
collector = MetricsCollector()

# 记录测试执行
collector.record_execution(TestExecution(
    execution_id="exec-001",
    test_suite="登录模块",
    start_time=datetime.now() - timedelta(hours=2),
    end_time=datetime.now(),
    total_cases=50,
    passed=45,
    failed=3,
    skipped=2,
    errors=0,
    duration=7200
))

# 记录缺陷
collector.record_defect(Defect(
    defect_id="bug-001",
    severity="high",
    priority="P1",
    status="resolved",
    found_in_phase="testing",
    found_by="tester1",
    created_at=datetime.now() - timedelta(days=2),
    resolved_at=datetime.now() - timedelta(days=1),
    root_cause="代码逻辑错误"
))

# 生成报告
reporter = MetricsReporter(collector)
report = reporter.generate_report()
print(report)
```

---

## 附：面试技巧

### 回答结构化

使用"首先...其次...最后..."的结构，让回答更有条理。

### 结合项目

用实际项目经验支撑回答，展示实战能力。

### 展示深度

不仅说怎么做，还要说为什么，展示技术深度。

### 承认不足

遇到不会的问题，诚实回答并展示学习能力。

### 提问互动

面试结束前，准备有质量的问题：

**示例问题：**
1. 贵公司的测试开发团队规模和分工是怎样的？
2. 测试开发团队如何与开发团队协作？
3. 贵公司对测试自动化的投入和期望是什么？
4. 测试开发人员的职业发展路径是怎样的？
5. 贵公司目前面临的最大测试挑战是什么？

## 附：评分标准

| 题型 | 分值 | 评分要点 |
|------|------|----------|
| 测试开发理解 | 15分 | 岗位认知、价值理解 |
| 框架开发 | 25分 | 架构设计、代码质量 |
| 自动化测试 | 20分 | 脚本质量、最佳实践 |
| 工具开发 | 20分 | 工具设计、实用性 |
| CI/CD | 10分 | 流程设计、自动化程度 |
| 综合能力 | 10分 | 问题解决、技术选型 |

**及格线：60分**
**优秀线：85分**
